## 1. CCM 核心进展（1）

### B（1）

#### [B] Radiation-induced versus sporadic cerebral cavernous malformations: comparative analysis of incidence and hemorrhage risk.

- PMID: 43063467
- Domain: CCM
- Domain 依据: Strong CCM disease/gene expression in title or abstract
- 转化相关性: Direct
- 转化相关性依据: The article has strong direct CCM evidence
- 优先级依据: B: CCM/related vascular-malformation clinical evidence
- 主题: CM药物治疗
- 来源类型: 今日新文献
- 期刊: Journal of neurosurgery
- 作者: Bishay AE, Koutsouras GW, Lyons AT, Narayanan SN, Jew NV, Chitale RV, et al.
- 发表日期: 2026 Nov 20
- DOI: 10.3171/2026.5.JNS26515
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/43063467/
- 日报日期: 2026-11-22

#### 摘要

OBJECTIVE: Radiation exposure has emerged as a recognized risk factor for cerebral cavernous malformation (CCM). Radiation-induced CCM (XRT-CCM) may arise months to decades after treatment and may carry higher hemorrhage risk than non-XRT-CCM, though studies remain limited. This study aimed to compare clinical characteristics, hemorrhage rates, and surgical management between XRT-CCM and non-XRT-CCM.
METHODS: A single-institution retrospective cohort study examined patients with MRI-confirmed CCM (1987-2024). All XRT-CCM and non-XRT-CCM patients, regardless of presentation type, were included. The primary outcome was symptomatic hemorrhage following diagnosis, and the secondary outcome was surgical intervention. Analysis included descriptive statistics, group comparisons, hemorrhage incidence, multivariable logistic regression for hemorrhage risk, and Cox regression for hemorrhage-free survival.
RESULTS: The cohort comprised 612 patients: 16 XRT-CCM and 596 non-XRT-CCM patients. The XRT-CCM group included 11 incidental (68.8%) and 5 non-seizure-associated hemorrhage (31.3%) presenters, while the non-XRT-CCM group included 336 incidental (56.4%), 123 non-seizure-associated hemorrhage (20.6%), and 137 seizure (23.0%) presenters. Demographic characteristics were similar between groups. XRT-CCM patients received radiation at a median age of 11.9 years, with diagnosis a median of 13.5 years later. The mean ± SD radiation dosage was 47.8 ± 15.1 Gy, most commonly for arteriovenous malformations (31%). Multivariable analysis showed that radiation was significantly associated with hemorrhage (OR 5.23, 95% CI 1.35-20.36), while presentation type was not. Hemorrhage incidence was 7.77 per 100 person-years for XRT-CCM patients versus 4.36 for non-XRT-CCM patients (incidence rate ratio 1.78). Surgical intervention rates did not differ significantly between groups (25.0% vs 21.1%), nor did primary surgical indications, with hemorrhage being the most common indication in both XRT-CCM (75.0%) and non-XRT-CCM (43.7%) patients.
CONCLUSIONS: XRT-CCM demonstrated a higher hemorrhage incidence compared to non-XRT-CCM, with radiation exposure significantly associated with symptomatic hemorrhage. However, radiation exposure was not significantly associated with time to hemorrhage on survival analysis. The extended latency from radiation to CCM diagnosis underscores the need for long-term surveillance in patients exposed to cranial irradiation.

#### [A] Treatment of venous malformations: first experience with a new sclerosing agent--a multicenter study.

- PMID: 21458186
- Domain: Venous malformation
- Domain 依据: Venous or slow-flow malformation expression in article content
- 转化相关性: Strong
- 转化相关性依据: Related vascular malformation with a transferable mechanism or targeted therapy
- 优先级依据: A: related vascular malformation + transferable mechanism/targeted therapy
- 主题: 静脉畸形基础与治疗
- 来源类型: 经典补位
- 期刊: European journal of radiology
- 作者: Schumacher M, Dupuy P, Bartoli JM, Ernemann U, Herbreteau D, Ghienne C, et al.
- 发表日期: 2011 Dec
- DOI: 10.1016/j.ejrad.2010.12.074
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/21458186/
- 日报日期: 2026-11-20

#### 摘要

PURPOSE: To study the efficacy and safety of a new sclerosing gel of absolute ethanol in the percutaneous treatment of venous malformations (VM).
MATERIALS AND METHODS: In this prospective, non-randomized multicenter study patients with clinically and by magnetic resonance imaging diagnosed VM were treated. Efficacy and safety of the gel was evaluated. Therapeutic outcome was judged at day 56 after the last sclerosing therapy. Blood ethanol levels of ethanol were measured after each infusion. Local and systemic adverse events were recorded.
RESULTS: Seventy-five (75) patients (age 4-46 y, mean 26 y) were treated in 172 sessions. Compared to no treatment, ethanol gel showed a complete cure rate of about 15% per session (p<0.00001). At the end of the last session, therapeutic outcome was complete (score 2) and partial (score 1) in 28 (37%) and 42 patients (56%), respectively, whereas treatment failure (score 0) was observed in 5 patients (7%). The plasmatic ethanol levels were very low (mean±SEM 0.03±0.06 g L(-1)), with only one patient above the legal 0.5 g L(-1) intoxication limit (0.6 g L(-1)). Forty-six (46) product-related adverse events (all local, none systemic) were reported. They included temporary mild isolated pain (N=21), inflammatory reactions (N=4), and local complications (7 skin necroses, 7 compressive neuropathies, 4 product leakage/fistula, 2 intralesional fibrous or granulomatous tissue, 1 dense node; 12.2% of the infusions). All local complications resolved spontaneously, except for 2 skin necroses requesting surgical paring.
CONCLUSION: Ethanol gel is an embosclerosing substance that provides high efficiency and improves safety of ethanol in the treatment of VM lesions.

#### [A] A PI3K-RAC1 signaling axis downstream of mutant TIE2 promotes endothelial dysfunction in venous malformation.

- PMID: 43059804
- Domain: Venous malformation
- Domain 依据: TEK/TIE2 appears with vascular-malformation context
- 转化相关性: Strong
- 转化相关性依据: Related vascular malformation with a transferable mechanism or targeted therapy
- 优先级依据: A: related vascular malformation + transferable mechanism/targeted therapy
- 主题: 静脉畸形基础与治疗
- 来源类型: 近期补位
- 期刊: bioRxiv : the preprint server for biology
- 作者: Sagar K, Schrenk S, Sherpa C, Crockett L, Soroko K, Cai Y, et al.
- 发表日期: 2026 Nov 13
- DOI: 10.64898/2026.11.12.766208
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/43059804/
- 日报日期: 2026-11-21

#### 摘要

BACKGROUND: Activating mutations in the endothelial receptor tyrosine kinase TIE2 are a cause of venous malformations (VMs), primarily through dysregulated PI3K/AKT signaling. However, the downstream mechanisms linking constitutively active mutant TIE2 to enlarged vascular lumens, endothelial dysfunction and VM remain incompletely understood. Here, we investigated the role of RAC1 signaling in mutant TIE2 p.L914F driven VM.
METHODS: Human umbilical vein endothelial cells (HUVECs) expressing wild-type TIE2 or constitutively active mutant TIE2-L914F were analyzed using RAC1 GTP pull-down assays, immunoblotting, and endothelial cell (EC) 2D and 3D functional assays. The role of RAC1 was evaluated using the RAC1 inhibitor NSC23766 in vitro and in vivo xenograft model of VM.
RESULTS & CONCLUSIONS: ECs expressing mutant TIE2-L914F showed increased active GTP-bound RAC1. TIE2-L914F ECs exhibited cytoskeletal changes with enhanced lamellipodia formation and maturation, accompanied by increased migration. Pharmacological inhibition of RAC1 significantly attenuated the aberrant vascular lumen area and migration in the mutant TIE2 ECs. Mechanistically, we showed that RAC1 hyperactivation is PI3K-dependent but not MEK/ERK-dependent. Consistent with these findings, RAC1 inhibition significantly reduced vascular lesion formation in a xenograft model of VM with TIE2-L914F ECs.Our study identifies RAC1 as a downstream mediator of TIE2-L914F driven endothelial dysfunction. These results establish a previously unrecognized PI3K-RAC1 signaling axis in the pathogenesis of TIE2-mutated VMs.

### Brain AVM / AVM（12）

#### [A] Blood Pressure Variability in Brain Arteriovenous Malformations: a Review of Pathophysiological Mechanisms, Clinical Relevance, and Therapeutic Insights.

- PMID: 43037016
- Domain: Brain AVM / AVM
- Domain 依据: Arteriovenous malformation expression in article content
- 转化相关性: Strong
- 转化相关性依据: Related vascular malformation with a transferable mechanism or targeted therapy
- 优先级依据: A: related vascular malformation + transferable mechanism/targeted therapy
- 主题: 脑AVM/动静脉畸形
- 来源类型: 今日新文献
- 期刊: Blood pressure
- 作者: Jiang X, Liang Z, Liang R, Wang Y, Wen S, Jiang T
- 发表日期: 2026 Nov 16
- DOI: 10.1080/08037051.2026.2687988
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/43037016/
- 日报日期: 2026-11-17

#### 摘要

Brain arteriovenous malformations (bAVMs) are high-flow vascular lesions linked to substantial hemorrhage risk and neurological impairment. Traditional risk stratification centers on static hemodynamic parameters, but blood pressure variability (BPV)-fluctuations in blood pressure over time-has emerged as a dynamic, modifiable risk factor. This review explores how pathophysiological pathways link BPV to bAVMs progression and rupture, with specific focus on how aberrant hemodynamics, endothelial dysfunction, compromised cerebral autoregulation, and blood steal syndrome interact. We look at how BPV can worsen wall stress, spark inflammatory responses, and drive vascular restructuring-all of which raise rupture risk. We discuss BPV's clinical impacts in both unruptured and ruptured bAVMs, including how it affects perioperative outcomes and long-term functional recovery. We also assess BPV as a therapeutic target, highlighting pharmacological and monitoring strategies built to stabilize blood pressure fluctuations. Finally, we point out critical knowledge gaps and suggest future research directions to fold BPV into personalized risk assessment and management protocols for bAVMs.

#### [A] The 50 Most-Cited Articles on Craniofacial Vascular Malformations: A Bibliometric and Clinical Network Analysis.

- PMID: 43037565
- Domain: Brain AVM / AVM
- Domain 依据: Arteriovenous malformation expression in article content
- 转化相关性: Strong
- 转化相关性依据: Related vascular malformation with a transferable mechanism or targeted therapy
- 优先级依据: A: related vascular malformation + transferable mechanism/targeted therapy
- 主题: 其他血管畸形/血管异常
- 来源类型: 今日新文献
- 期刊: The Journal of craniofacial surgery
- 作者: Liu S, Wei L, Li L, Zhao J, Ling W, Xu Q, et al.
- 发表日期: 2026 Nov 16
- DOI: 10.1097/SCS.0000000000013573
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/43037565/
- 日报日期: 2026-11-17

#### 摘要

BACKGROUND: Craniofacial vascular malformations require management that accounts for lesion subtype, anatomic extent, functional risk, and esthetic impact. The authors characterized the most influential literature in this field and examined how anatomy, disease subtype, and treatment were related.
METHODS: The Web of Science Core Collection was searched in June 2026. The 50 most-cited eligible articles were evaluated for citation characteristics, journals, countries, institutions, authorship, study design, and level of evidence. VOSviewer was used for collaboration and keyword analyses. Anatomic regions, malformation subtypes, and treatments were manually classified to construct a 3-level co-occurrence network.
RESULTS: The 50 articles accumulated 3733 citations and appeared in 32 journals. The United States contributed 24 articles and 1868 citations. Retrospective studies were most common (n=25), whereas only 3 randomized controlled trials were identified. Research themes shifted from conventional lesion-directed interventions toward laser treatment, targeted therapy, and molecular investigation. The network identified 3 prominent clinical patterns: cervicofacial lymphatic malformations treated with sclerotherapy and surgery; upper facial capillary malformations and port-wine stains treated with laser-based therapy; and arteriovenous malformations treated with embolization and surgery.
CONCLUSIONS: Influential research on craniofacial vascular malformations is multidisciplinary but concentrated in specialist centers and predominantly observational. Relating anatomic site and lesion subtype to treatment provides a clinically interpretable account of the field and supports prospective, multicenter research incorporating esthetic, functional, and patient-reported outcomes.

#### [B] Microsurgery, stereotactic radiosurgery and endovascular treatment of sylvian fissure arteriovenous malformations: a systematic review.

- PMID: 43026378
- Domain: Brain AVM / AVM
- Domain 依据: Arteriovenous malformation expression in article content
- 转化相关性: Moderate
- 转化相关性依据: Related vascular malformation without a strong targeted mechanism signal
- 优先级依据: B: CCM/related vascular-malformation clinical evidence
- 主题: 脑AVM/动静脉畸形
- 来源类型: 近期补位
- 期刊: Neurosurgical review
- 作者: Konovalov AN, Sergeeva SS, Artemyev AA, Erokhin IA, Starostenko DA, Pilipenko YV, et al.
- 发表日期: 2026 Nov 14
- DOI: 10.1007/s10143-026-04579-1
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/43026378/
- 日报日期: 2026-11-17

#### 摘要

Sylvian-fissure-region arteriovenous malformations (AVMs) are uncommon and anatomically heterogeneous; published labels often describe non-comparable lesions or populations. To update evidence for microsurgery, stereotactic radiosurgery (SRS), and endovascular treatment (EVT). Following PRISMA 2020, five databases were searched through May 2026 with citation chaining. Two reviewers independently screened records and extracted data. Dedicated sylvian/perisylvian cohorts were distinguished from broader cohorts and contextual technical reports. Incompatible endpoints, non-independent arms, and potentially overlapping cohorts were not pooled. Risk of bias and applicability were appraised. Nineteen reports were retained: 16 in the evidence synthesis and 3 as contextual background. Microsurgery achieved complete resection in 8/8, 40/41, and 94/94 patients. Transient deficits occurred in 4/8, 4/28, 9/94, and 18/41 patients; postoperative hematoma occurred in 2/94. Mortality windows varied. In the dedicated SRS cohort (n = 87), actuarial obliteration was 35% at 3 years, 60% at 4-5 years, and 76% at 10 years. Cumulative post-SRS hemorrhage was 5% at 1, 5, and 10 years (approximately 1% annually during latency); mortality was unreported. No dedicated curative EVT series for true sylvian AVMs was identified; available data described broader middle cerebral artery cohorts without sylvian-specific angiographic cure. Microsurgery is supported for carefully selected accessible sylvian-fissure AVMs, whereas SRS and embolization have complementary roles. Comparative superiority cannot be inferred. Future studies require explicit anatomical definitions, modality-specific denominators, angiographic endpoints, and standardized follow-up.

#### [B] De Novo Arteriovenous Malformation in a Pediatric Patient: Case Report and Review of the Literature.

- PMID: 29294397
- Domain: Brain AVM / AVM
- Domain 依据: Arteriovenous malformation expression in article content
- 转化相关性: Moderate
- 转化相关性依据: Related vascular malformation without a strong targeted mechanism signal
- 优先级依据: B: CCM/related vascular-malformation clinical evidence
- 主题: 脑AVM/动静脉畸形
- 来源类型: 经典补位
- 期刊: World neurosurgery
- 作者: Santos R, Aguilar-Salinas P, Entwistle JJ, Aldana PR, Beier AD, Hanel RA
- 发表日期: 2018 Mar
- DOI: 10.1016/j.wneu.2017.12.145
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/29294397/
- 日报日期: 2026-11-17

#### 摘要

BACKGROUND: An arteriovenous malformation (AVM) consists of a pathologic arteriovenous shunt formed from a tangle of vessels lacking a capillary bed. AVMs were previously accepted as congenital in nature; however, an increase in the number of reported de novo cerebral AVMs challenges the assertion that all AVMs develop in utero hence, the possibility of these lesions presenting postnatally cannot be excluded. A review of literature revealed 31 published cases of de novo AVM formation between 1996 and 2017.
CASE DESCRIPTION: We present the case of a 7-year-old boy with a history of an intracranial aneurysm that was found to have an asymptomatic de novo AVM formation on a routine follow-up angiography. He was being followed since the age of 8 months when the aneurysm was incidentally found during work-up for mild hemiparesis. At that time, magnetic resonance imaging revealed a 4-mm right posterior communicating artery aneurysm along with a right hemispheric stroke and cortical dysplasia. No arteriovenous malformation was noted.
CONCLUSIONS: The exact pathophysiology of de novo AVMs is not completely understood, but a combination of genetic, molecular, and environmental factors may play a role. Our case report only adds to the growing body of clinical evidence supporting the notion that these lesions may be acquired. It would be appropriate to consider an AVM as an abnormal vascular response to an injury rather than a static congenital lesion.

#### [B] Spontaneous Cystic Arteriovenous Malformation-A Novel Treatment Alternative.

- PMID: 29174879
- Domain: Brain AVM / AVM
- Domain 依据: Arteriovenous malformation expression in article content
- 转化相关性: Moderate
- 转化相关性依据: Related vascular malformation without a strong targeted mechanism signal
- 优先级依据: B: CCM/related vascular-malformation clinical evidence
- 主题: 脑AVM/动静脉畸形
- 来源类型: 经典补位
- 期刊: Journal of stroke and cerebrovascular diseases : the official journal of National Stroke Association
- 作者: Carpani F, Eberbach F, Rodriguez Lucci FM, Miranda JC, Ameriso SF
- 发表日期: 2018 Mar
- DOI: 10.1016/j.jstrokecerebrovasdis.2017.09.034
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/29174879/
- 日报日期: 2026-11-17

#### 摘要

BACKGROUND: Brain arteriovenous malformation (BAVM) is a life-threatening vascular congenital malformation due to the risk of intracerebral hemorrhage. The formation of a spontaneous cyst within a BAVM, without history of hemorrhage or gamma knife surgery, is rare and has an unknown pathophysiology. We suggest a novel theory of spontaneous cystic BAVM formation, subsequently treated with endovascular embolization followed by surgery with a favorable long-term outcome.
METHODS: Review of the literature using PUBMED database and comparison between clinical presentation, diagnostic imaging studies, and treatment options.
RESULTS: The high flow causing a shear stress over the drainage vein may be responsible for venous ectasia and thus incrementing hydrostatic intranidal pressure causing a serous effusion that develops into a pseudocyst. We hypothesize that hemodynamic factors may be responsible for both origin and growth of such cystic lesions.
CONCLUSIONS: Endovascular treatment allowed us to achieve cyst volume reduction by managing its unique flow characteristics. To our knowledge, this is the first case report of combined treatment with endovascular and surgical approach of a cystic BAVM.

#### [B] A Focal "Inferno Sign" With an Aberrant Innominate Artery Feeder: A Thyroid Arteriovenous Malformation Mimicking Malignancy in a Young Woman.

- PMID: 43036464
- Domain: Brain AVM / AVM
- Domain 依据: Arteriovenous malformation expression in article content
- 转化相关性: Moderate
- 转化相关性依据: Related vascular malformation without a strong targeted mechanism signal
- 优先级依据: B: CCM/related vascular-malformation clinical evidence
- 主题: 血管畸形PI3K-AKT-mTOR通路
- 来源类型: 今日新文献
- 期刊: Case reports in endocrinology
- 作者: Nyakubaho A, Ayikoru B, Okwir MA, Nyakake J, Ochom J, Babirye D
- 发表日期: 2026
- DOI: 10.1155/crie/6016666
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/43036464/
- 日报日期: 2026-11-17

#### 摘要

Arteriovenous malformations (AVMs) of the thyroid gland are exceptionally rare, with only 11 cases identified in our review of the literature. They are clinically important because their hypervascularity on imaging can raise suspicion of malignancy, and biopsy or surgery may provoke life-threatening hemorrhage. We present a case of a 30-year-old female with a decade-long history of anterior neck swelling that recently became painful and enlarged. Ultrasound demonstrated a well-defined hypoechoic right-lobe nodule with marked color Doppler hypervascularity (described here as a focal "inferno sign"), a tangled vascular nidus, low-resistance arterial flow in the dominant feeding vessel, and early arterialization of the draining vein; together, these findings suggested arteriovenous shunting. Computed tomography angiography (CTA) characterized the lesion as a 1.0 cm × 0.9 cm intensely enhancing nodule supplied by an aberrant artery arising directly from the innominate artery, immediately medial to the origin of the right common carotid artery, with drainage into the right internal jugular vein. A radiological diagnosis of thyroid AVM was made without histopathological or angiographic confirmation, and fine-needle aspiration (FNA) was avoided because of the bleeding risk. The patient was referred for multidisciplinary management but was lost to follow-up. To our knowledge, this is the first reported thyroid AVM supplied by an innominate artery branch, a variant relevant to surgical and endovascular planning. Thyroid AVM should be considered in the differential diagnosis of hypervascular thyroid nodules, and CTA is valuable for mapping its vascular anatomy noninvasively.

#### [B] Microcatheter navigation using a large compliant balloon placed in a cortical vein during transvenous access.

- PMID: 40555476
- Domain: Brain AVM / AVM
- Domain 依据: Arteriovenous malformation expression in article content

- 作者: Noda R, Che Ros MIA, Saleme S, Rouchaud A, Mounayer C
- 发表日期: 2026 Nov 17
- DOI: 10.1136/jnis-2025-023596
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/40555476/
- 日报日期: 2026-11-18

#### 摘要

Transvenous embolization (TVE) has emerged as the new approach for the curative treatment of brain arteriovenous malformations (bAVMs), though challenges persist in navigating microcatheters through dural venous sinuses. This video article demonstrates a novel balloon-assisted technique to facilitate microcatheter navigation in a complex case of bAVM. A large compliant Copernic RC balloon was temporarily placed in a cortical vein, serving as a buttress to guide the microcatheter into the draining vein. This method enabled the successful cannulation and advancement of a microcatheter in a draining cortical vein using a large compliant balloon without complications. The case highlights the technical feasibility and safety of this approach in TVE for challenging bAVM cases. neurintsurg;18/12/2323/V1F1V1Video 1 Microcatheter navigation using a large compliant balloon placed in a cortical vein during a transvenous access.

#### 摘要

Treatment of non-bleeding cerebral arteriovenous malformations (AVMs) causes a lot of controversy among neurosurgeons around the world. The most discussed issue is choosing the method and indications for a certain treatment option. Despite the accumulated data, including the results of randomized studies, there is no consensus on this issue among experts. The purpose of this review is to analyze current concepts of the management and reasonability of microsurgical treatment of non-bleeding cerebral AVMs based on the latest published studies.

#### [B] Volume-staged versus dose-staged stereotactic radiosurgery outcomes for large brain arteriovenous malformations: a systematic review.

- PMID: 28128692
- Domain: Brain AVM / AVM
- Domain 依据: Arteriovenous malformation expression in article content
- 转化相关性: Moderate
- 转化相关性依据: Related vascular malformation without a strong targeted mechanism signal
- 优先级依据: B: CCM/related vascular-malformation clinical evidence
- 主题: 脑AVM/动静脉畸形
- 来源类型: 经典补位
- 期刊: Journal of neurosurgery
- 作者: Ilyas A, Chen CJ, Ding D, Taylor DG, Moosa S, Lee CC, et al.
- 发表日期: 2018 Jan
- DOI: 10.3171/2016.9.JNS161571
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/28128692/
- 日报日期: 2026-11-18

#### 摘要

OBJECTIVE Several recent studies have improved our understanding of the outcomes of volume-staged (VS) and dose-staged (DS) stereotactic radiosurgery (SRS) for the treatment of large (volume > 10 cm3) brain arteriovenous malformations (AVMs). In light of these recent additions to the literature, the aim of this systematic review is to provide an updated comparison of VS-SRS and DS-SRS for large AVMs. METHODS A systematic review of the literature was performed using PubMed to identify cohorts of 5 or more patients with large AVMs who had been treated with VS-SRS or DS-SRS. Baseline data and post-SRS outcomes were extracted for analysis. RESULTS A total of 11 VS-SRS and 10 DS-SRS studies comprising 299 and 219 eligible patients, respectively, were included for analysis. The mean obliteration rates for VS-SRS and DS-SRS were 41.2% (95% CI 31.4%-50.9%) and 32.3% (95% CI 15.9%-48.8%), respectively. Based on pooled individual patient data, the outcomes for patients treated with VS-SRS were obliteration in 40.3% (110/273), symptomatic radiation-induced changes (RICs) in 13.7% (44/322), post-SRS hemorrhage in 19.5% (50/256), and death in 7.4% (24/323); whereas the outcomes for patients treated with DS-SRS were obliteration in 32.7% (72/220), symptomatic RICs in 12.2% (31/254), post-SRS hemorrhage in 10.6% (30/282), and death in 4.6% (13/281). CONCLUSIONS Volume-staged SRS appears to afford higher obliteration rates than those achieved with DS-SRS, although with a less favorable complication profile. Therefore, VS-SRS or DS-SRS may be a reasonable treatment approach for large AVMs, either as stand-alone therapy or as a component of a multimodality management strategy.

#### [B] Unruptured Brain Arteriovenous Malformations: Primary ONYX Embolization in ARUBA (A Randomized Trial of Unruptured Brain Arteriovenous Malformations)-Eligible Patients.

- PMID: 29114088
- Domain: Brain AVM / AVM
- Domain 依据: Arteriovenous malformation expression in article content

- 作者: Singfer U, Hemelsoet D, Vanlangenhove P, Martens F, Verbeke L, Van Roost D, et al.
- 发表日期: 2017 Dec
- DOI: 10.1161/STROKEAHA.117.018605
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/29114088/
- 日报日期: 2026-11-18

#### 摘要

BACKGROUND AND PURPOSE: In light of evidence from ARUBA (A Randomized Trial of Unruptured Brain Arteriovenous Malformations), neurovascular specialists had to reconsider deliberate treatment of unruptured brain arteriovenous malformations (uBAVMs). Our objective was to determine the outcomes of uBAVM treated with primary embolization using ethylene vinyl alcohol (ONYX).
METHODS: Patients with uBAVM who met the inclusion criteria of ARUBA and were treated with primary Onyx embolization were assigned to this retrospective study. The primary outcome was the modified Rankin Scale score. Secondary outcomes were stroke or death because of uBAVM or intervention and uBAVM obliteration.
RESULTS: Sixty-one patients (mean age, 38 years) were included. The median observation period was 60 months. Patients were treated by embolization alone (41.0%), embolization and radiosurgery (57.4%), or embolization and excision (1.6%). Occlusion was achieved in 44 of 57 patients with completed treatment (77.2%). Forty-seven patients (77.1%) had no clinical impairment at the end of observation (modified Rankin Scale score of <2). Twelve patients (19.7%) reached the outcome of stroke or death because of uBAVM or intervention. Treatment-related mortality was 6.6% (4 patients).
CONCLUSIONS: In uBAVM, Onyx embolization alone or combined with stereotactic radiosurgery achieves a high occlusion rate. Morbidity remains a challenge, even if it seems lower than in the ARUBA trial.

#### [B] Long-term staged management of an extensive pelvic and right lower-extremity vascular malformation with major venous abnormalities and postprocedural pulmonary embolism: A case report.

- PMID: 43061714
- Domain: Brain AVM / AVM
- Domain 依据: Arteriovenous malformation expression in article content
- 转化相关性: Moderate
- 转化相关性依据: Related vascular malformation without a strong targeted mechanism signal
- 优先级依据: B: CCM/related vascular-malformation clinical evidence
- 主题: 静脉畸形基础与治疗
- 来源类型: 近期补位
- 期刊: Radiology case reports
- 作者: Shchebetun A, Abramchuk O, Biletska A, Fomenko Y, Koshlak L, Chiernobaievska O, et al.
- 发表日期: 2026 Dec
- DOI: 10.1016/j.radcr.2026.10.042
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/43061714/
- 日报日期: 2026-11-21

#### 摘要

Extensive vascular malformations involving a limb and major named veins may require repeated treatment over many years. Their abnormal venous anatomy may remain clinically important even when local symptoms are controlled, particularly when subsequent interventions are planned. We report the long-term course of a 26-year-old man with a congenital vascular malformation involving the pelvis and right lower extremity, diagnosed at 13 years of age and historically described as an arteriovenous malformation. Historical CT angiography reports described arteriovenous components together with extensive venous abnormalities, including aneurysmal dilatation of the right popliteal and external iliac veins. Staged surgery during adolescence was followed by an approximately 13-year interval without further invasive treatment, during which the patient initially remained clinically stable before gradually developing recurrent symptoms. At 26 years of age, progressive symptoms prompted combined endovenous laser treatment, electrocoagulation, and sclerotherapy. On postoperative day 7, the patient developed occlusive right popliteal venous thrombosis and acute bilateral pulmonary embolism requiring systemic thrombolysis followed by anticoagulation. The patient stabilized after treatment. At approximately 3-month cardiology follow-up, he was asymptomatic, with preserved cardiac function and good exercise tolerance. Longer-term symptom control was maintained with class III compression therapy, therapeutic exercise, and regular physical activity, with improvement in pain and lower-extremity edema. This case illustrates that major venous abnormalities may remain clinically relevant throughout the long-term course of a complex vascular malformation. In patients undergoing further intervention, lesion-specific venous anatomy and pre-existing thrombotic changes within malformed venous spaces should be considered alongside conventional peri-procedural thromboembolic risk factors. Long-term management should remain individualized and focused on symptoms, function, and surveillance of major vascular abnormalities.

#### [C] Progressive Plantar Foot Pain as a Presentation of Arteriovenous Malformation: A Case Report of Diagnostic Delay.

- PMID: 43034803
- Domain: Brain AVM / AVM
- Domain 依据: Arteriovenous malformation expression in article content
- 转化相关性: Moderate
- 转化相关性依据: Related vascular malformation without a strong targeted mechanism signal

- 主题: 静脉畸形基础与治疗
- 来源类型: 近期补位
- 期刊: Cureus
- 作者: Zheng HW
- 发表日期: 2026 Oct
- DOI: 10.7759/cureus.117941
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/43034803/
- 日报日期: 2026-11-17

#### 摘要

Arteriovenous malformations (AVMs) of the foot are rare vascular anomalies that may present with nonspecific symptoms and mimic common emergency department conditions. We report a 26-year-old man with progressive plantar foot pain and swelling who had multiple healthcare encounters before an AVM was diagnosed. Initial evaluation included unremarkable radiographs, treatment for presumed cellulitis, and negative venous duplex ultrasonography for deep venous thrombosis. Persistent and worsening symptoms ultimately prompted targeted ultrasonography, which identified a plantar AVM. On subsequent emergency department presentation for severe pain and inability to bear weight, examination demonstrated a warm, exquisitely tender plantar lesion with pulsatile Doppler flow, and computed tomography angiography confirmed the diagnosis. The patient underwent transarterial embolization with subsequent improvement in pain, swelling, and ambulation. This case highlights the importance of reconsidering the differential diagnosis in patients with persistent or recurrent symptoms, particularly when clinical features are atypical for common conditions such as cellulitis or when abnormal vascular findings are present.

#### [A] [Vascular ultrasonography].

- PMID: 22358939
- Domain: Other vascular malformation
- Domain 依据: Other vascular malformation/anomaly expression in article content
- 转化相关性: Strong
- 转化相关性依据: Related vascular malformation with a transferable mechanism or targeted therapy
- 优先级依据: A: related vascular malformation + transferable mechanism/targeted therapy
- 主题: 静脉畸形基础与治疗
- 来源类型: 经典补位
- 期刊: Der Internist
- 作者: Stiegler H
- 发表日期: 2012 Mar
- DOI: 10.1007/s00108-011-2956-7
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/22358939/
- 日报日期: 2026-11-18

#### 摘要

Vascular ultrasound plays an important role in the visual depiction of arteries, veins, and changes of tissue in lymphatic diseases. In the case of arteries, this ranges from endothelial dysfunction over measuring the increase of intima media thickness to the detection of stenoses, occlusion, or aneurysm. Ultrasound helps to differentiate in functional arterial diseases such as primary and secondary Raynaud's syndrome as well as arterial compression syndromes like entrapment syndrome of different arterial regions or the chronic exceptional compartment syndrome of the lower leg. Ultrasound plays a central role in the diagnosis of rare arterial diseases like large vessel vasculitis, arterial dissection, cystic adventitial degeneration, and the differentiation of vascular malformation especially in children, thus, permitting ultrasound-guided intervention and follow-up controls. In venous thrombosis, sonography is the primary imaging method, while follow-up controls help in the prediction of recurrent venous thrombosis. Ultrasound is a tool to determine the cause and severity of chronic venous insufficiency and allows different therapeutic procedures for the treatment of varicose veins to be visually monitored.

#### [A] Age-Stratified Sirolimus Trough Concentrations in Pediatric Vascular Malformations: A Real-World Retrospective Study.

- PMID: 43062181
- Domain: Other vascular malformation
- Domain 依据: Other vascular malformation/anomaly expression in article content
- 转化相关性: Strong
- 转化相关性依据: Related vascular malformation with a transferable mechanism or targeted therapy
- 优先级依据: A: related vascular malformation + transferable mechanism/targeted therapy
- 主题: 血管畸形PI3K-AKT-mTOR通路
- 来源类型: 今日新文献
- 期刊: Therapeutic drug monitoring
- 作者: Jung SM, Ha S, Kwon YJ, Kwon H, Namgoong JM, Kim DY
- 发表日期: 2026 Nov 20
- DOI: 10.1097/FTD.0000000000001554
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/43062181/
- 日报日期: 2026-11-21

#### 摘要

BACKGROUND: Sirolimus is increasingly being used off-label to treat pediatric vascular malformations. In this study, the age-stratified drug exposure, pharmacokinetic variability, and long-term treatment characteristics of sirolimus were evaluated in a real-world pediatric population.
METHODS: In this study, 144 patients (younger than 19 years) with vascular malformations who were treated with sirolimus at a single center (July 2018 and March 2025) were retrospectively analyzed. A total of 2054 therapeutic drug monitoring measurements were obtained. The patients were stratified into 6 age groups (1 month and younger to 12-18 years). The outcomes included trough concentration, interindividual variability (coefficient of variation), target range attainment, treatment duration, and MRI-based response.
RESULTS: The median follow-up was 18.3 months (range, 0.3-74.1 months). Neonates (1 month and younger) showed the highest interindividual variability (coefficient of variation = 61.6%), with lower variability observed in adolescents (40.0%, P < 0.001). Overall, 73.4% of the trough concentrations were within the target range (4-10 ng/mL), and the mean duration within the therapeutic range per patient was 70.9%. Supratherapeutic levels (>15 ng/mL) were most frequent in neonates (6.6%). Prolonged therapy (≥24 months) was observed in 31.9% of patients, with a median duration of 35.1 months. Among the 44 patients who underwent follow-up MRI, 84.1% showed disease improvement or stability.
CONCLUSIONS: Sustained long-term treatment continuity suggests that sirolimus was generally tolerated across pediatric age groups, although direct toxicity data were not systematically captured in this study. Younger patients exhibited greater pharmacokinetic variability, necessitating closer monitoring and cautious dose adjustment. Prospective PK-PD studies incorporating standardized clinical outcomes are needed to define optimal trough targets and individualized dosing strategies.

#### [B] General anesthesia with supplemental oxygen during HMME-mediated photodynamic therapy for port wine stains: a retrospective comparison study.

- PMID: 43025828
- Domain: Other vascular malformation
- Domain 依据: Other vascular malformation/anomaly expression in article content
- 转化相关性: Moderate
- 转化相关性依据: Related vascular malformation without a strong targeted mechanism signal
- 优先级依据: B: CCM/related vascular-malformation clinical evidence
- 主题: 静脉畸形基础与治疗
- 来源类型: 近期补位
- 期刊: Photodiagnosis and photodynamic therapy
- 作者: Diao P, Han C, Liu L, Li X, Zhang L, Zhou H, et al.
- 发表日期: 2026 Nov 13
- DOI: 10.1016/j.pdpdt.2026.105713
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/43025828/
- 日报日期: 2026-11-17

#### 摘要

BACKGROUND: Port wine stain (PWS) is a congenital, progressive vascular malformation that can cause significant disfigurement and psychosocial distress. HMME-mediated photodynamic therapy has been approved in China for PWS and has shown promising efficacy, particularly for lesions with limited response to pulsed dye laser(PDL). General anesthesia (GA) is administered to control treatment-related pain and ensure immobilization, and it is commonly accompanied by supplemental oxygen. However, the effect of supplemental oxygen on HMME-PDT efficacy remains unclear.
METHODS: This retrospective study included 247 PWS patients under the age of 18, who received a single session of HMME-PDT under three clinical protocols: non-GA with air inhalation (21% FiO2, n=95), GA with 50% air and 50% oxygen (≈60% FiO2, n=49), and GA with pure oxygen (100% FiO2, n=103). Treatment outcomes were assessed using standardized quartile grading (excellent ≥75%, good 50-74%, fair 25-49%, poor ≤24%) at least two months post-treatment. SpO2 was continuously monitored during treatment, and available SpO2 data were compared among groups.
RESULTS: The overall improvement rate (including fair, good, and excellent responses) was 65.3% in the non-GA/air group, 83.7% in the GA/60% FiO2 group, and 88.3% in the GA/100% FiO2 group. The corresponding rates of excellent improvement were 7.2%, 26.5%, and 35.0%, respectively. Both GA groups showed significantly better outcomes than the non-GA group (p<0.001). No significant difference was observed between the GA/60% FiO2 and GA/100% FiO2 groups (p=0.243). SpO2 remained ≥95% during HMME infusion and illumination in all groups, with no significant intergroup difference (p>0.05). No severe systemic adverse events occurred.
CONCLUSION: GA-based protocols were associated with improved efficacy compared with awake treatment under room air breathing, possible attributed to the integrated anesthetic regimen - pain control, immobilization, hemodynamic stability, and controlled ventilation.

#### 摘要

Spinal dural arteriovenous fistulas are rare spinal vascular malformations which can cause progressive paraparesis and paraplegia if not treated. As symptoms are unspecific diagnosis is often delayed and clinical outcome is dependent on early therapy. While magnetic resonance imaging (MRI) is the first choice imaging procedure, selective spinal digital subtraction angiography is necessary to analyze the angioarchitecture and to plan the treatment. This article provides an overview on the epidemiology, etiology, clinical aspects and imaging features as well as therapeutic aspects of spinal dural arteriovenous fistulas. Knowledge of spinal vascular anatomy is the basis for understanding spinal dural arteriovenous fistulas.

#### [B] [Gorham's disease and intra-osseous vascular abnormalities].

- PMID: 22504738
- Domain: Other vascular malformation
- Domain 依据: Other vascular malformation/anomaly expression in article content

- 作者: Héritier S, Donadieu J
- 发表日期: 2012 May
- DOI: 10.1684/bdc.2012.1572
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/22504738/
- 日报日期: 2026-11-17

#### 摘要

Gorham's disease is a rare condition of unknown etiology, which is characterized by intra-osseous vascular abnormalities leading to osteolysis and sometimes, extension to adjacent tissues. Although there is no histological evidence of malignancy, the lesions display an aggressive potential responsible of osteolysis with pathological fractures and respiratory failure. The extension of the disease varies from one location to multiple bone lesions throughout the skeleton and the clinical presentations depend on the sites of involvement. In the ISSVA (International Society for the Study of Vascular Abnormalities) classification, its affiliation to the group of vascular tumors or vascular malformations is still debated. To date, there is no standard treatment defined for this disease. The proposed treatments are inhibitors of bone resorption (bisphosphonates) and interferon alpha therapy or anti-angiogenic molecules. Radiation therapy and surgery may also have a place in the therapeutic strategy.

#### [B] Advantages of blood pool contrast agents in MR angiography: a pictorial review.

- PMID: 22498192

- 作者: Lewis M, Yanny S, Malcolm PN
- 发表日期: 2012 Apr
- DOI: 10.1111/j.1754-9485.2012.02347.x
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/22498192/
- 日报日期: 2026-11-17

#### 摘要

Gadofosveset trisodium (Ablavar®, formerly Vasovist®) is the first intravascular contrast agent approved for clinical use in peripheral vascular disease. The purpose of this review is to illustrate the clinical uses of gadofosveset-enhanced magnetic resonance angiography in patients referred for assessment of arteriovenous disease. Superior T1 shortening enables first pass renal and peripheral arteriography of quality comparable with larger doses of extracellular agents. In applications such as thoracic outlet syndrome, there may be other advantages such as superior venous imaging and need for only one injection. Steady-state delayed imaging provides high resolution mapping of both arterial and venous systems and imaging of multiple territories. A combination of dynamic and delayed steady-state imaging can provide detailed anatomy and flow characteristics of vascular malformations and mapping for percutaneous sclerotherapy at one investigation. The ability to image in the steady state can provide minimally invasive imaging of thrombo-occlusive disease of central veins.

#### [B] A Practical Reference for Stereotactic Radiosurgery Planning for Intracranial Indications.

- PMID: 42294942

- 作者: Meng Y, Mashiach E, Donahue B, Bernstein K, Silverman JS, Golfinos J, et al.
- 发表日期: 2026 Dec 01
- DOI: 10.1227/neu.0000000000004092
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42294942/
- 日报日期: 2026-11-17

#### 摘要

Stereotactic radiosurgery evolved from the desire to achieve highly precise and accurate target ablation using radiation energy. Over the past 3 decades, the range of indications for stereotactic radiosurgery has expanded significantly, making it a valuable treatment option-and often a first-line approach-for various intracranial conditions, including benign and malignant brain tumors, vascular malformations, and functional disorders. This narrative review offers a comprehensive, yet not exhaustive, overview of the current evidence, technical considerations, and areas of nuance and controversy regarding these indications and dose selection. It serves as a quick reference guide for neurosurgeons and radiation oncologists working in this field. In addition, tables are included that detail the indications, expected results, dose prescriptions, and anticipated outcomes, assisting clinicians in both clinical settings and procedural planning.

#### [B] Bevacizumab in patients with hereditary hemorrhagic telangiectasia and severe hepatic vascular malformations and high cardiac output.

- PMID: 22396517
- Domain: Other vascular malformation
- Domain 依据: Other vascular malformation/anomaly expression in article content
- 转化相关性: Moderate
- 转化相关性依据: Related vascular malformation without a strong targeted mechanism signal
- 优先级依据: B: CCM/related vascular-malformation clinical evidence
- 主题: 静脉畸形基础与治疗
- 来源类型: 经典补位
- 期刊: JAMA
- 作者: Dupuis-Girod S, Ginon I, Saurin JC, Marion D, Guillot E, Decullier E, et al.
- 发表日期: 2012 Mar 07
- DOI: 10.1001/jama.2012.250
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/22396517/
- 日报日期: 2026-11-18

#### 摘要

CONTEXT: The only treatment available to restore normal cardiac output in patients with hereditary hemorrhagic telangiectasia (HHT) and cardiac failure is liver transplant. Anti-vascular endothelial growth factor treatments such as bevacizumab may be an effective treatment.
OBJECTIVES: To test the efficacy of bevacizumab in reducing high cardiac output in severe hepatic forms of HHT and to assess improvement in epistaxis duration and quality of life.
DESIGN, SETTING, AND PATIENTS: Single-center, phase 2 trial with national recruitment from the French HHT Network. Patients were 18 to 70 years old and had confirmed HHT, severe liver involvement, and a high cardiac index related to HHT.
INTERVENTION: Bevacizumab, 5 mg per kg, every 14 days for a total of 6 injections. The total duration of the treatment was 2.5 months; patients were followed up for 6 months after the beginning of the treatment.
MAIN OUTCOME MEASURE: Decrease in cardiac output at 3 months after the first injection, evaluated by echocardiography.
RESULTS: A total of 25 patients were included between March 2009 and November 2010. Of the 24 patients who had echocardiograms available for reread, there was a response in 20 of 24 patients with normalization of cardiac index (complete response [CR]) in 3 of 24, partial response (PR) in 17 of 24, and no response in 4 cases. Median cardiac index at beginning of the treatment was 5.05 L/min/m(2) (range, 4.1-6.2) and significantly decreased at 3 months after the beginning of the treatment with a median cardiac index of 4.2 L/min/m(2) (range, 2.9-5.2; P < .001). Median cardiac index at 6 months was significantly lower than before treatment (4.1 L/min/m(2); range, 3.0-5.1). Among 23 patients with available data at 6 months, we observed CR in 5 cases, PR in 15 cases, and no response in 3 cases. Mean duration of epistaxis, which was 221 minutes per month (range, 0-947) at inclusion, had significantly decreased at 3 months (134 minutes; range, 0-656) and 6 months (43 minutes; range, 0-310) (P = .008). Quality of life had significantly improved. The most severe adverse events were 2 cases of grade 3 systemic hypertension, which were successfully treated.
CONCLUSION: In this preliminary study of patients with HHT associated with severe hepatic vascular malformations and high cardiac output, administration of bevacizumab was associated with a decrease in cardiac output and reduced duration and number of episodes of epistaxis.
TRIAL REGISTRATION: clinicaltrials.gov Identifier: NCT00843440.

- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/21796011/
- 日报日期: 2026-11-18

#### 摘要

BACKGROUND: Giant perimedullary arteriovenous fistulas (GPMAVFs) located in the cervical region are a rare pathology with distinctive characteristics.
OBJECTIVE: To evaluate clinical presentation and different endovascular treatment options of cervical GPMAVFs and review previously published data in the literature regarding cervical GPMAVFs.
METHODS: Six patients with cervical GPMAVFs were found in the spinal vascular malformations database of our group collected between 1990 and 2009. Endovascular techniques and treatment outcomes were evaluated and compared with other published series.
RESULTS: Clinical presentations were progressive motor deficit (5 patients), hematomyelia (1 patient), meningeal syndrome (1 patient), and respiratory arrest and gait apraxia (1 patient). Three patients were treated by the transarterial approach. One patient was treated by the transvenous approach due to previous embolizations resulting in a proximal occlusion and preventing a safe transarterial approach. A transvenous approach was used in another patient due to complex arterial anatomy. In 1 patient, direct percutaneous puncture of the venous pouch was necessary because of previous proximal occlusion of the arteries. All embolizations resulted in complete occlusions with clinical improvement, and there was no recanalization during a mean follow-up of 21 months.
CONCLUSION: Transarterial embolization of cervical GPMAVFs is safe and effective when it is done in highly experienced centers. Cervical GPMAVFs that cannot be accessed by the transarterial technique due to their complex angioarchitecture can be treated by transvenous embolization or direct puncture of the venous pouch.

#### [B] Case report: gastrointestinal hemorrhage caused by a pancreas transplant arteriovenous fistula with large psuedoanuerysm 9 years after transplantation.

- 发表日期: 2011 Dec

#### 摘要

Reported cases of arteriovenous fistulae in transplant recipients are uncommon. We present a case of an arteriovenous fistula associated with a large pseudoaneurysm in the root of the small bowel mesentery of a pancreas transplant. Uniquely, in our case, the arteriovenous fistula presented with an episode of gastrointestinal (GI) hemorrhage 9 years postoperatively. Radiographic imaging including coronal computed tomography angiogram and conventional angiogram demonstrated an arteriovenous fistula in the patient's pancreas transplant between the distal superior mesenteric artery (SMA) and superior mesenteric vein (SMV) with 6 cm aneurysmal dilatation. The tremendous flow in the fistula in the root of the graft small intestine mesentery led to graft duodenal mucosal congestion and lower GI hemorrhage. After successful embolization of the SMA-SMV fistula and pseudoaneurysm using interventional radiographic techniques, the arteriovenous fistula remained thrombosed. The patient had no further episodes of GI bleeding and her endoscopic evaluation was otherwise negative. The presence of arteriovenous fistulae and pseudoaneurysms in pancreas transplant recipients is uncommon, but has been previously documented. This case is further distinguished from previous reports by the notable 9-year interval between transplantation and the onset of hemorrhage. Historically, symptomatic vascular malformations have been associated with significant patient morbidity and mortality. Successful patient management involves timely and accurate diagnosis and intervention.

#### [B] Case Report: Submandibular plunging ranula secondary to sublingual gland venous vascular malformation.

- PMID: 43054327
- Domain: Other vascular malformation
- Domain 依据: Other vascular malformation/anomaly expression in article content

- 发表日期: 2026
- DOI: 10.3389/fsurg.2026.2002032
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/43054327/
- 日报日期: 2026-11-21

#### 摘要

Venous vascular malformation of the sublingual gland is rare, and secondary submandibular plunging ranula has not been previously documented. Here we report a case of a 32-year-old man presenting with a painless, progressively enlarging mass in the right submandibular region for one month. No abnormalities were found in the oral floor. Preoperative ultrasonography and magnetic resonance imaging revealed a well-circumscribed oval mass measuring approximately 40 mm × 30 mm in the submandibular region, with a tortuous vascular isthmus ("tail sign") extending from the posterosuperior aspect of the mass toward the sublingual space. The lesions demonstrated low signal on T1-weighted imaging and high signal on T2-weighted imaging. Contrast-enhanced T1 imaging highlighted the sublingual gland, the tortuous vascular isthmus, and the wall of the submandibular mass. Intraoperatively, the cyst, the sublingual gland, and the isthmus connecting them, were completely excised. The contents of the cyst were viscous, egg-white-like fluid. Hematoxylin-eosin staining revealed malformation-like proliferation of thin-walled vessels accompanied by hemorrhage or congestion within the sublingual gland and the outer layer of the cyst wall. Immunohistochemistry showed positive expression of CD31 and CD34 and negative expression of D2-40 in both the sublingual gland and the outer layer of the cyst wall, supporting a blood vascular (non-lymphatic) endothelial phenotype; VEGF expression was also observed. Clinicopathological diagnosis was a case of venous vascular malformation with plunging ranula of the sublingual gland. The patient recovered uneventfully without complications, and no recurrence was observed during 54 months follow-up. We hypothesize that the venous vascular malformation contributed to ranula formation, although temporal causality cannot be established from a single case.

#### [B] Spontaneous Renal Rupture Requiring Nephrectomy in a Patient With IgA Nephropathy.

- DOI: 10.1111/nep.70323

- 日报日期: 2026-11-21

#### 摘要

Approximately 5% of patients who develop renal rupture lack an identifiable underlying lesion, such as tumours, hydronephrosis, or vascular malformations. In this case report, we detail a case with a left renal rupture of a 29-year-old male diagnosed with IgA nephropathy (IgAN) and hypertensive nephropathy by renal biopsy. The patient was hospitalized with hypertension accompanied by renal dysfunction and heart failure. Before the onset of renal rupture, he had been initiated on corticosteroids for IgAN, and anticoagulant for persistent atrial fibrillation. The patient presented with sudden-onset left lower flank pain and haemorrhagic shock. Abdominal computed tomography revealed a left renal haemorrhage. Despite emergency angiography and transcatheter renal artery embolization on two occasions, the bleeding remained uncontrolled, ultimately requiring a left nephrectomy. Given the anatomical discrepancy between the rupture site and the biopsy puncture site, as well as the clinical course in which haemorrhage occurred more than 30 days following the renal biopsy, the biopsy was considered unlikely to be the direct etiological cause. This case suggests that renal rupture may have been promoted by renal vascular injury due to severe hypertension, uraemia, anticoagulation and corticosteroid-related tissue fragility. This case highlights that life-threatening renal rupture can occur even without tumour or vascular malformation and may arise from multiple coexisting bleeding-risk factors.

#### [B] Intrahepatic Portosystemic Shunt as a Cause of Recurrent Encephalopathy in a Non-cirrhotic Patient: Successful Combined Transhepatic and Transjugular Embolization.

- PMID: 43055015
- Domain: Other vascular malformation
- Domain 依据: Other vascular malformation/anomaly expression in article content

- DOI: 10.7759/cureus.118185
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/43055015/
- 日报日期: 2026-11-22

#### 摘要

Intrahepatic portosystemic shunts (IPSS) are rare vascular anomalies that can cause recurrent encephalopathy in non-cirrhotic patients. We present a case of a female with recurrent episodes of delirium, in whom initial laboratory tests showed hyperammonemia with preserved hepatic function. Cross-sectional imaging revealed a left-sided IPSS connecting distal branches of the left portal vein to the left hepatic vein. Given her persistent neurocognitive symptoms despite optimal medical therapy, endovascular closure was performed using a dual-access strategy: transhepatic coil embolization of the portal afferent branches, followed by transjugular deployment of an Amplatzer plug in the venous outflow. After the procedure, the patient experienced full resolution of symptoms, with no recurrent neurological episodes during follow-up. This case highlights the importance of considering IPSS as a reversible cause of unexplained encephalopathy and supports the efficacy of combined-access embolization.

#### [B] The Effectiveness of Pulsed Dye Laser Combined With CO2 Fractional Laser in the Treatment of Port-Wine Stain in Chinese Patients: A Retrospective Study.

- PMID: 43047021
- Domain: Other vascular malformation
- Domain 依据: Other vascular malformation/anomaly expression in article content
- 转化相关性: Moderate
- 转化相关性依据: Related vascular malformation without a strong targeted mechanism signal

- 主题: 其他血管畸形/血管异常

- 作者: Ning J, Li M, Yang Y, Fu G, Su X, Wang C, et al.
- 发表日期: 2026 Jul
- DOI: 10.1111/jocd.71055
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/43047021/
- 日报日期: 2026-11-22

#### 摘要

BACKGROUND: Port-wine stain (PWS) is a common type of congenital capillary malformation, often occurring on the face and causing a significant psychological impact on patients. Until now, pulsed dye laser (PDL) remains the criterion standard for PWS treatment, but some patients show poor effectiveness and treatment-resistance. Fractional laser was previously tried for the treatment in several cases of PWS. However, the effectiveness of its combination with PDL is still unclear.
OBJECTIVE: This study aims to investigate the effectiveness of PDL combined with CO2 fractional laser (CO2FL) in treating PWS.
METHODS: A retrospective analysis was performed based on the PWS patients who received PDL combined with CO2FL treatment in Xijing Hospital, China from January 2014 to December 2024. Clearance rates were recorded to measure clinical effectiveness.
RESULTS: A total of 91 PWS patients were enrolled and analyzed. The age range is from 20 days to 60 years (median age, 11 years), 33 males (36.26%), and 58 females (63.74%). The sites of lesions were predominantly located on the head and face (92.31%), followed by the limbs and trunk (4.40%), and the neck (3.30%). Eighteen patients (19.78%) exhibit nodular hypertrophy type. The results showed that after six sessions of treatments, the average clearance rate was 49.85% ± 22.65%, reaching the plateau. To evaluate the optimal timing of the CO2FL combination, we categorized the patients into two groups: those who commenced the combination of PDL and CO2FL within the first five sessions, referred to as the early combination group, and those who began the combination after five sessions, designated as the delayed combination group. After eight treatment sessions, compared with the delayed combination group, patients in the early combination group had a higher clearance rate (62.23% ± 23.04% vs. 52.37% ± 22.23%, p < 0.05).
CONCLUSION: The combination of PDL and CO2FL is safe and effective for treating PWS. Early combination therapy is associated with better treatment outcomes.

#### [A] Blood Pressure Variability in Brain Arteriovenous Malformations: a Review of Pathophysiological Mechanisms, Clinical Relevance, and Therapeutic Insights.

- PMID: 43037016
- Domain: Brain AVM / AVM
- Domain 依据: Arteriovenous malformation expression in article content
- 转化相关性: Strong
- 转化相关性依据: Related vascular malformation with a transferable mechanism or targeted therapy

- 来源类型: 今日新文献
- 期刊: Blood pressure
- 作者: Jiang X, Liang Z, Liang R, Wang Y, Wen S, Jiang T
- 发表日期: 2026 Nov 16
- DOI: 10.1080/08037051.2026.2687988
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/43037016/
- 日报日期: 2026-11-17

#### 摘要

Brain arteriovenous malformations (bAVMs) are high-flow vascular lesions linked to substantial hemorrhage risk and neurological impairment. Traditional risk stratification centers on static hemodynamic parameters, but blood pressure variability (BPV)-fluctuations in blood pressure over time-has emerged as a dynamic, modifiable risk factor. This review explores how pathophysiological pathways link BPV to bAVMs progression and rupture, with specific focus on how aberrant hemodynamics, endothelial dysfunction, compromised cerebral autoregulation, and blood steal syndrome interact. We look at how BPV can worsen wall stress, spark inflammatory responses, and drive vascular restructuring-all of which raise rupture risk. We discuss BPV's clinical impacts in both unruptured and ruptured bAVMs, including how it affects perioperative outcomes and long-term functional recovery. We also assess BPV as a therapeutic target, highlighting pharmacological and monitoring strategies built to stabilize blood pressure fluctuations. Finally, we point out critical knowledge gaps and suggest future research directions to fold BPV into personalized risk assessment and management protocols for bAVMs.

#### [A] Hypertensive stress rewires macrophage signaling in heart failure with preserved ejection fraction independent of obesity.

- PMID: 43035499
- Domain: Cross-domain vascular biology
- Domain 依据: Transferable mechanism appears with explicit vascular/endothelial context
- 转化相关性: Moderate
- 转化相关性依据: Different disease with a highly transferable vascular/endothelial mechanism

- 期刊: Nature communications
- 作者: Kinoshita Y, Sato M, Kadomatsu T, Katoh M, Ikeda K, Yoshioka G, et al.
- 发表日期: 2026 Oct 16
- DOI: 10.1038/s41467-026-78375-2
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/43035499/
- 日报日期: 2026-11-17

#### 摘要

Heart failure with preserved ejection fraction (HFpEF) remains a major unmet clinical challenge. Although obesity is common in many HFpEF cohorts from Europe and North America, three Japanese HFpEF cohorts identified hypertension and sympathetic overactivation, rather than obesity, as dominant features. To model these features, we established an HFpEF mouse model in which sympathetic activation induces hypertensive heart disease, while endothelial dysfunction acts as a second hit driving progression to HFpEF. Hypertensive stress rewires cardiac macrophage-derived intercellular signaling, impairing macrophage-to-cardiomyocyte BMP signaling and fatty acid oxidation while enhancing macrophage-to-fibroblast PDGF signaling, thereby promoting metabolic remodeling, inflammation, and fibrosis. ANGPTL2 inhibition preserves macrophage intercellular communication and prevents HFpEF development. These findings identify cardiac macrophage signaling rewiring as a central mechanism linking hypertensive stress to metabolic remodeling and inflammation, revealing a pathogenic pathway for HFpEF in the absence of obesity that is distinct from HFpEF associated with obesity.

#### [A] [Vascular ultrasonography].

- PMID: 22358939
- Domain: Other vascular malformation
- Domain 依据: Other vascular malformation/anomaly expression in article content

- 作者: Stiegler H
- 发表日期: 2012 Mar
- DOI: 10.1007/s00108-011-2956-7
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/22358939/
- 日报日期: 2026-11-18

#### 摘要

Vascular ultrasound plays an important role in the visual depiction of arteries, veins, and changes of tissue in lymphatic diseases. In the case of arteries, this ranges from endothelial dysfunction over measuring the increase of intima media thickness to the detection of stenoses, occlusion, or aneurysm. Ultrasound helps to differentiate in functional arterial diseases such as primary and secondary Raynaud's syndrome as well as arterial compression syndromes like entrapment syndrome of different arterial regions or the chronic exceptional compartment syndrome of the lower leg. Ultrasound plays a central role in the diagnosis of rare arterial diseases like large vessel vasculitis, arterial dissection, cystic adventitial degeneration, and the differentiation of vascular malformation especially in children, thus, permitting ultrasound-guided intervention and follow-up controls. In venous thrombosis, sonography is the primary imaging method, while follow-up controls help in the prediction of recurrent venous thrombosis. Ultrasound is a tool to determine the cause and severity of chronic venous insufficiency and allows different therapeutic procedures for the treatment of varicose veins to be visually monitored.

#### [A] Hypoxia-activated microglia promote pathological angiogenesis via an FN/ITGB3/FAK/Akt pathway.

- PMID: 42959614
- Domain: Cross-domain vascular biology
- Domain 依据: Transferable mechanism appears with explicit vascular/endothelial context
- 转化相关性: Moderate
- 转化相关性依据: Different disease with a highly transferable vascular/endothelial mechanism

- 主题: 神经免疫/炎症治疗
- 来源类型: 今日新文献
- 期刊: International journal of ophthalmology
- 作者: Hao LC, Li DH, Zhang K, Liu CZ, Sun MH, Gao XX, et al.
- 发表日期: 2026
- DOI: 10.18240/ijo.2026.11.02
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42959614/
- 日报日期: 2026-11-18

#### 摘要

AIM: To investigate whether hypoxia-activated microglia drive pathological neovascularization through extracellular matrix (ECM) remodeling and endothelial integrin signaling, a mechanism that may function in parallel with vascular endothelial growth factor (VEGF) signaling.
METHODS: The oxygen-induced retinopathy (OIR) mouse model was used. Retinal phenotypes were assessed by flat mounts, histology, and vascular leakage assays. Key pathways were screened by transcriptomics and validated via Western blot, immunofluorescence, and quantitative polymerase chain reaction (qPCR). In vitro, brain microvascular endothelial cells were treated with conditioned medium from hypoxic microglia (Hyp-CM), with cell migration and tube formation assessed. Interventions employed the integrin β3 (ITGB3) inhibitor cilengitide, the focal adhesion kinase (FAK) inhibitor PF-573228 and a neutralizing anti-VEGF antibody. In vivo, cilengitide was administered by intravitreal injection to evaluate its therapeutic effect on neovascularization.
RESULTS: OIR retinas showed significant avascularity, pathological neovascularization, and barrier disruption. Transcriptomics revealed activation of ECM-receptor interaction and focal adhesion pathways, with upregulation of Fn1 and Itgb3. Increased protein levels of fibronectin (FN) and ITGB3, alongside elevated phosphorylation of FAK and protein kinase B (Akt), were confirmed in vivo. Activated microglia co-localized with upregulated FN and ITGB3. Conditioned medium from Hyp-CM activated the ITGB3-FAK-Akt pathway in endothelial cells and promoted their migration and tube formation. These effects were nearly abolished by cilengitide or PF-573228. VEGF neutralization partially reduced Hyp-CM-induced migration and tube formation. Intravitreal injection of cilengitide significantly reduced retinal neovascularization in OIR mice.
CONCLUSION: Hypoxia-activated microglia promote pathological angiogenesis, and the FN/ITGB3/FAK/Akt axis is identified as a key mediator of this process, revealing a novel mechanism that operates in parallel with VEGF signaling. Targeting this "hypoxia-microglia-ECM-integrin" axis represents a promising therapeutic strategy to overcome anti-VEGF resistance in ischemic retinopathy.

#### [A] Bacteria-responsive programmed antibacterial liposome to modulate the inflammatory microenvironment for intracranial methicillin-resistant Staphylococcus aureus infections.

- Domain: Cross-domain vascular biology

#### 摘要

For intracranial methicillin-resistant Staphylococcus aureus (MRSA) infections, conventional antibiotics exhibit poor blood-brain barrier (BBB) permeability and limited anti-inflammatory activity. To address these challenges, we developed a precisely targeted liposomal system, QAI-lipo, which combines passive BBB accumulation, potent antibiotic-free antibacterial activity, favorable biosafety, and effective modulation of inflammation. Benefiting from lipophilic properties resembling those of cell membranes, QAI-lipo can efficiently cross the BBB. Upon reaching intracranial infection foci, the liposomes are triggered to rupture and release encapsulated payloads by α-toxin secreted by MRSA. Meanwhile, QAI-lipo achieves robust antibacterial effects via dual mechanisms: direct disruption of bacterial membranes and photodynamic action. Animal experiments further verify that QAI-lipo exerts anti-inflammatory activity by inhibiting the MyD88/TRAF-6 signaling pathway, which downregulates the expression of downstream proinflammatory cytokines and upregulates that of anti-inflammatory factors. Moreover, QAI-lipo attenuates the activation of inflammatory cells (including microglia and astrocytes) and suppresses proinflammatory microglial polarization, ultimately improving the survival outcomes of infected animals. This platform offers a general strategy for central nervous system drug delivery and holds promise for broader therapeutic applications, such as the treatment of bacteremia and sepsis.

#### [A] Metabolic reprogramming of macrophage-specific transketolase orchestrates angiogenesis in diabetic wounds.

- PMID: 43039938
- Domain: Cross-domain vascular biology
- Domain 依据: Transferable mechanism appears with explicit vascular/endothelial context
- 转化相关性: Moderate

- 作者: Xiong Y, Lu L, Wu Q, Wang H, Hu H, Zhao H, et al.
- 发表日期: 2026 Oct 17
- DOI: 10.1038/s41467-026-78765-6
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/43039938/
- 日报日期: 2026-11-18

#### 摘要

Diabetic wound healing is profoundly impaired by macrophage metabolic dysfunction, which compromises angiogenesis. Here, we show a macrophage transketolase (TKT) as a crucial metabolic checkpoint governing vascular regeneration in diabetes. We demonstrate that loss of TKT in macrophages accelerates amino acid and fatty acid catabolism, elevates reactive oxygen species, and disrupts endothelial cell proliferation and differentiation. Mechanistically, hyperglycemia activates the AGEs-RAGE signaling cascade through suppressing TKT expression, establishing a pathological TKT-AGEs-RAGE axis that reprograms macrophage metabolism toward dysfunction. Then, we engineer a nanozyme-integrated hydrogel patch that selectively increases TKT level in macrophages, which rebalances macrophage metabolism, reduces oxidative stress, and rescues angiogenic capacity. In a diabetic pig model, localized delivery of the nano-system markedly accelerates wound closure and vascular repair. These findings not only elucidate a macrophage-specific TKT metabolic reprogramming mechanism for enhancing angiogenesis, but also offer a potential therapeutic strategy for the clinical management of diabetic wounds.

#### [A] Ganoderma lucidum for Alzheimer's disease: A neuroinflammatory and oxidative stress perspective.

- PMID: 42810460
- Domain: Cross-domain vascular biology
- Domain 依据: Transferable mechanism appears with explicit vascular/endothelial context
- 转化相关性: Moderate
- 转化相关性依据: Different disease with a highly transferable vascular/endothelial mechanism
- 优先级依据: A: highly transferable cross-domain vascular mechanism
- 主题: 神经免疫/炎症治疗
- 来源类型: 经典补位
- 期刊: Journal of ethnopharmacology
- 作者: Gao J, Zhang W, Yang F, Peng G, Zhang T, Dong T, et al.
- 发表日期: 2027 Feb 10
- DOI: 10.1016/j.jep.2026.122442
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42810460/
- 日报日期: 2026-11-19

#### 摘要

ETHNOPHARMACOLOGICAL RELEVANCE: Alzheimer's disease (AD) is a progressive neurodegenerative disorder that causes memory loss, cognitive decline, and ultimately dementia. Available pharmacotherapies mainly provide symptomatic relief and do not adequately address the underlying pathology. Ganoderma lucidum (Curtis) P. Karst (G. lucidum) is a widely used medicinal mushroom rich in bioactive compounds, including polysaccharides, triterpenoids, and proteins. These constituents may alleviate the neuroinflammation and oxidative stress involved in AD pathogenesis.
AIM OF THE REVIEW: This review critically integrates evidence for the therapeutic effects of G. lucidum on AD-associated neuroinflammation and oxidative stress. It has three aims: (1) to examine how G. lucidum may disrupt the bidirectional vicious cycle between neuroinflammation and oxidative stress; (2) to compare the pharmacological profiles of different preparations and purified constituents; and (3) to evaluate AD-specific barriers to translation and inform future clinical development.
MATERIALS AND METHODS: We searched PubMed, Web of Science, Google Scholar, and CNKI for studies on G. lucidum and AD through 2026 using combinations of the primary term "Ganoderma lucidum" with "Alzheimer's disease," "neuroinflammation," "oxidative stress," and related terms. Included were original research, mechanism-based studies, and clinical evidence on G. lucidum bioactives in AD or related models. Evidence was stratified by design robustness and presented as a narrative synthesis.
RESULTS: The available evidence indicates that G. lucidum may act on several pathological processes associated with AD. Experimental studies report effects on abnormal amyloid-β (Aβ) aggregation, tau hyperphosphorylation, cholinergic dysfunction, neuroinflammation, oxidative stress, and neuronal apoptosis. These findings illustrate the multi-target pharmacology of traditional Chinese medicines and help explain the potential activity of G. lucidum against AD.
CONCLUSIONS: G. lucidum has anti-inflammatory, antioxidant, and neuroprotective activities that may be relevant to AD management. This review summarizes its reported effects on AD-related neuroinflammation and oxidative stress and examines the underlying mechanisms. Clinical translation remains limited by poor blood-brain barrier (BBB) permeability, uncertain pharmacokinetics, and non-standardized dosing. Rigorous preclinical studies and controlled early-phase trials are therefore required. Addressing these barriers may inform the potential translation of G. lucidum as an adjunctive strategy in multimodal AD therapy. We also identify key evidence gaps and propose a component-specific framework for future research.

#### [A] Novel insights into the bone microenvironment in myeloma bone disease.

- PMID: 43036809
- Domain: Cross-domain vascular biology
- Domain 依据: Transferable mechanism appears with explicit vascular/endothelial context
- 转化相关性: Moderate
- 转化相关性依据: Different disease with a highly transferable vascular/endothelial mechanism
- 优先级依据: A: highly transferable cross-domain vascular mechanism
- 主题: 神经免疫/炎症治疗
- 来源类型: 经典补位
- 期刊: Journal of bone oncology
- 作者: Jundt F, Cheng WH, Kumari P, Weidner H, Fischer L, Merz M, et al.
- 发表日期: 2026 Dec
- DOI: 10.1016/j.jbo.2026.100815
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/43036809/
- 日报日期: 2026-11-19

#### 摘要

Myeloma bone disease develops in patients with a monoclonal expansion of malignant plasma cells in the bone marrow that induce osteoclast activity and suppress osteoblast function. Consequently, osteolytic bone lesions occur, causing fractures, pain, and limiting quality of life. Recent experimental, imaging, biomaterial, and translational studies indicate that multiple myeloma remodels the bone microenvironment at several interconnected levels: the extracellular matrix and matrisome, adhesion-dependent microenvironmental interactions, the immune contexture of the bone microenvironment, osteocyte lacunar architecture, bone material quality, mechanosensing, and the capacity for remineralization. Emerging evidence further suggests that endothelial adhesion molecules such as junctional adhesion molecule-A (JAM-A) and spatially organized immune-suppressive niches contribute to angiogenesis, tumor dissemination, immune escape, and persistence within osteolytic bone lesions. The emerging concept is that the myeloma bone niche is a structurally, mechanically, and molecularly altered ecosystem that both supports tumor growth and limits skeletal repair. In parallel, preclinical concepts suggest that the myeloma bone niche can be modulated by biophysical stimuli to induce skeletal repair in myeloma patients. Additionally, increasing evidence indicates that anti-myeloma therapies, including proteasome inhibitors, immunomodulatory agents, monoclonal antibodies, and B-cell maturation antigen (BCMA)-directed chimeric antigen receptor (CAR) T-cell approaches, not only control tumor burden but also modulate bone remodeling through direct effects on osteoclasts and osteoblasts as well as indirect reprogramming of osteoimmune interactions within the bone marrow microenvironment. While some of these therapies promote bone formation or reduce resorption, their overall impact on skeletal repair remains heterogeneous and incompletely understood.
