- 发表日期: 2027 Mar-Apr 01
- DOI: 10.4103/mgr.MEDGASRES-D-26-00141
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/43062001/
- 日报日期: 2026-11-21

#### 摘要

Despite the crucial role of inflammation in depression-like behavior, the low blood-brain barrier penetration of existing anti-inflammatory agents presents a substantial obstacle to effective treatment. Owing to the excellent blood-brain barrier permeability and anti-inflammatory ability of hydrogen-rich saline, this study explores its therapeutic effects on lipopolysaccharide-induced inflammation-associated depression-like behavior in mice. In this study, mice intraperitoneally received lipopolysaccharide (0.83 mg/kg). Thirty minutes later, they intraperitoneally received hydrogen-rich saline (5 mL/kg). Results showed that hydrogen-rich saline significantly alleviated anxiety- and depression-like behaviors in lipopolysaccharide-treated mice, as assessed by open field tests, elevated plus maze tests, forced swim tests, and the tail suspension test. Peripheral inflammation was significantly reduced, associated with inhibited splenomegaly and decreased serum levels of interleukin-1β and tumor necrosis factor-α. Central inflammation was also decreased, related to the reduced number of activated microglia, inhibition of Tolllike receptor 4/myeloid differentiation primary response 88/nuclear factor-κB signaling, and repaired synaptic damage in the hippocampus. These findings suggest that early hydrogen-rich saline administration may attenuate lipopolysaccharide-induced inflammation-associated depression-like behaviors, at least in part, by suppressing microglial pro-inflammatory activation and Tolllike receptor 4/nuclear factor-κB signaling.

#### [A] Astrocyte-related pathological mechanisms.

- PMID: 43062773
- Domain: Cross-domain vascular biology
- Domain 依据: Transferable mechanism appears with explicit vascular/endothelial context
- 转化相关性: Moderate
- 转化相关性依据: Different disease with a highly transferable vascular/endothelial mechanism
- 优先级依据: A: highly transferable cross-domain vascular mechanism
- 主题: 神经免疫/炎症治疗
- 来源类型: 今日新文献
- 期刊: Metabolic brain disease
- 作者: Pang Y, Han X, Zhang Y, Yu A, Zhang Z, Zhang X
- 发表日期: 2026 Nov 20
- DOI: 10.1007/s11011-026-02021-5
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/43062773/
- 日报日期: 2026-11-21

#### 摘要

Sepsis-associated encephalopathy (SAE) stands as one of the most severe complications of sepsis. Currently, the clinical management of SAE follows a comprehensive management strategy centered on treatment of the underlying sepsis, supplemented by symptomatic management of neurological dysfunction. The cornerstone of SAE management is the prompt and effective treatment of the underlying sepsis, including early source control, appropriate antimicrobial therapy, and comprehensive organ support, thereby mitigating the systemic inflammatory response that contributes to brain injury. On this basis, symptomatic and supportive management of brain dysfunction is crucial. Although no targeted specific drugs are currently available, future research will focus on targeting pathological pathways such as neuroinflammation, preserving mitochondrial function, and stabilizing the blood-brain barrier (BBB), with particular attention to how impaired astrocytic glutamate clearance, metabolic coupling, and synaptic regulation may translate glial dysfunction into neuronal bioenergetic failure, synaptic instability, delirium, and long-term cognitive impairment, with the aim of achieving genuinely neuroprotective therapy. This review summarizes the current evidence on the molecular pathology of SAE, with a particular focus on astrocyte-related mechanisms, including recent advances in the classification of astrocyte states, mitochondrial dysfunction, autophagy, and astrocyte-neuron interactions. In terms of therapeutic strategies, activating antioxidant pathways and modulation of autophagy have both shown potential therapeutic value. Future research should focus on developing multi-target synergistic intervention strategies, promoting the translation of basic research findings into clinical practice, and providing new directions for the precision prevention and control of SAE.

#### [A] Unraveling the Shaping of the Microenvironment in Brain Metastasis: From Dissemination to Overt Lesions.

- PMID: 43061288
- Domain: Cross-domain vascular biology
- Domain 依据: Transferable mechanism appears with explicit vascular/endothelial context
- 转化相关性: Moderate
- 转化相关性依据: Different disease with a highly transferable vascular/endothelial mechanism
- 优先级依据: A: highly transferable cross-domain vascular mechanism
- 主题: 神经免疫/炎症治疗
- 来源类型: 今日新文献
- 期刊: Cancer communications (London, England)
- 作者: Anwar L, Rehman AU, Khan MA, Batra SK, Nasser MW
- 发表日期: 2026
- DOI: 10.34133/cancomm.0072
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/43061288/
- 日报日期: 2026-11-21

#### 摘要

Brain metastasis (BrM) is a devastating complication of solid tumors, especially melanoma, breast, and lung cancers, and is often associated with a period of latency. The development of BrM is a highly inefficient, multistep process in which disseminated tumor cells must reach the cerebral vasculature after surviving in circulation, traverse the blood-brain barrier (BBB), and then adapt to the specialized neural microenvironment, evade immune surveillance, and instigate a reconfiguration of the microenvironment at the metastatic site to establish a clinically overt metastatic lesion. Increasing evidence has therefore shifted the field from a predominantly cancer cell-centric view toward an integrated model in which metastatic progression depends on continuous reciprocal interactions between tumor cells and the brain microenvironment. In this review, we examine BrM through different stages of the metastatic cascade, from intravasation and vascular dissemination to cerebral arrest, extravasation, dormancy, immune evasion, and metastatic outgrowth. We discuss how cancer cells acquire properties that facilitate their interaction with the cerebral vasculature and how tumor-derived factors remodel the BBB endothelial cells and other components of the metastatic niche. We further highlight the dynamic roles of astrocytes, microglia, infiltrating macrophages, natural killer cells, neutrophils, and other immune populations, which initially restrict metastatic establishment but become progressively reprogrammed to support immune evasion and survival of the metastatic cells and then also help in tumor expansion. Beyond immune and stromal remodeling, emerging studies reveal extensive metabolic and neuronal adaptation within BrM, including altered glucose, lipid, amino acid, and neurotransmitter utilization, as well as direct exploitation of neuronal signaling and synaptic communication. Together, these findings establish the brain metastatic niche as a dynamic ecosystem that is actively remodeled throughout disease progression rather than a passive site of secondary tumor growth. We also discuss advances in BrM detection and therapeutic development, including artificial intelligence-based approaches, and highlight the need for spatially and temporally resolved models that integrate tumor, vascular, immune, glial, neuronal, and metabolic programs to identify stage-specific vulnerabilities and improve BrM prevention and treatment.

#### [A] Antimalarial activity of Croton caudatus Geiseler leaf extract and its ability to reduce experimental cerebral malaria pathogenesis.

- PMID: 43057496
- Domain: Cross-domain vascular biology
- Domain 依据: Transferable mechanism appears with explicit vascular/endothelial context
- 转化相关性: Moderate
- 转化相关性依据: Different disease with a highly transferable vascular/endothelial mechanism
- 优先级依据: A: highly transferable cross-domain vascular mechanism
- 主题: 神经免疫/炎症治疗
- 来源类型: 今日新文献
- 期刊: Journal of ethnopharmacology
- 作者: Chatterjee A, Singh N, Chanu WK, Nayak P, Das R, Singh CB, et al.
- 发表日期: 2026 Nov 19
- DOI: 10.1016/j.jep.2026.122617
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/43057496/
- 日报日期: 2026-11-21

#### 摘要

ETHNOPHARMACOLOGICAL RELEVANCE: Croton caudatus Geiseler is widely used in traditional medicine to treat various ailments, including malaria. However, scientific evidences supporting its antimalarial activity and the associated molecular mechanisms, and its potential to reduce severe malaria complications are lacking.
AIM OF THE STUDY: The present study was aimed to evaluate the antimalarial activity of C. caudatus leaf extract and its fractions, and to investigate the underlying molecular mechanisms.
MATERIALS AND METHODS: Methanolic leaf extract of C. caudatus (CcLE) was fractionated into water (CcWater), butanol (CcBut), chloroform (CcChl), and hexane (CcHex) fractions. In vitro antiplasmodial activity was assessed against two different strains of Plasmodium falciparum (Pf): Pf3D7 and PfCam3.IR539T, alongside cytotoxicity and hemolysis assays. Phytochemical profiling of the most active fraction was performed by chemical fingerprinting. Mechanistic studies included β-hematin polymerization inhibition, hemozoin quantification, and reactive oxygen species (ROS) measurement. Acute oral toxicity and in vivo antimalarial efficacy were carried out in Plasmodium berghei (Pb) ANKA-infected BALB/c mice. Experimental cerebral malaria (ECM) studies were performed in Pb ANKA-infected C57BL/6 mice through the assessment of blood-brain barrier integrity, inflammatory marker expression, and immune cell infiltration analyses. Examining the gametocyte numbers and ex vivo exflagellation assays were carried out with Pb ANKA-infected mouse blood to assess the gametocytocidal activity.
RESULTS: CcHex exhibited the highest antiplasmodial activity against Pf 3D7 (IC50 = 6.84 ± 0.61 μg/mL) without detectable cytotoxic or hemolytic effects. Phytochemical analysis suggested the presence of bioactive metabolites associated with antimalarial activity. CcHex inhibited β-hematin formation in a cell-free assay and reduced hemozoin levels in Pf3D7 parasites, supporting its potential to interfere with parasite heme detoxification. It also increased intracellular ROS levels in the parasites, suggesting the disruption of heme detoxification. Further, CcHex treatment reduced gametocyte formation and impaired male gametocyte exflagellation, indicating gametocytocidal activity. Acute toxicity studies demonstrated a favourable safety profile. In Pb-infected Balb/c mice, CcHex treatment produced ∼62% parasite suppression at 1000 mg/kg/day. Importantly, CcHex treatment could protect against ECM by preserving blood-brain barrier integrity, and significantly reducing the expression of pro-inflammatory cytokines (TNF-α and IFN-γ), chemokines (CXCL9, CXCL10, and CXCR3), the adhesion molecule ICAM-1, and CD8+ T-cell infiltration in the brain.
CONCLUSIONS: The present study demonstrates that C. caudatus, a traditionally used medicinal plant, possesses significant antiplasmodial activity, exhibits protective effects against ECM, and impairs male gametocyte exflagellation. CcHex showed potent antimalarial efficacy through inhibition of parasite heme detoxification, induction of oxidative stress, and modulation of host inflammatory responses. These results support the ethnopharmacological use of C. caudatus and highlight its potential as a source of bioactive phytochemicals for the development of novel antimalarials and adjunctive therapies against severe malaria.

#### [A] LTBP4-TSP1 axis regulates angiogenesis and vascular normalization in colorectal cancer.

- PMID: 43064058
- Domain: Cross-domain vascular biology
- Domain 依据: Transferable mechanism appears with explicit vascular/endothelial context
- 转化相关性: Moderate
- 转化相关性依据: Different disease with a highly transferable vascular/endothelial mechanism
- 优先级依据: A: highly transferable cross-domain vascular mechanism
- 主题: 神经免疫/炎症治疗
- 来源类型: 今日新文献
- 期刊: Cancer letters
- 作者: Miao G, Du S, Hou Y, Wang H, Huang X, Yu J, et al.
- 发表日期: 2026 Nov 20
- DOI: 10.1016/j.canlet.2026.218994
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/43064058/
- 日报日期: 2026-11-22

#### 摘要

LTBP4 loss promotes colorectal cancer (CRC) progression, but its role in tumor angiogenesis remains undefined. Here, we report that LTBP4 deficiency drives intratumoral angiogenesis in CRC. In murine CRC models, Ltbp4-knockout tumors exhibited increased vessel density and reduced blood oxygen saturation compared with wild-type controls. Consistent with these observations, LTBP4 expression in human CRC tissues inversely correlated with vessel junctions and total vessel area. Functional assays further confirmed that conditioned medium from LTBP4-knockdown CRC cells promoted endothelial tube formation, migration, and proliferation, whereas LTBP4 overexpression suppressed these effects. Mechanistically, LTBP4 deficiency increased active TGF-β1 levels and reduced TSP1 expression through the BMP4/SMAD5 axis, both of which contributed to enhanced endothelial angiogenic responses. Therapeutically, administration of recombinant TSP1 to Ltbp4-deficient tumors promoted vascular normalization, as evidenced by increased pericyte coverage, improved perfusion, and reduced hypoxia. TSP1 treatment also enhanced the efficacy of anti-PD-1 immunotherapy, which was associated with increased CD8+ T-cell infiltration and a shift towards M1-like macrophage polarization. In clinical CRC cohorts, concurrent low expression of LTBP4 and TSP1 identified a patient subgroup with vascular abnormalities, an immunosuppressive tumor microenvironment, and poor prognosis. Collectively, our findings establish an LTBP4-BMP4-SMAD5-TSP1 regulatory axis that links angiogenesis with immune modulation in CRC, and suggest that this axis may serve as a prognostic biomarker and a potential therapeutic target for vascular normalization to improve immunotherapy response.

#### [A] The 50 Most-Cited Articles on Craniofacial Vascular Malformations: A Bibliometric and Clinical Network Analysis.

- Domain: Brain AVM / AVM
- Domain 依据: Arteriovenous malformation expression in article content

- 作者: Liu S, Wei L, Li L, Zhao J, Ling W, Xu Q, et al.
- 发表日期: 2026 Nov 16
- DOI: 10.1097/SCS.0000000000013573
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/43037565/
- 日报日期: 2026-11-17

#### 摘要

BACKGROUND: Craniofacial vascular malformations require management that accounts for lesion subtype, anatomic extent, functional risk, and esthetic impact. The authors characterized the most influential literature in this field and examined how anatomy, disease subtype, and treatment were related.
METHODS: The Web of Science Core Collection was searched in June 2026. The 50 most-cited eligible articles were evaluated for citation characteristics, journals, countries, institutions, authorship, study design, and level of evidence. VOSviewer was used for collaboration and keyword analyses. Anatomic regions, malformation subtypes, and treatments were manually classified to construct a 3-level co-occurrence network.
RESULTS: The 50 articles accumulated 3733 citations and appeared in 32 journals. The United States contributed 24 articles and 1868 citations. Retrospective studies were most common (n=25), whereas only 3 randomized controlled trials were identified. Research themes shifted from conventional lesion-directed interventions toward laser treatment, targeted therapy, and molecular investigation. The network identified 3 prominent clinical patterns: cervicofacial lymphatic malformations treated with sclerotherapy and surgery; upper facial capillary malformations and port-wine stains treated with laser-based therapy; and arteriovenous malformations treated with embolization and surgery.
CONCLUSIONS: Influential research on craniofacial vascular malformations is multidisciplinary but concentrated in specialist centers and predominantly observational. Relating anatomic site and lesion subtype to treatment provides a clinically interpretable account of the field and supports prospective, multicenter research incorporating esthetic, functional, and patient-reported outcomes.

#### 摘要

Temporomandibular joint osteoarthritis (TMJ-OA) causes chronic pain, cartilage degeneration and pathological neurovascular remodeling, yet effective local therapies remain limited. Here we develop a minimally invasive near-infrared (NIR)-responsive microneedle system (SFZICC MNs) that integrates cryptochlorogenic acid (CCA)- and carbon quantum dot-loaded ZIF-8 nanoparticles within a methacrylated silk fibroin hydrogel. The system provides mechanical integrity, photothermal responsiveness and NIR-assisted local drug release. In a mouse TMJ-OA model, SFZICC MNs combined with NIR irradiation reduce mechanical hypersensitivity, anxiety-like behaviors, cartilage degeneration and pathological neurovascularization, and outperform free CCA, blank microneedles and drug-loaded microneedles without NIR. Mechanistically, CCA directly interacts with MMP3, MMP9 and MMP13 and moderately inhibits their catalytic activities in vitro, while reducing intracellular superoxide accumulation, NF-κB p65 phosphorylation and MMP protein abundance. In vivo imaging further supports reduced overall MMP-associated proteolytic activity. These findings support SFZICC MNs as a locally responsive therapeutic platform for TMJ-OA.

#### [A] Hypoxia-activated microglia promote pathological angiogenesis via an FN/ITGB3/FAK/Akt pathway.

- PMID: 42959614
- Domain: Cross-domain vascular biology
- Domain 依据: Transferable mechanism appears with explicit vascular/endothelial context
- 转化相关性: Moderate
- 转化相关性依据: Different disease with a highly transferable vascular/endothelial mechanism
- 优先级依据: A: highly transferable cross-domain vascular mechanism
- 主题: 神经免疫/炎症治疗
- 来源类型: 今日新文献
- 期刊: International journal of ophthalmology
- 作者: Hao LC, Li DH, Zhang K, Liu CZ, Sun MH, Gao XX, et al.
- 发表日期: 2026
- DOI: 10.18240/ijo.2026.11.02
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42959614/
- 日报日期: 2026-11-18

#### 摘要

AIM: To investigate whether hypoxia-activated microglia drive pathological neovascularization through extracellular matrix (ECM) remodeling and endothelial integrin signaling, a mechanism that may function in parallel with vascular endothelial growth factor (VEGF) signaling.
METHODS: The oxygen-induced retinopathy (OIR) mouse model was used. Retinal phenotypes were assessed by flat mounts, histology, and vascular leakage assays. Key pathways were screened by transcriptomics and validated via Western blot, immunofluorescence, and quantitative polymerase chain reaction (qPCR). In vitro, brain microvascular endothelial cells were treated with conditioned medium from hypoxic microglia (Hyp-CM), with cell migration and tube formation assessed. Interventions employed the integrin β3 (ITGB3) inhibitor cilengitide, the focal adhesion kinase (FAK) inhibitor PF-573228 and a neutralizing anti-VEGF antibody. In vivo, cilengitide was administered by intravitreal injection to evaluate its therapeutic effect on neovascularization.
RESULTS: OIR retinas showed significant avascularity, pathological neovascularization, and barrier disruption. Transcriptomics revealed activation of ECM-receptor interaction and focal adhesion pathways, with upregulation of Fn1 and Itgb3. Increased protein levels of fibronectin (FN) and ITGB3, alongside elevated phosphorylation of FAK and protein kinase B (Akt), were confirmed in vivo. Activated microglia co-localized with upregulated FN and ITGB3. Conditioned medium from Hyp-CM activated the ITGB3-FAK-Akt pathway in endothelial cells and promoted their migration and tube formation. These effects were nearly abolished by cilengitide or PF-573228. VEGF neutralization partially reduced Hyp-CM-induced migration and tube formation. Intravitreal injection of cilengitide significantly reduced retinal neovascularization in OIR mice.
CONCLUSION: Hypoxia-activated microglia promote pathological angiogenesis, and the FN/ITGB3/FAK/Akt axis is identified as a key mediator of this process, revealing a novel mechanism that operates in parallel with VEGF signaling. Targeting this "hypoxia-microglia-ECM-integrin" axis represents a promising therapeutic strategy to overcome anti-VEGF resistance in ischemic retinopathy.

#### [A] A PI3K-RAC1 signaling axis downstream of mutant TIE2 promotes endothelial dysfunction in venous malformation.

- PMID: 43059804
- Domain: Venous malformation
- Domain 依据: TEK/TIE2 appears with vascular-malformation context
- 转化相关性: Strong
- 转化相关性依据: Related vascular malformation with a transferable mechanism or targeted therapy
- 优先级依据: A: related vascular malformation + transferable mechanism/targeted therapy
- 主题: 静脉畸形基础与治疗
- 来源类型: 近期补位
- 期刊: bioRxiv : the preprint server for biology
- 作者: Sagar K, Schrenk S, Sherpa C, Crockett L, Soroko K, Cai Y, et al.
- 发表日期: 2026 Nov 13
- DOI: 10.64898/2026.11.12.766208
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/43059804/
- 日报日期: 2026-11-21

#### 摘要

BACKGROUND: Activating mutations in the endothelial receptor tyrosine kinase TIE2 are a cause of venous malformations (VMs), primarily through dysregulated PI3K/AKT signaling. However, the downstream mechanisms linking constitutively active mutant TIE2 to enlarged vascular lumens, endothelial dysfunction and VM remain incompletely understood. Here, we investigated the role of RAC1 signaling in mutant TIE2 p.L914F driven VM.
METHODS: Human umbilical vein endothelial cells (HUVECs) expressing wild-type TIE2 or constitutively active mutant TIE2-L914F were analyzed using RAC1 GTP pull-down assays, immunoblotting, and endothelial cell (EC) 2D and 3D functional assays. The role of RAC1 was evaluated using the RAC1 inhibitor NSC23766 in vitro and in vivo xenograft model of VM.
RESULTS & CONCLUSIONS: ECs expressing mutant TIE2-L914F showed increased active GTP-bound RAC1. TIE2-L914F ECs exhibited cytoskeletal changes with enhanced lamellipodia formation and maturation, accompanied by increased migration. Pharmacological inhibition of RAC1 significantly attenuated the aberrant vascular lumen area and migration in the mutant TIE2 ECs. Mechanistically, we showed that RAC1 hyperactivation is PI3K-dependent but not MEK/ERK-dependent. Consistent with these findings, RAC1 inhibition significantly reduced vascular lesion formation in a xenograft model of VM with TIE2-L914F ECs.Our study identifies RAC1 as a downstream mediator of TIE2-L914F driven endothelial dysfunction. These results establish a previously unrecognized PI3K-RAC1 signaling axis in the pathogenesis of TIE2-mutated VMs.

#### [A] Age-Stratified Sirolimus Trough Concentrations in Pediatric Vascular Malformations: A Real-World Retrospective Study.

- PMID: 43062181
- Domain: Other vascular malformation
- Domain 依据: Other vascular malformation/anomaly expression in article content
- 转化相关性: Strong
- 转化相关性依据: Related vascular malformation with a transferable mechanism or targeted therapy
- 优先级依据: A: related vascular malformation + transferable mechanism/targeted therapy
- 主题: 血管畸形PI3K-AKT-mTOR通路
- 来源类型: 今日新文献
- 期刊: Therapeutic drug monitoring
- 作者: Jung SM, Ha S, Kwon YJ, Kwon H, Namgoong JM, Kim DY
- 发表日期: 2026 Nov 20
- DOI: 10.1097/FTD.0000000000001554
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/43062181/
- 日报日期: 2026-11-21

#### 摘要

BACKGROUND: Sirolimus is increasingly being used off-label to treat pediatric vascular malformations. In this study, the age-stratified drug exposure, pharmacokinetic variability, and long-term treatment characteristics of sirolimus were evaluated in a real-world pediatric population.
METHODS: In this study, 144 patients (younger than 19 years) with vascular malformations who were treated with sirolimus at a single center (July 2018 and March 2025) were retrospectively analyzed. A total of 2054 therapeutic drug monitoring measurements were obtained. The patients were stratified into 6 age groups (1 month and younger to 12-18 years). The outcomes included trough concentration, interindividual variability (coefficient of variation), target range attainment, treatment duration, and MRI-based response.
RESULTS: The median follow-up was 18.3 months (range, 0.3-74.1 months). Neonates (1 month and younger) showed the highest interindividual variability (coefficient of variation = 61.6%), with lower variability observed in adolescents (40.0%, P < 0.001). Overall, 73.4% of the trough concentrations were within the target range (4-10 ng/mL), and the mean duration within the therapeutic range per patient was 70.9%. Supratherapeutic levels (>15 ng/mL) were most frequent in neonates (6.6%). Prolonged therapy (≥24 months) was observed in 31.9% of patients, with a median duration of 35.1 months. Among the 44 patients who underwent follow-up MRI, 84.1% showed disease improvement or stability.
CONCLUSIONS: Sustained long-term treatment continuity suggests that sirolimus was generally tolerated across pediatric age groups, although direct toxicity data were not systematically captured in this study. Younger patients exhibited greater pharmacokinetic variability, necessitating closer monitoring and cautious dose adjustment. Prospective PK-PD studies incorporating standardized clinical outcomes are needed to define optimal trough targets and individualized dosing strategies.

#### 摘要

BACKGROUND: Craniofacial vascular malformations require management that accounts for lesion subtype, anatomic extent, functional risk, and esthetic impact. The authors characterized the most influential literature in this field and examined how anatomy, disease subtype, and treatment were related.
METHODS: The Web of Science Core Collection was searched in June 2026. The 50 most-cited eligible articles were evaluated for citation characteristics, journals, countries, institutions, authorship, study design, and level of evidence. VOSviewer was used for collaboration and keyword analyses. Anatomic regions, malformation subtypes, and treatments were manually classified to construct a 3-level co-occurrence network.
RESULTS: The 50 articles accumulated 3733 citations and appeared in 32 journals. The United States contributed 24 articles and 1868 citations. Retrospective studies were most common (n=25), whereas only 3 randomized controlled trials were identified. Research themes shifted from conventional lesion-directed interventions toward laser treatment, targeted therapy, and molecular investigation. The network identified 3 prominent clinical patterns: cervicofacial lymphatic malformations treated with sclerotherapy and surgery; upper facial capillary malformations and port-wine stains treated with laser-based therapy; and arteriovenous malformations treated with embolization and surgery.
CONCLUSIONS: Influential research on craniofacial vascular malformations is multidisciplinary but concentrated in specialist centers and predominantly observational. Relating anatomic site and lesion subtype to treatment provides a clinically interpretable account of the field and supports prospective, multicenter research incorporating esthetic, functional, and patient-reported outcomes.

#### [A] Translating the pathophysiology of sepsis-associated acute kidney injury: From bench to bedside.

- PMID: 43017485
- Domain: Cross-domain vascular biology
- Domain 依据: Transferable mechanism appears with explicit vascular/endothelial context
- 转化相关性: Moderate
- 转化相关性依据: Different disease with a highly transferable vascular/endothelial mechanism
- 优先级依据: A: highly transferable cross-domain vascular mechanism
- 主题: 神经免疫/炎症治疗
- 来源类型: 经典补位
- 期刊: Journal of intensive medicine
- 作者: Lu M, Zhang Q, Pang M, Wang H, Zhang M, Li Y, et al.
- 发表日期: 2026 Dec
- DOI: 10.1016/j.jointm.2026.08.005
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/43017485/
- 日报日期: 2026-11-19

#### 摘要

Sepsis-associated acute kidney injury (SA-AKI) is a major complication in the intensive care unit (ICU), associated with increased mortality and progression to chronic kidney disease (CKD) and end-stage renal disease (ESRD). This review summarizes current advances in the definition, epidemiology, pathophysiology, diagnosis, experimental modeling, and therapeutic strategies of SA-AKI, with emphasis on translational challenges. The conceptual framework of SA-AKI has evolved from global renal hypoperfusion to a multifactorial, predominantly non-ischemic process characterized by inflammatory dysregulation, microcirculatory dysfunction, and adaptive metabolic reprogramming. Tubular metabolic shutdown is increasingly recognized as a protective yet maladaptive response contributing to functional decline. Regulated cell death pathways, including pyroptosis and ferroptosis, further drive tubular injury. In parallel, organ crosstalk - particularly along the lung-gut-heart-kidney axis - amplifies renal dysfunction during sepsis. Recent diagnostic advances are highlighted, including the 2023 Acute Disease Quality Initiative (ADQI) consensus and emerging biomarkers reflecting distinct pathophysiological domains: tubular injury (neutrophil gelatinase-associated lipocalin [NGAL] and kidney injury molecule-1 [KIM-1]), cell cycle arrest (Tissue Inhibitor of Metalloproteinases-2[TIMP-2]·insulin-like growth factor-binding protein 7 [IGFBP7]), and endothelial dysfunction (angiopoietin-2 [Angpt-2] and the tyrosine kinase with immunoglobulin-like and EGF-like domains 2 receptor [Tie2]), supporting early risk stratification beyond serum creatinine (sCr). Experimental models ranging from endotoxemia and cecal ligation and puncture to two-hit and comorbidity-based paradigms are critically appraised. Finally, current and investigational therapies - including hemodynamic optimization, extracorporeal cytokine removal, mitochondria-targeted interventions, and regenerative approaches - are discussed in the context of persistent barriers to clinical translation.

### glycolysis / mitochondria / metabolic reprogramming（5）

#### [A] Metabolic reprogramming of macrophage-specific transketolase orchestrates angiogenesis in diabetic wounds.

- PMID: 43039938
- Domain: Cross-domain vascular biology
- Domain 依据: Transferable mechanism appears with explicit vascular/endothelial context
- 转化相关性: Moderate
- 转化相关性依据: Different disease with a highly transferable vascular/endothelial mechanism
- 优先级依据: A: highly transferable cross-domain vascular mechanism
- 主题: 跨领域顶刊启发
- 来源类型: 顶刊扩展
- 来源说明: 神经血管/脑血管顶刊扩展
- 期刊: Nature communications
- 作者: Xiong Y, Lu L, Wu Q, Wang H, Hu H, Zhao H, et al.
- 发表日期: 2026 Oct 17
- DOI: 10.1038/s41467-026-78765-6
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/43039938/
- 日报日期: 2026-11-18

#### 摘要

Diabetic wound healing is profoundly impaired by macrophage metabolic dysfunction, which compromises angiogenesis. Here, we show a macrophage transketolase (TKT) as a crucial metabolic checkpoint governing vascular regeneration in diabetes. We demonstrate that loss of TKT in macrophages accelerates amino acid and fatty acid catabolism, elevates reactive oxygen species, and disrupts endothelial cell proliferation and differentiation. Mechanistically, hyperglycemia activates the AGEs-RAGE signaling cascade through suppressing TKT expression, establishing a pathological TKT-AGEs-RAGE axis that reprograms macrophage metabolism toward dysfunction. Then, we engineer a nanozyme-integrated hydrogel patch that selectively increases TKT level in macrophages, which rebalances macrophage metabolism, reduces oxidative stress, and rescues angiogenic capacity. In a diabetic pig model, localized delivery of the nano-system markedly accelerates wound closure and vascular repair. These findings not only elucidate a macrophage-specific TKT metabolic reprogramming mechanism for enhancing angiogenesis, but also offer a potential therapeutic strategy for the clinical management of diabetic wounds.

- 作者: Yao X, Liu D, Zhao Z, Gong S, Fu H, Wang Q, et al.
- 发表日期: 2026 Nov 19
- DOI: 10.1007/s12035-026-06333-6
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/43056036/
- 日报日期: 2026-11-20

#### 摘要

Ischemic stroke triggers mitochondrial dysfunction and the subsequent release of mitochondrial DNA (mtDNA) into the cytosol, where it activates the cyclic GMP-AMP synthase (cGAS)-stimulator of interferon genes (STING) innate immune pathway and drives a complex neuroinflammatory response. This review synthesizes recent advances to examine how mitochondrial stress leads to mtDNA leakage across different brain cell types, how cGAS-STING signaling is activated in a cell-type-specific manner, and how this pathway interacts with immune cell metabolism to shape neurological outcomes. We propose that the net effect of cGAS-STING activation is not intrinsically protective or detrimental, but rather depends on the intensity and duration of the activating signal and on the metabolic state of the responding cell. A recurring theme from our analysis is that the consequences of pathway activation vary markedly among microglia, neurons, and astrocytes, and that this variation shifts over time as the inflammatory response evolves from the hyperacute to the chronic phase. On this basis, we evaluate three categories of therapeutic strategies, including preservation of mitochondrial integrity, direct inhibition of cGAS-STING components, and reprogramming of immune metabolism. We also discuss the barriers to clinical translation, such as limited blood-brain barrier penetration, the lack of cell-type-specific targeting, and the absence of validated biomarkers for patient selection. We conclude that effective therapy will require stage-specific and cell-selective strategies rather than uniform inhibition of the pathway, and that immunometabolic reprogramming may offer a more durable approach for promoting long-term recovery.

#### [A] Myeloid immune dynamics and potential reprogramming in high-altitude disorders: temporal trajectories, endothelial crosstalk, and translational implications.

- PMID: 43055909
- Domain: Cross-domain vascular biology
- Domain 依据: Transferable mechanism appears with explicit vascular/endothelial context
- 转化相关性: Moderate
- 转化相关性依据: Different disease with a highly transferable vascular/endothelial mechanism
- 优先级依据: A: highly transferable cross-domain vascular mechanism
- 主题: 神经免疫/炎症治疗
- 来源类型: 今日新文献
- 期刊: Immunologic research
- 作者: Pang L, Yang L
- 发表日期: 2026 Nov 19
- DOI: 10.1007/s12026-026-09883-y
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/43055909/
- 日报日期: 2026-11-20

#### 摘要

High-altitude hypobaric hypoxia rapidly alters circulating myeloid-cell distribution, inflammatory signaling, metabolism, and vascular interactions, but these changes are often interpreted without sufficient attention to exposure phase, tissue compartment, or persistence after recovery. This narrative review addresses a central conceptual problem: transient leukocyte mobilization and cytokine elevation are not equivalent to durable myeloid reprogramming. Evidence from human field studies, patient cohorts, hypobaric-hypoxia models, mechanistic normobaric-hypoxia experiments, and related vascular-disease models is organized within a three-level framework comprising transient exposure responses, functional adjustment during ongoing hypoxia, and persistent cell-intrinsic reprogramming. We map these states from the first hours after ascent through acclimatization, prolonged exposure, decompensation, and return to low altitude, emphasizing hypoxia-inducible-factor-dependent immunometabolism, mitochondrial stress signaling, inflammasomes, chromatin regulation, and myeloid-endothelial crosstalk. Disease-specific appraisal suggests that high-altitude pulmonary edema (HAPE) involves complex interactions among elevated pulmonary vascular and capillary pressures, alveolar-capillary barrier dysfunction, and inflammatory responses, with inflammation potentially contributing to disease susceptibility and progression; experimental evidence implicates microglial-neurovascular interactions in high-altitude cerebral edema (HACE), but direct human causal evidence is limited; and high-altitude pulmonary hypertension (HAPH) currently has the most extensive combined human observational and experimental evidence for monocyte-macrophage involvement. Candidate biomarkers and therapeutic targets remain exploratory and are discussed in relation to sampling phase, clinical context, evidence strength, and safety constraints; prospective disease-specific validation is required. Candidate biomarkers and therapeutic targets are aligned with sampling phase, disease stage, evidence strength, and safety constraints. The resulting time-resolved, evidence-calibrated model separates reversible acclimatization from pathological decompensation and true persistence after stimulus withdrawal. Longitudinal within-person recovery studies combining sorted-cell functional assays, metabolic flux, chromatin profiling, and spatial localization are required to define the prevalence, cellular breadth, and clinical consequences of persistent high-altitude myeloid reprogramming and inform future trials of disease-specific adjunctive therapy.

#### [A] Astrocyte-related pathological mechanisms.

- PMID: 43062773
- Domain: Cross-domain vascular biology
- Domain 依据: Transferable mechanism appears with explicit vascular/endothelial context
- 转化相关性: Moderate
- 转化相关性依据: Different disease with a highly transferable vascular/endothelial mechanism
- 优先级依据: A: highly transferable cross-domain vascular mechanism
- 主题: 神经免疫/炎症治疗
- 来源类型: 今日新文献
- 期刊: Metabolic brain disease
- 作者: Pang Y, Han X, Zhang Y, Yu A, Zhang Z, Zhang X
- 发表日期: 2026 Nov 20
- DOI: 10.1007/s11011-026-02021-5
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/43062773/
- 日报日期: 2026-11-21

#### 摘要

Sepsis-associated encephalopathy (SAE) stands as one of the most severe complications of sepsis. Currently, the clinical management of SAE follows a comprehensive management strategy centered on treatment of the underlying sepsis, supplemented by symptomatic management of neurological dysfunction. The cornerstone of SAE management is the prompt and effective treatment of the underlying sepsis, including early source control, appropriate antimicrobial therapy, and comprehensive organ support, thereby mitigating the systemic inflammatory response that contributes to brain injury. On this basis, symptomatic and supportive management of brain dysfunction is crucial. Although no targeted specific drugs are currently available, future research will focus on targeting pathological pathways such as neuroinflammation, preserving mitochondrial function, and stabilizing the blood-brain barrier (BBB), with particular attention to how impaired astrocytic glutamate clearance, metabolic coupling, and synaptic regulation may translate glial dysfunction into neuronal bioenergetic failure, synaptic instability, delirium, and long-term cognitive impairment, with the aim of achieving genuinely neuroprotective therapy. This review summarizes the current evidence on the molecular pathology of SAE, with a particular focus on astrocyte-related mechanisms, including recent advances in the classification of astrocyte states, mitochondrial dysfunction, autophagy, and astrocyte-neuron interactions. In terms of therapeutic strategies, activating antioxidant pathways and modulation of autophagy have both shown potential therapeutic value. Future research should focus on developing multi-target synergistic intervention strategies, promoting the translation of basic research findings into clinical practice, and providing new directions for the precision prevention and control of SAE.

### iron / hemosiderin / ferroptosis（1）

#### [A] Translating the pathophysiology of sepsis-associated acute kidney injury: From bench to bedside.

- PMID: 43017485
- Domain: Cross-domain vascular biology
- Domain 依据: Transferable mechanism appears with explicit vascular/endothelial context
- 转化相关性: Moderate
- 转化相关性依据: Different disease with a highly transferable vascular/endothelial mechanism
- 优先级依据: A: highly transferable cross-domain vascular mechanism
- 主题: 神经免疫/炎症治疗
- 来源类型: 经典补位
- 期刊: Journal of intensive medicine
- 作者: Lu M, Zhang Q, Pang M, Wang H, Zhang M, Li Y, et al.
- 发表日期: 2026 Dec
- DOI: 10.1016/j.jointm.2026.08.005
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/43017485/
- 日报日期: 2026-11-19

#### 摘要

Sepsis-associated acute kidney injury (SA-AKI) is a major complication in the intensive care unit (ICU), associated with increased mortality and progression to chronic kidney disease (CKD) and end-stage renal disease (ESRD). This review summarizes current advances in the definition, epidemiology, pathophysiology, diagnosis, experimental modeling, and therapeutic strategies of SA-AKI, with emphasis on translational challenges. The conceptual framework of SA-AKI has evolved from global renal hypoperfusion to a multifactorial, predominantly non-ischemic process characterized by inflammatory dysregulation, microcirculatory dysfunction, and adaptive metabolic reprogramming. Tubular metabolic shutdown is increasingly recognized as a protective yet maladaptive response contributing to functional decline. Regulated cell death pathways, including pyroptosis and ferroptosis, further drive tubular injury. In parallel, organ crosstalk - particularly along the lung-gut-heart-kidney axis - amplifies renal dysfunction during sepsis. Recent diagnostic advances are highlighted, including the 2023 Acute Disease Quality Initiative (ADQI) consensus and emerging biomarkers reflecting distinct pathophysiological domains: tubular injury (neutrophil gelatinase-associated lipocalin [NGAL] and kidney injury molecule-1 [KIM-1]), cell cycle arrest (Tissue Inhibitor of Metalloproteinases-2[TIMP-2]·insulin-like growth factor-binding protein 7 [IGFBP7]), and endothelial dysfunction (angiopoietin-2 [Angpt-2] and the tyrosine kinase with immunoglobulin-like and EGF-like domains 2 receptor [Tie2]), supporting early risk stratification beyond serum creatinine (sCr). Experimental models ranging from endotoxemia and cecal ligation and puncture to two-hit and comorbidity-based paradigms are critically appraised. Finally, current and investigational therapies - including hemodynamic optimization, extracorporeal cytokine removal, mitochondria-targeted interventions, and regenerative approaches - are discussed in the context of persistent barriers to clinical translation.

### [A] Rapidly Progressive Interstitial Lung Disease with Coexisting Anti-MDA5 and Anti-OJ Antibodies Successfully Treated with Intensive Immunosuppression and Plasma Exchange.

- PMID: 41922244

- 发表日期: 2026 Nov 15
- DOI: 10.2169/internalmedicine.7090-26
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41922244/
- 日报日期: 2026-11-17

#### 摘要

Anti-melanoma differentiation-associated gene 5 (MDA5) antibody-positive dermatomyositis is frequently complicated by rapidly progressive interstitial lung disease, whereas anti-aminoacyl-tRNA synthetase (ARS) antibody-positive interstitial lung disease typically follows a subacute or chronic course and responds to immunosuppressive therapy. The coexistence of anti-MDA5 and anti-ARS antibodies, particularly anti-OJ antibodies, is very rare. We report the case of a 58-year-old man who was double positive for anti-MDA5 and anti-OJ antibodies and developed rapidly progressive interstitial lung disease. The disease continued to progress with triple immunosuppressive therapy; however, the introduction of plasma exchange led to marked clinical and radiological improvements. This case highlights the importance of recognizing the coexistence of antibodies and integrating serological, radiological, and clinical findings to guide timely and appropriate treatment.

### [A] First-in-human anti-Trop2 CAR-T cell therapy in refractory INI-1/BRM-deficient sinonasal carcinoma: A case report.

- PMID: 43034827

- 发表日期: 2026 Dec 17
- DOI: 10.1016/j.omton.2026.201366
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/43034827/
- 日报日期: 2026-11-17

#### 摘要

INI-1-deficient sinonasal carcinoma is an aggressive malignancy with a dismal prognosis and no established salvage therapies. While trophoblast cell-surface antigen 2 (Trop2) is a promising immunotherapeutic target, its clinical utility in sinonasal cancer remains unexplored. We report the first-in-human application of anti-Trop2 CAR-T cell therapy in a 61-year-old male with refractory INI-1/BRM-deficient sinonasal carcinoma. Following lymphodepletion, the CAR-T cells demonstrated robust in vivo expansion, peaking within 14 days and coinciding with transient cytokine elevation. Toxicities were manageable but included grade 2 cytokine release syndrome and grade 3 hepatotoxicity that required multiple sessions of plasma exchange. Notably, MRI at day 78 post-infusion revealed dramatic tumor regression with extensive necrosis and liquefaction, which correlated with the complete resolution of intractable orbital pain. This single-case observation provides preliminary proof-of-concept for the safety and anti-tumor activity of Trop2-targeted CAR-T cells in refractory sinonasal malignancies, though further studies are needed to confirm durability and generalizability.

### [A] Clinical and demographical profile of pediatric MOGAD patients: Portuguese national data.

- PMID: 43038642
- Domain: Out-of-scope

- 作者: Krupka D, Silva C, Monteiro I, Painho T, Cadete V, Matos CM, et al.
- 发表日期: 2026 Nov 14
- DOI: 10.1016/j.ejpn.2026.11.003
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/43038642/
- 日报日期: 2026-11-17

#### 摘要

INTRODUCTION: Myelin oligodendrocyte glycoprotein antibody-associated disease (MOGAD) is an autoimmune demyelinating disease affecting both adults and children. Despite of its clinical heterogeneity, the prognosis is often favorable. This study aimed to describe demographic and clinical characteristics of pediatric MOGAD patients in Portugal and to identify potential risk factors for relapsing disease.
METHODS: We conducted a retrospective and multicenter study including pediatric patients (age below 18 years-old) with MOGAD diagnosed between 2011 and 2025. Data were collected from clinical records from participant centers.
RESULTS: Seventy-seven patients met the inclusion criteria; 49.7% were male and the mean age was 7.8 years-old. A preceding infection was reported in 45.5% of cases. ADEM was the most frequent phenotype (61%), being more frequent in toddlers (p = 0.005). The EDSS at onset was of 4.0 (0-9). Anti-MOG antibodies were positive at diagnosis in 97.4% of patients with seroreversion occurring in 49.5%, which was not correlated with relapsing disease (p = 0.5). Most of the patients received intravenous methylprednisolone (97.4%); nine patients (11.7%) with more severe disease had add-on treatment with plasmapheresis (p = 0.003). Complete recovery was observed in 81.6% of patients at last follow-up visit, while 26% had a relapsing disease. A univariate analysis showed positive correlations between EDSS at presentation and at follow-up (p = 0.009) and time of follow-up and relapsing disease (p = 0.001).
CONCLUSIONS: Portuguese national data confirm previously described features of MOGAD. Therapeutic approaches appear consistent throughout centers and guided by disease severity ab initio even though standardized treatment guidelines are missing.

### [A] Complete remission achieved by multidisciplinary immunosuppressive therapy including plasma exchange in Kimura disease-associated membranoproliferative glomerulonephritis pattern of injury: a case report.

- PMID: 43033311
- Domain: Out-of-scope
- Domain 依据: No strong CCM/vascular-malformation or transferable vascular mechanism evidence
- 转化相关性: Exploratory
- 转化相关性依据: Independent plasma exchange/blood exchange research interest; not disease-domain relevance

- 主题: 换血/血浆置换疗法
- 来源类型: 近期补位
- 独立科研兴趣: Plasma exchange / blood exchange
- 期刊: CEN case reports
- 作者: Kinguchi S, Oizumi T, Kumagai E, Koike J, Eto H, Komiya S, et al.
- 发表日期: 2026 Nov 14
- DOI: 10.1007/s13730-026-01208-0
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/43033311/
- 日报日期: 2026-11-17

#### 摘要

Kimura disease (KD) is a rare chronic inflammatory disorder characterized by cervical lymphadenopathy or subcutaneous masses in the head and neck region, accompanied by peripheral eosinophilia and elevated serum immunoglobulin E (IgE) levels. Nephrotic syndrome is a well-recognized complication of KD. Herein, we report a case of refractory nephrotic syndrome due to KD-associated membranoproliferative glomerulonephritis (MPGN) pattern of injury, in whom complete remission was achieved following multidisciplinary immunosuppressive therapy including plasma exchange (PE). A 33-year-old man with a history of KD presented to our hospital with nephrotic syndrome accompanied by a painless left cervical mass and elevated serum IgE levels. Kidney biopsy revealed MPGN pattern of injury. Nephrotic-range proteinuria and elevated serum IgE levels persisted, despite initiation of prednisolone (60 mg/day) and cyclosporine (150 mg/day) therapy. Six sessions of PE were subsequently administered, during which both proteinuria and serum IgE levels decreased. Because proteinuria tended to increase again after completion of PE, mycophenolate mofetil (1000 mg/day) was added. Prednisolone was successfully tapered to a maintenance dose of 7.5 mg/day, and complete remission has been maintained for 6 months. Th2-predominant immune responses and increased cytokine production have been implicated in the pathogenesis of KD, and elevated serum IgE levels have been associated with an elevated risk of disease recurrence. PE was used to rapidly remove circulating immune mediators potentially involved in KD. Our case suggests that PE may serve as a bridging intervention within multidisciplinary immunosuppressive therapy for refractory nephrotic syndrome due to KD-associated MPGN pattern of injury, although its independent therapeutic contribution remains uncertain.

#### 摘要

Systemic lupus erythematosus (SLE) and neuromyelitis optica spectrum disorder (NMOSD) are autoimmune diseases that have been reported to coexist. We describe the case of a 61-year-old woman with long-standing SLE and Sjögren's disease who developed recurrent longitudinally extensive transverse myelitis associated with concomitant NMOSD. Despite treatment with glucocorticoids, plasma exchange, and rituximab, she experienced multiple neurological relapses, with persistent positivity of anti-aquaporin-4 antibodies and magnetic resonance imaging findings consistent with NMOSD, while SLE remained clinically inactive. Therefore, we initiated treatment with an anti-C5 monoclonal antibody for relapse prevention. To our knowledge, this is the first reported case of a patient with coexisting SLE and NMOSD treated with an anti-C5 monoclonal antibody, remaining relapse-free throughout a 22-month follow-up period.

### [A] Delayed Severe Liver Injury After Combined Acetaminophen and Iron Ingestion: Importance of Serial Reassessment in Mixed Overdose.

- DOI: 10.1002/ams2.70181
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/43032881/
- 日报日期: 2026-11-17

#### 摘要

BACKGROUND: Assessment of combined acetaminophen and iron ingestion may be complicated when clinical manifestations, toxin concentrations, and evolving organ injury do not progress in parallel.
CASE PRESENTATION: A 12-year-old girl ingested at least 6 g of APAP (≥ 146 mg/kg) and 1 g of elemental iron (24 mg/kg). Two hours after ingestion, serum iron was 503 μg/dL while liver biochemical tests were normal. Her gastrointestinal symptoms transiently improved, but she later developed recurrent symptoms followed by severe acute liver injury and coagulopathy. At 42 h, serum iron had decreased to 219 μg/dL and APAP was < 5 μg/mL, whereas AST and ALT exceeded 5000 U/L, and PT-INR was 4.68. She received N-acetylcysteine, plasma exchange, and continuous renal replacement therapy and recovered without liver transplantation.
CONCLUSION: Serial clinical and biochemical reassessment is essential in mixed overdose when symptoms, toxin concentrations, and evolving organ injury are discordant.

### [A] Clinical and Laboratory Factors Associated with Exchange Transfusion in Neonates with Severe Hyperbilirubinaemia: A Retrospective Cohort Study.

- PMID: 43030903
- Domain: Out-of-scope
- Domain 依据: No strong CCM/vascular-malformation or transferable vascular mechanism evidence
- 转化相关性: Exploratory

- 主题: 换血/血浆置换疗法

- 期刊: Journal of clinical medicine
- 作者: İzol A, Yazıcı S, Ercan S
- 发表日期: 2026 Nov 09
- DOI: 10.3390/jcm15218762
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/43030903/
- 日报日期: 2026-11-17

#### 摘要

Background and Objectives: Exchange transfusion (ET) remains a critical intervention for severe neonatal hyperbilirubinaemia, yet laboratory associations with ET receipt require careful interpretation. We examined characteristics associated with ET and post-procedure laboratory changes. Materials and Methods: This retrospective tertiary-centre cohort included 98 neonates evaluated between January 2020 and December 2025 (65 received ET; 33 did not). A four-covariate logistic regression model assessed associations with ET receipt. Discrimination, bootstrap optimism, cross-validated calibration and an exploratory creatinine-containing model were examined. Results: Peak total serum bilirubin (TSB) was higher among ET recipients than non-recipients (33.69 ± 6.31 versus 26.19 ± 3.64 mg/dL; p < 0.001). In the primary model (n = 98), peak TSB was associated with ET receipt (adjusted odds ratio 1.31 per mg/dL, 95% confidence interval 1.16-1.48; p < 0.001). The apparent area under the receiver operating characteristic curve (AUC) was 0.860, decreasing to 0.837 after bootstrap optimism correction. An exploratory six-covariate model (n = 97) yielded an apparent AUC of 0.874 and an optimism-corrected AUC of 0.836. Creatinine showed an adjusted association (odds ratio 1.58 per 0.1 mg/dL, 95% confidence interval 1.07-2.32), but its incremental AUC was uncertain (difference 0.014, 95% confidence interval -0.022 to 0.053). Following ET, bilirubin, platelet count and total calcium decreased. Conclusions: Peak TSB strongly distinguished infants who received ET, but this association partly reflects the bilirubin-based indication for treatment. Creatinine did not demonstrate a reliable incremental discrimination benefit. These findings reinforce guideline-based ET assessment and post-exchange monitoring, not the adoption of new treatment thresholds or prediction tools.

### [A] Persistent cryoglobulinaemia after HCV cure: implications for kidney transplantation.

- PMID: 43032321
- Domain: Out-of-scope
- Domain 依据: No strong CCM/vascular-malformation or transferable vascular mechanism evidence
- 转化相关性: Exploratory
- 转化相关性依据: Independent plasma exchange/blood exchange research interest; not disease-domain relevance
- 优先级依据: A: plasma exchange/plasmapheresis/blood exchange/immunoadsorption
- 主题: 换血/血浆置换疗法
- 来源类型: 近期补位
- 独立科研兴趣: Plasma exchange / blood exchange
- 期刊: Clinical kidney journal
- 作者: Nguyen HT, Nguyen TT, Nguyen TTM, Le TTL, Tran TT, Nghiem DT
- 发表日期: 2026 Nov
- DOI: 10.1093/ckj/sfag388
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/43032321/
- 日报日期: 2026-11-17

#### 摘要

Direct-acting antivirals cure most patients with chronic hepatitis C virus (HCV) infection, but recovery from HCV-associated mixed cryoglobulinaemia is less uniform: clinical and immunologic responses may be incomplete, delayed, or followed by relapse. Cryoglobulins, rheumatoid factor, hypocomplementaemia, and residual B-cell clones frequently persist after sustained virologic response (SVR), and it is often unclear whether these findings reflect ongoing cryoglobulinaemic disease, slowly resolving immunologic abnormality or, where organ dysfunction persists, irreversible chronic damage. The distinction is consequential in kidney transplant candidates, in whom persistent abnormalities may raise concern about transplant timing, recurrent cryoglobulinaemic glomerulonephritis and the use of pretransplant rituximab or plasma exchange, yet transplant-specific evidence is minimal. This narrative review examines evidence relevant to individualized pretransplant assessment, prompted by a patient with advanced chronic kidney disease, persistent type II cryoglobulinaemia, and predominantly chronic biopsy findings who sought advice about future transplantation. We distinguish direct transplant observations from non-transplant post-SVR data, treatment evidence for active disease, and expert-derived proposals. Direct evidence in HCV-cured patients with cryoglobulinaemic kidney disease is sparse, and comparative evidence for pretransplant immunomodulation for isolated serologic persistence is lacking. We propose an unvalidated expert framework comprising active disease, uncertain activity, and clinical remission with persistent serologic abnormalities; these are provisional clinical descriptions rather than validated risk categories or eligibility criteria. Assessment should centre on organ activity, clinically significant clonal disease, and follow-up requirements. In our view, persistent serologic abnormalities alone, in the absence of objective organ activity or clinically significant clonal disease, should not be the determinant of transplant eligibility.

#### 摘要

BACKGROUND AND AIM: Leptospirosis is a zoonotic infection with a wide spectrum of clinical manifestations. Hepatic involvement is common, particularly in severe forms, such as Weil's disease, and poses diagnostic and therapeutic challenges.
MATERIALS AND METHODS: We retrospectively evaluated the general characteristics and outcomes of patients diagnosed with leptospirosis at Harran University, Department of Gastroenterology between May 2019 and September 2025.
RESULTS: We included 30 patients (86.7% male) with a median age of 45 years (range: 18-78 years); the median total bilirubin at presentation was 9.1 mg/dL (range: 2.5-49.0 mg/dL). All patients showed features of moderate-to-severe liver injury. A total of 18 (60%) patients developed acute kidney injury, 10 (33.3%) had pulmonary involvement, myocarditis was identified in 3 (10%) patients, and 5 (16.7%) patients exhibited neurological manifestations. All patients received antibiotics (doxycycline, cephalosporins, and carbapenems). Corticosteroids were given to nine patients. Therapeutic plasma exchange was performed in 12 (40%) patients. Seventeen (56.7%) patients required intensive care unit (ICU) admission, and 3 (10%) required invasive mechanical ventilation. Median creatinine (2.1 vs. 1.2 mg/dL, p=0.004) was higher, pulmonary involvement was more common (52.9% vs. 7.7%, p=0.017), plasma exchange (58.8% vs. 15.4%, p=0.026) and corticosteroid use (52.9% vs. 0%, p = 0.003) were more common among patients admitted to the ICU than among those not admitted to the ICU. Two (6.7%) patients did not respond to therapy and died during follow-up.
CONCLUSION: Leptospirosis presenting with liver injury and pulmonary involvement is associated with a higher probability of ICU admission. Corticosteroids and/or plasma exchange can be used, but prospective studies are necessary to reveal their prognostic relevance in patients presenting with severe leptospirosis.

### [A] Therapeutic plasma exchange and continuous renal replacement therapy for severe dengue with multiorgan failure: a case report and focused review.

- 作者: Mestre Tejo A, Sales Dos Santos M, Fortes Osório Bustamante P, Carvalho Sousa Esteves FV, Medauar Reis de Andrade Moreira F, Castelo-Branco U, et al.

- DOI: 10.1016/S1473-3099(26)00483-4
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/43044623/
- 日报日期: 2026-11-18

#### 摘要

Severe dengue can progress to acute liver failure, haemolysis, encephalopathy, shock, and multiorgan dysfunction, but the role of therapeutic plasma exchange in treatment remains uncertain. We report a 17-year-old adolescent with NS1-positive dengue managed with continuous renal replacement therapy, therapeutic plasma exchange, and comprehensive intensive care, and provide a focused narrative review of therapeutic plasma exchange in severe dengue. The patient developed abrupt massive intravascular haemolysis, profound anaemia, thrombocytopenia, coagulopathy, encephalopathy requiring invasive mechanical ventilation, oliguric acute kidney injury, shock, and acute liver failure. She also met five of eight HLH-2004 criteria for secondary haemophagocytic lymphohistiocytosis. Continuous renal replacement therapy was initiated before three therapeutic plasma exchange sessions, beginning with an exchange of 1·5 estimated plasma volumes using fresh frozen plasma. Haemodynamic, hepatic, renal, and coagulation parameters subsequently improved. Secondary infection and haemothorax requiring video-assisted thoracoscopic drainage complicated recovery; she was discharged 38 days after admission. Published evidence on the use of therapeutic plasma exchange in severe dengue consists mainly of case reports, small series, and observational cohorts. Therapeutic plasma exchange can be considered an adjunctive rescue therapy in selected refractory cases, particularly acute liver failure or thrombotic microangiopathy phenotypes, but its independent benefit, optimal timing, prescription, and target population remain undefined.

### [A] Granulomatosis with polyangiitis complicated by anti-GBM nephritis: a clinicopathological case of PR3-ANCA-positive double-positive disease.

- PMID: 43043004
- Domain: Out-of-scope
- Domain 依据: No strong CCM/vascular-malformation or transferable vascular mechanism evidence
- 转化相关性: Exploratory
- 转化相关性依据: Independent plasma exchange/blood exchange research interest; not disease-domain relevance
- 优先级依据: A: plasma exchange/plasmapheresis/blood exchange/immunoadsorption
- 主题: 换血/血浆置换疗法
- 来源类型: 今日新文献
- 独立科研兴趣: Plasma exchange / blood exchange
- 期刊: Modern rheumatology case reports
- 作者: Hiraoka A, Iwai T, Aoki T, Sasaki Y, Kosaka T, Yoshinaga S, et al.
- 发表日期: 2026 Nov 17
- DOI: 10.1093/mrcr/rxag102
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/43043004/
- 日报日期: 2026-11-18

#### 摘要

Anti-glomerular basement membrane (anti-GBM) nephritis is an autoimmune disease characterized by rapidly progressive glomerulonephritis, which is histologically defined by crescentic glomerulonephritis and linear immunoglobulin G (IgG) deposition. Granulomatosis with polyangiitis (GPA) is an anti-neutrophil cytoplasmic antibody (ANCA)-associated vasculitis characterized by necrotizing granulomatous inflammation and is strongly associated with proteinase 3 (PR3)-ANCA. The coexistence of anti-GBM nephritis and ANCA-associated vasculitis, termed "double-positive" disease, is increasingly recognized, although most cases are associated with myeloperoxidase-ANCA. A 77-year-old woman presented with fever, hearing loss, and renal dysfunction. Renal biopsy revealed crescentic glomerulonephritis with linear IgG deposition, and serum anti-GBM antibodies were positive, consistent with anti-GBM nephritis. She was diagnosed with GPA based on refractory otitis media, PR3-ANCA positivity, and granulomatous inflammation with multinucleated giant cells on renal biopsy. The patient was treated with glucocorticoids, intravenous cyclophosphamide, and plasma exchange (14 sessions), followed by avacopan. Renal function was preserved, and no progression to end-stage renal disease was observed at 10 months. We report a rare clinicopathological case of PR3-ANCA-positive double-positive disease with coexisting GPA and anti-GBM nephritis. This case highlights the characteristic clinicopathological features supporting the coexistence of these two diseases. The possible relationship between GPA and anti-GBM disease remains hypothetical and requires further investigation.

### [A] Therapeutic plasma exchange improves short-term survival in patients with acute-on-chronic liver failure: A randomized controlled trial.

- PMID: 41911559
- Domain: Out-of-scope

- 作者: Swaroop S, Biswas S, Aggarwal A, Coshic P, Kumar S, Agarwal S, et al.
- 发表日期: 2026 Dec 01
- DOI: 10.1097/HEP.0000000000001755
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41911559/
- 日报日期: 2026-11-18

#### 摘要

BACKGROUND AND AIMS: This randomized controlled trial (RCT) evaluated the efficacy and safety of therapeutic plasma exchange (TPE) versus standard medical therapy (SMT) in acute-on-chronic liver failure (ACLF).
APPROACH AND RESULTS: In this single-center, open-label RCT (February 2022-March 2025), we randomly (1:1) assigned 194 adult patients (aged 18-60 y) with ACLF [European Association for the Study of the Liver-Chronic Liver Failure Consortium (EASL CLIF-C) grades 1-3b] to receive SMT plus 5 sessions of TPE (n=97) or SMT alone (n=97). The primary outcome was all-cause mortality at 28 days. Secondary outcomes included 90-day mortality, changes in prognostic scores, resolution of organ failures, infections, and adverse events. Baseline characteristics were comparable between groups. Seventy-two (74.2%) patients completed 3 sessions of TPE, while 56 (57.7%) received all 5 sessions. Hemodynamic instability was the leading cause for discontinuation of TPE (18/41 patients, 43.9%). The 28-day mortality was significantly lower with TPE (43 patients, 44.3%; 95% CI: 34.2%-54.8%) than SMT (62 patients, 63.9%; 95% CI: 53.5%-73.4%), p =0.006; absolute risk reduction: 19.6% (95% CI: 5.8%-33.3%). Ninety-day mortality was similar between groups. TPE resulted in greater reductions in ammonia and higher rates of resolution of hepatic and coagulation failure by day 7 as compared with SMT. Subgroup analysis did not reveal significant heterogeneity across ACLF grade or etiology. Dyselectrolytemia and shivering were the most common complications after TPE.
CONCLUSION: TPE significantly improves 28-day survival and organ failure resolution in patients with ACLF, but does not reduce 90-day mortality [CTRI/2022/01/039094].

### [A] Acquired thrombotic thrombocytopenic purpura in limited cutaneous systemic sclerosis: a case report.

- PMID: 43055731
- Domain: Out-of-scope
- Domain 依据: No strong CCM/vascular-malformation or transferable vascular mechanism evidence
- 转化相关性: Exploratory
- 转化相关性依据: Independent plasma exchange/blood exchange research interest; not disease-domain relevance

- 主题: 换血/血浆置换疗法
- 来源类型: 今日新文献
- 独立科研兴趣: Plasma exchange / blood exchange
- 期刊: Modern rheumatology case reports
- 作者: Mori K, Hoshi Y, Sato H, Shirai T, Fujii H
- 发表日期: 2026 Nov 19
- DOI: 10.1093/mrcr/rxag107
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/43055731/
- 日报日期: 2026-11-20

#### 摘要

Although thrombotic microangiopathy (TMA) is occasionally observed in patients with systemic sclerosis (SSc), thrombotic thrombocytopenic purpura (TTP) is extremely rare. Herein, we report a rare case of limited cutaneous SSc (lcSSc) complicated by TTP. A 44-year-old woman with anti-centromere antibody (ACA)-positive lcSSc of two years' duration presented with purpura, severe thrombocytopenia, and microangiopathic haemolytic anaemia. Her blood pressure, serum creatinine levels, and neurological examination results were normal. Although TTP, SSc-associated TMA, and scleroderma renal crisis (SRC) were considered in the differential diagnosis, plasma exchange alone was initially started because glucocorticoids (GCs) are associated with an increased risk of renal crisis. Subsequent testing revealed ADAMTS13 activity below 1%, with an inhibitor level of 1.0 Bethesda units (BU)/mL, establishing a diagnosis of acquired TTP. Despite starting prednisolone 40 mg/day, her platelet count dropped to 8,000/μL and she developed dysarthria, right hemiparesis, fever, and facial palsy. Moreover, her inhibitor level rose to 1.9 BU/mL after eight exchanges. GC pulse therapy and rituximab were added while plasma exchange continued. The platelet count recovered, and she has remained relapse-free for four years. A literature review identified 16 reports on 17 patients with SSc and TTP. None met current diagnostic criteria or had a documented ACA. In conclusion, acquired TTP can occur in ACA-positive lcSSc. Empirical GC therapy while awaiting the ADAMTS13 result may be considered in selected patients in whom TTP is strongly suspected and the estimated risk of SRC is low.

### [A] From rescue to precision: breaking the salvage-therapy curse in refractory septic shock.

- PMID: 43050886
- Domain: Out-of-scope
- Domain 依据: No strong CCM/vascular-malformation or transferable vascular mechanism evidence
- 转化相关性: Exploratory
- 转化相关性依据: Independent plasma exchange/blood exchange research interest; not disease-domain relevance
- 优先级依据: A: plasma exchange/plasmapheresis/blood exchange/immunoadsorption
- 主题: 换血/血浆置换疗法
- 来源类型: 今日新文献
- 独立科研兴趣: Plasma exchange / blood exchange
- 期刊: Journal of critical care
- 作者: Maan P
- 发表日期: 2026 Nov 18
- DOI: 10.1016/j.jcrc.2026.155826
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/43050886/
- 日报日期: 2026-11-20

#### 摘要

Refractory septic shock is managed with a small core of evidence-based treatments and a growing number of salvage therapies, including methylene blue, the vitamin C cocktail, and several blood purification devices. Each has a plausible mechanism, may improve physiological or haemodynamic surrogates, and most remain without definitive evidence of survival benefit in unselected patients. Recent biomarker-selected evidence with polymyxin B, however, suggests that patient selection may be critical. This article argues that most remain unproven because of how they are deployed and tested, not because their mechanisms fail. Given late to the sickest patients, they are subject to confounding by indication, a ceiling effect on attribution, and responder-biased reporting, which together generate a misleading reputation independent of any true effect. Drawing on oXiris, vitamin C, and polymyxin B as worked examples, and on methylene blue and therapeutic plasma exchange as unresolved cases now in trials, the article contends that the way to resolve this problem is to identify a measurable treatable trait and randomise the patients who have it. Polymyxin B illustrates this, having been neutral in an unselected trial and beneficial once patients were selected by endotoxin activity.

### [A] Influenza A-Associated Encephalitis and Encephalopathy in Adults: An Exploratory Criteria-Based Analysis.

- PMID: 43049832
- Domain: Out-of-scope
- Domain 依据: No strong CCM/vascular-malformation or transferable vascular mechanism evidence
- 转化相关性: Exploratory
- 转化相关性依据: Independent plasma exchange/blood exchange research interest; not disease-domain relevance
- 优先级依据: A: plasma exchange/plasmapheresis/blood exchange/immunoadsorption
- 主题: 换血/血浆置换疗法
- 来源类型: 今日新文献
- 独立科研兴趣: Plasma exchange / blood exchange
- 期刊: Neurology and therapy
- 作者: Gómez-Porro Sánchez P, Trebolle Banet J, de la Puente Garrido B, Ruiz Molina Á, González Santiago R, Brea Álvarez B, et al.
- 发表日期: 2026 Nov 18
- DOI: 10.1007/s40120-026-01087-8
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/43049832/
- 日报日期: 2026-11-20

#### 摘要

INTRODUCTION: Influenza A can cause encephalopathy or encephalitis, yet adult data are scarce and the two entities are seldom distinguished systematically. This small retrospective study of five adults explored the feasibility and limitations of classifying these complications with the International Encephalitis Consortium (IEC) 2013 criteria and describes their clinical, radiological, cerebrospinal fluid (CSF), and treatment characteristics.
METHODS: We retrospectively reviewed five consecutive adults admitted to a tertiary center with polymerase chain reaction (PCR)-confirmed influenza A and acute neurological symptoms, all evaluated with brain magnetic resonance imaging (MRI), lumbar puncture, and electroencephalography (EEG). Encephalitis was defined by the IEC 2013 criteria (altered mental status > 24 h plus ≥ 2 minor criteria of central nervous system (CNS) inflammation). Data were analyzed descriptively.
RESULTS: Three patients met criteria for encephalitis and two for encephalopathy (four women; age 44-68 years). Respiratory influenza A PCR was positive and CSF PCR negative in all. CSF pleocytosis occurred in two patients, bilateral thalamic and subcortical white matter MRI hyperintensities in two, generalized EEG slowing in four, and focal status epilepticus in one. All received oseltamivir; three received high-dose methylprednisolone, two of whom also required intravenous immunoglobulin and plasma exchange. The categories overlapped substantially: EEG slowing and negative CSF PCR occurred in both, and treatment escalation followed clinical course rather than category, with the most intensive regimen required by one patient in each. At 3 months, three patients were asymptomatic and two, both with encephalitis and structural MRI lesions, had persistent deficits.
CONCLUSION: In this small, hypothesis-generating study, IEC-based classification was feasible, but encephalitis and encephalopathy overlapped substantially and the distinction did not determine treatment. The main contribution lies in illustrating both the feasibility and limitations of criteria-based classification in adults with influenza A, rather than in establishing its clinical utility.

### [A] Clinical and biochemical factors associated with short-term outcomes in patients with acute-on-chronic liver failure managed with an extracorporeal liver support-based protocol in Vietnam.

- PMID: 43049743
- Domain: Out-of-scope

- 期刊: Blood purification
- 作者: Vuong Xuan T, Van Bui C, Dang Quoc T, Nguyen Cong T, Bui Thi Huong G, Pham The T, et al.
- 发表日期: 2026 Nov 18
- DOI: 10.1159/bpu/abpag016
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/43049743/
- 日报日期: 2026-11-20

#### 摘要

INTRODUCTION: To describe clinical and biochemical factors associated with short-term outcomes, adverse events, and longitudinal biomarker trajectories in patients with acute-on-chronic liver failure (ACLF) managed using an extracorporeal liver support (ELS) - based protocol.
METHODS: This prospective single-center observational cohort at Bach Mai Hospital included a convenience sample of 70 patients with ACLF: 35 received high-volume plasma exchange (HVP) and 35 received double plasma molecular adsorption system plus low-volume plasma exchange (DPMAS+LPE), selected by the treating team. The modalities were not randomized or designed for comparative effectiveness evaluation. Longitudinal measurements used the CRF-defined procedural labels: immediately before an ELS session (T0), immediately after removal of the DPMAS adsorption cartridges (T1), and at completion of the entire ELS session (T2). Outcomes were survival at Day 28 after hospital admission and recovery status at hospital discharge.
RESULTS: Survival at Day 28 was 92.9% (65/70). Recovery at hospital discharge was successful in 37.1% (26/70). Baseline direct bilirubin was lower in the mortality group, but the mortality analysis was limited by only five deaths. Successful recovery was associated with lower neutrophil percentage, creatinine, IL-6, and severity scores and with higher platelet count, PT percentage, and bicarbonate. Descriptive modality-stratified trajectories showed decreases in total bilirubin after ELS; these trajectories should not be interpreted as a comparative treatment-effect analysis.
CONCLUSION: In this small real-world cohort managed under an ELS-based protocol, recovery was associated with systemic inflammation, renal function, coagulation, and organ-failure severity. Because treatment allocation was non-randomized and only five deaths occurred, the findings are exploratory and require external validation.

### [A] Apheresis activities of the transplantation and cellular therapies society in Türkiye: Current practice, scientific contributions, and a roadmap for the cellular-therapy era.

- PMID: 43044382
- Domain: Out-of-scope
- Domain 依据: No strong CCM/vascular-malformation or transferable vascular mechanism evidence
- 转化相关性: Exploratory
- 转化相关性依据: Independent plasma exchange/blood exchange research interest; not disease-domain relevance
- 优先级依据: A: plasma exchange/plasmapheresis/blood exchange/immunoadsorption
- 主题: 换血/血浆置换疗法
- 来源类型: 近期补位
- 独立科研兴趣: Plasma exchange / blood exchange
- 期刊: Transfusion and apheresis science : official journal of the World Apheresis Association : official journal of the European Society for Haemapheresis
- 作者: Erkurt MA, Korkmaz S, Dal MS, Yigenoğlu TN, Altuntas F
- 发表日期: 2026 Nov 14
- DOI: 10.1016/j.transci.2026.104570
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/43044382/
- 日报日期: 2026-11-20

#### 摘要

Apheresis has become a core clinical and manufacturing interface linking transfusion medicine, hematology, hematopoietic cell transplantation, immune modulation, and advanced cellular therapy. Türkiye has developed substantial activity in this field through dedicated apheresis units, high-volume transplant programs, national surveys, multicenter registries, and a growing body of PubMed-indexed scientific work. This review examines Turkish apheresis activity from the perspective of the Transplantation and Cellular Therapies Society and emphasizes how therapeutic plasma exchange (TPE), thrombotic thrombocytopenic purpura (TTP) and other thrombotic microangiopathies (TMAs), extracorporeal photopheresis (ECP), hematopoietic progenitor cell (HPC) mobilization and collection, and cellular-therapy leukapheresis form a unified clinical and quality continuum. A landmark national survey documented 24,912 TPE procedures in 3203 patients across 28 Turkish apheresis units, while subsequent registry and multicenter publications demonstrated the feasibility of broader collaborative data collection. Turkish investigators have contributed studies addressing hematologic TPE, HELLP syndrome, TMA, ECP for acute and chronic graft-versus-host disease (GVHD), venous access for HPC collection, mobilization strategies and kinetics, and other procedure-related questions. The 17th International Congress of the World Apheresis Association, held in Istanbul on 14-17 September 2022, further strengthened international visibility and professional networking. The field should now transition from descriptive procedure counts to disease-specific, outcome-oriented quality systems. We propose a society-based roadmap built on five pillars: a prospective national registry, standardized high-impact clinical pathways, a common quality dashboard, structured multidisciplinary education and competency assessment, and deliberate integration of apheresis with CAR-T and other advanced cellular-therapy programs. Such an approach would position apheresis as a strategic national infrastructure for precision hematology, transplantation, and cellular therapy rather than as an isolated technical service.
