- 作者: Guo L, Nian Y, Du Q, Xu Y, Yang S, Zhao J, et al.
- 发表日期: 2026
- DOI: 10.3389/fimmu.2026.1897363
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/43054022/
- 日报日期: 2026-11-20

#### 摘要

Severe iliac arteriosclerosis is a contraindication for kidney transplantation (KT). This report details a case of a 34-year-old female with type 2 diabetes mellitus and severe iliac arteriosclerosis who underwent ABO-incompatible living donor KT in which the renal graft artery was anastomosed to the self-expandable endovascular covered stent placed in the external iliac artery. The initial anti-blood group anti-A antibody titers were 1:4 and 1:32 for IgG and IgM, respectively. Routine pre-transplant therapy including rituximab 200mg, triple immunosuppressants included tacrolimus (7mg/d, tacrolimus concentration was between 3.8 and 9.2 ng/mL), mycophenolate mofetil (1500mg/d), methylprednisolone (16mg/d). Following one session of double filtration plasmapheresis, anti-blood group antibody titers were reduced 1:4 and 1:8 for IgG and IgM. Two days prior to surgery, patient underwent one session of plasma exchange and antibody titers were IgG 1:4 and IgM 1:8 on the day of KT. Basiliximab was administered for immune induction. The initial routine was performed with conventional end-to-side anastomosis between the renal graft artery and the right external iliac artery. However, pulsation of the transplant renal artery disappeared shortly. Upon opening the anastomosis, thrombosis at the anastomotic site was identified due to plaque embolism from the external iliac artery. Consequently, a self-expandable endovascular covered stent was implanted into the external iliac artery via the right femoral artery under intervention. Because part of the external iliac artery wall was defective, the endovascular stent was partially exposed, used the prosthetic graft for coverage and reinforcement. Finally, the renal allograft artery was anastomosed end-to-side to the stent-prosthetic complex. The anti-blood group antibody titers were 1:2 and 1:4 for IgG and IgM and serum creatinine was 124 μmol/L at 1 month. During the 2 years follow-up, graft function remained satisfactory, the serum creatinine levels were 116, 107, and 113 μmol/L at 6 months, 1 year, and 2 years postoperatively, and no signs of right lower limb ischemia were evident. This study demonstrates that renal graft implanted on the endovascular stent is a feasible therapeutic option for recipients with severe iliac arteriosclerosis.

### [A] Case Report: Raw grass carp gallbladder ingestion and acute liver injury.

- PMID: 43041350
- Domain: Out-of-scope
- Domain 依据: No strong CCM/vascular-malformation or transferable vascular mechanism evidence
- 转化相关性: Exploratory
- 转化相关性依据: Independent plasma exchange/blood exchange research interest; not disease-domain relevance
- 优先级依据: A: plasma exchange/plasmapheresis/blood exchange/immunoadsorption
- 主题: 换血/血浆置换疗法
- 来源类型: 近期补位
- 独立科研兴趣: Plasma exchange / blood exchange
- 期刊: Frontiers in pharmacology
- 作者: Xiang S, Zheng Z, Chen Y, Du Y
- 发表日期: 2026
- DOI: 10.3389/fphar.2026.1925389
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/43041350/
- 日报日期: 2026-11-20

#### 摘要

Raw grass carp gallbladder ingestion for folk medicinal purposes may cause severe acute liver injury driven by the hepatotoxin 5α-cyprinol sulfate, yet high-dose human poisoning cases remain infrequently documented. We report a 60-year-old male (60 kg body weight) who developed severe intoxication 22 h after consuming six raw gallbladders from grass carps of approximately 1.5 kg each. Nausea, vomiting, abdominal pain and watery diarrhoea occurred within 2 hours of ingestion. Laboratory testing on admission demonstrated extreme hepatocellular injury (ALT 6285 U/L, AST 9321 U/L). Conventional hepatoprotective agents plus pre-hospital haemoperfusion yielded limited biochemical improvement. While admitted to the intensive care unit, the patient received one session of double plasma molecular adsorption system, three sessions of plasma exchange and four sessions of continuous renal replacement therapy (CRRT, CVVHDF modality) alongside comprehensive supportive care. Liver function normalized within 14 hospital days; no acute kidney injury was observed. Complete symptomatic recovery was documented at the 4-week follow-up visit, though longer-term outcomes remain unknown given the limited follow-up duration. Toxin-mediated hepatocyte damage is partially supported by pre-clinical cyprinol-sulfate experimental work; many downstream oxidative, mitochondrial, endoplasmic-reticulum and inflammatory molecular pathways remain hypothetical and are largely extrapolated from unrelated liver-injury models, without direct validation in human carp-gallbladder-poisoning tissue specimens. Biochemical improvement was temporally associated with multimodal extracorporeal purification, but causal therapeutic benefit can't be proven from this uncontrolled single case; spontaneous hepatic regeneration may also contribute to recovery. This case highlights substantial hazards associated with folk-health beliefs promoting raw fish-gallbladder consumption, emphasising the need for early clinical recognition of fish-gallbladder poisoning.

- 日报日期: 2026-11-20

#### 摘要

Intravenous immunoglobulins (IVIg), together with plasma exchange (PLEX), remain one of the standard treatments for acute worsening of generalized autoimmune myasthenia gravis, including myasthenic exacerbations and myasthenic crises, the latter representing the most severe form of disease deterioration. In a context marked by the emergence of new targeted therapies, particularly neonatal Fc receptor (FcRn) inhibitors and complement inhibitors (CI), as well as recurrent shortages of IVIg supply, the ability to identify patients who are likely or unlikely to respond to IVIg has become a major clinical challenge. We retrospectively analysed a cohort of 93 patients who received IVIg during a first myasthenic exacerbation or crisis. Clinical improvement was observed in 87% of patients. No clinical, biological, electrophysiological, or disease-history-related factor was significantly associated with a poorer treatment response. These findings confirm the overall effectiveness of IVIg and support their continued role as a first-line treatment for acute worsening in generalized myasthenia gravis.

### [A] Functional Autoantibodies Against G-Protein-Coupled Receptors: Insights into Molecular Mechanisms, Clinical Relevance and Therapeutic Perspectives.

- PMID: 43027730
- Domain: Out-of-scope
- Domain 依据: No strong CCM/vascular-malformation or transferable vascular mechanism evidence
- 转化相关性: Exploratory
- 转化相关性依据: Independent plasma exchange/blood exchange research interest; not disease-domain relevance
- 优先级依据: A: plasma exchange/plasmapheresis/blood exchange/immunoadsorption
- 主题: 换血/血浆置换疗法
- 来源类型: 近期补位
- 独立科研兴趣: Plasma exchange / blood exchange
- 期刊: International journal of molecular sciences
- 作者: Zwerenz R, Lakatos P, Flecks M, Wallukat G, Hohberger B
- 发表日期: 2026 Oct 23
- DOI: 10.3390/ijms27219475
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/43027730/
- 日报日期: 2026-11-20

#### 摘要

G-protein-coupled receptors (GPCRs) represent the largest superfamily of membrane-bound receptors in the human organism and are involved in the regulation of numerous physiological processes in almost all organ systems. Characterized by seven transmembrane domains and coupling to heterotrimeric G-proteins, they mediate signal transduction between extracellular stimuli and intracellular responses. Through these mechanisms they regulate critical processes such as cardiovascular function, neurotransmission, immune function, endocrine secretion, and metabolic homeostasis. Growing evidence suggests that functional autoantibodies directed against GPCRs (GPCR-fAAbs) may contribute to the pathophysiology of various diseases. By binding to extracellularly accessible regions of their target receptors, these antibodies can directly modulate receptor function and intracellular signaling. Depending on the receptor, epitope, and functional properties of the autoantibody, GPCR-fAAbs can act as agonists and activate receptor function or as antagonists and inhibit receptor function or alter the receptor signaling. Importantly, the presence of GPCR-directed autoantibodies does not necessarily indicate functional activity or pathogenicity, and their biological effects may depend on both autoantibody-specific and cellular factors. The potential role of GPCR-fAAbs has been described in a growing number of autoimmune and non-autoimmune diseases affecting various organ systems. This review article aims to provide a comprehensive overview of the current knowledge on GPCRs and GPCR-fAAbs focusing on their molecular mechanisms, functional characteristics, and potential pathophysiological roles in various diseases. Furthermore, current detection methods, clinical findings and established and emerging therapeutic strategies, including immunoadsorption (IA), aptamer-based neutralization, receptor blocking, and targeted B cell modulation, are critically discussed. Although all these approaches present certain challenges in their application, specific prospects for clinical implementation can be highlighted. Overall, GPCR-fAAbs represent an increasingly recognized pathophysiological factor with potential clinical implications, while their targeted therapeutic modulation remains an evolving area of translational research.

### [A] Ocular-Predominant Waldenström Macroglobulinemia Presenting With Subconjunctival Hemorrhage, Diffuse Roth Spots, and Bilateral Neurosensory Retinal Detachment: A Case Report.

- DOI: 10.7759/cureus.118113
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/43048614/
- 日报日期: 2026-11-20

#### 摘要

Waldenström macroglobulinemia (WM) is a rare lymphoplasmacytic lymphoma characterized by bone marrow infiltration and monoclonal immunoglobulin M (IgM) production. Ophthalmologic findings as the initial manifestation are uncommon. A 66-year-old man presented with sudden-onset left subconjunctival hemorrhage, ocular pruritus, and reduced visual acuity (VA). Fundoscopy revealed bilateral diffuse retinal hemorrhages with 360-degree Roth spots, neurosensory retinal detachment, and macular edema. Laboratory evaluation showed normocytic anemia, thrombocytopenia, and an IgM-kappa monoclonal protein (serum IgM: 5,040 mg/dL; reference range: 40-230 mg/dL); imaging demonstrated splenomegaly and lymphadenopathy. Bone marrow biopsy supported the diagnosis of WM. The course was complicated by hyperviscosity, hyperuricemia, and cytomegalovirus (CMV) reactivation. Therapeutic plasma exchange and systemic therapy achieved marked hematologic response; however, neurosensory retinal detachment persisted despite a single intravitreal aflibercept 2 mg in each eye. This case represents an unusual ocular-predominant presentation of WM. Roth spots, retinal hemorrhages, and neurosensory retinal detachment, particularly with cytopenias or hyperproteinemia, should prompt investigation for systemic hematologic disease. Visual recovery may remain incomplete despite adequate hematologic response.

- DOI: 10.1055/a-3006-7350
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/43057321/

#### 摘要

CASPR2-associated neurological diseases cover a broad clinical spectrum, with limbic encephalitis and Morvan syndrome representing the most characteristic autoimmune phenotypes. We report the case of a 2-year-old boy who presented with abdominal and generalized pain, refusal to eat or drink, weight loss, loss of mobility, and behavioral changes, including sleep disturbances. Further clinical evaluation revealed pharmacoresistant arterial hypertension and hyperhidrosis. Extensive diagnostic testing excluded infectious and toxic causes. CASPR2 antibodies were detected in both serum and cerebrospinal fluid. Initial therapy with corticosteroids and intravenous immunoglobulins resulted in only minimal clinical improvement. Significant clinical recovery was only achieved after escalation of immunotherapy with plasmapheresis and subsequent B-cell-depleting therapy with rituximab. At the most recent follow-up visit, seven months after the first rituximab infusion, the patient showed complete recovery without residual symptoms. This case underscores the diagnostic challenges of CASPR2-associated autoimmunity in early childhood and highlights the importance of early recognition of this rare but treatable disease.

### [A] Status Epilepsy Syndromes Made Easy: Pediatric Perspectives.

- PMID: 41462849
- Domain: Out-of-scope
- Domain 依据: No strong CCM/vascular-malformation or transferable vascular mechanism evidence
- 转化相关性: Exploratory
- 转化相关性依据: Independent plasma exchange/blood exchange research interest; not disease-domain relevance
- 优先级依据: A: plasma exchange/plasmapheresis/blood exchange/immunoadsorption
- 主题: 换血/血浆置换疗法
- 来源类型: 经典补位
- 独立科研兴趣: Plasma exchange / blood exchange
- 期刊: Children (Basel, Switzerland)
- 作者: Hon KLE, Leung AKC, Leung KKY, Torres AR
- 发表日期: 2025 Dec 17
- DOI: 10.3390/children12121709
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41462849/
- 日报日期: 2026-11-21

#### 摘要

Introduction: Refractory Status Epilepsy Syndrome is a heterogeneous group of diseases associated with status epilepsy. Literature and definition have been conflicting and confusing in terms of their nomenclatures. New-onset refractory status epilepticus (NORSE) is a syndrome characterized by new onset refractory seizures in a previously health child. Febrile infection-related epilepsy syndrome (FIRES) is a similar syndrome now considered a variant of NORSE and is defined as a febrile event taking place between twenty-four hours and two weeks prior to the commencement of refractory status epilepticus. An autoimmune or inflammatory etiology is often implied in both conditions because infection is rarely identified. Aim: This review provides an update on hypotheses, etiology, pathophysiology, clinical features, diagnosis, laboratory evaluation, treatment, and perspectives for NORSE/FIRES. Methods: A PubMed Clinical Queries search is performed using keywords of NORSE and FIRES, on human subjects up to May 2025. All reviews, systematic reviews, case series and case reports were included. Results: Seizures are typically recalcitrant in NORSE/FIRES. Treatments include anti-seizure medications (ASM), ketogenic diet, immunotherapy (intravenous immunoglobulin ± plasmapheresis ± corticosteroid). The prognosis is usually poor. Most children would suffer refractory epilepsy and associated cognitive impairment if they survived. Guidelines and new consensus on NORSE/FIRES terminology have aided clinicians in managing status epilepticus in a previously healthy child that occurs ± a minor febrile episode. When an autoimmune or paraneoplastic condition is subsequently identified, the condition will be named accordingly. Conclusions: NORSE and FIRES are similar conditions except that vagus nerve stimulation appears to be more efficacious in NORSE than FIRES. We propose to define these heterogeneous and confusing conditions as "NOSES" as a two-criteria syndrome: New Onset + Status Epilepticus Syndrome, lasting for over 24 h despite the use of two standard ASM. Autoimmune, paraneoplastic and infectious encephalitis are specific diagnoses of NOSES with etiology subsequently identified.

### [A] Neonatal Hyperbilirubinemia in Uganda: Mechanisms, Clinical Consequences, and Health-System Challenges.

- PMID: 41438168
- Domain: Out-of-scope
- Domain 依据: No strong CCM/vascular-malformation or transferable vascular mechanism evidence
- 转化相关性: Exploratory
- 转化相关性依据: Independent plasma exchange/blood exchange research interest; not disease-domain relevance
- 优先级依据: A: plasma exchange/plasmapheresis/blood exchange/immunoadsorption
- 主题: 换血/血浆置换疗法
- 来源类型: 经典补位
- 独立科研兴趣: Plasma exchange / blood exchange
- 期刊: Pediatric health, medicine and therapeutics
- 作者: Hamdi MY, Nankunda J, Hassan HA, Said ZA, Mohamud AH, Kahie FD, et al.
- 发表日期: 2025
- DOI: 10.2147/PHMT.S530595
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41438168/
- 日报日期: 2026-11-21

#### 摘要

OBJECTIVE: Neonatal hyperbilirubinemia is a prevalent condition characterized by elevated serum bilirubin levels, affecting approximately 50% of term and 80% of preterm infants. While mild cases often resolve without intervention, severe hyperbilirubinemia can lead to life-threatening complications such as bilirubin-induced neurological dysfunction (BIND) and kernicterus, resulting in permanent brain damage, developmental delays, and hearing impairment.
MATERIALS AND METHODS: A literature review was conducted by searching PubMed, Google Scholar, and UpToDate for English-language articles published between January 2010 and March 2025 on neonatal hyperbilirubinemia. Inclusion criteria were studies on mechanisms, clinical outcomes, and interventions in low- and middle-income countries (LMICs), with a focus on Uganda. Exclusion criteria included case reports, non-English studies, and articles without primary data.
RESULTS: The condition arises from an imbalance between bilirubin production and elimination, influenced by immature liver function, increased red blood cell turnover, enhanced enterohepatic circulation, and genetic predisposition. In low-income countries such as Uganda, additional contributors include delayed postnatal care-seeking, limited availability of phototherapy units, and inadequate early screening programs. The low-income country has inappropriate early screening and poor treatment options, which further exacerbate the burden, contributing to preventable neonatal morbidity and mortality.
CONCLUSION: Timely screening, improved access to phototherapy and exchange transfusion, and increased awareness among healthcare providers and caregivers are essential for reducing the impact of neonatal hyperbilirubinemia. By integrating biological mechanisms, clinical consequences, and health-system challenges into a single synthesis, this review provides novel, actionable insights for policy-makers and clinicians in Uganda.

### [A] Plasma exchange for acute and acute-on-chronic liver failure: A systematic review and patient-level survival data meta-analysis.

- PMID: 43064247
- Domain: Out-of-scope
- Domain 依据: No strong CCM/vascular-malformation or transferable vascular mechanism evidence
- 转化相关性: Exploratory
- 转化相关性依据: Independent plasma exchange/blood exchange research interest; not disease-domain relevance
- 优先级依据: A: plasma exchange/plasmapheresis/blood exchange/immunoadsorption
- 主题: 换血/血浆置换疗法
- 来源类型: 今日新文献
- 独立科研兴趣: Plasma exchange / blood exchange
- 期刊: Annals of hepatology
- 作者: Sogbe M, de Rojas JP, Rubio-Ardanaz M, Pena-Carbo E, Hidalgo F, Duarte-Rojo A, et al.
- 发表日期: 2026 Nov 20
- DOI: 10.1016/j.aohep.2026.102494
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/43064247/
- 日报日期: 2026-11-22

#### 摘要

INTRODUCTION AND OBJECTIVES: Acute liver failure (ALF) and acute-on-chronic liver failure (ACLF) are associated with high short-term mortality. Plasma exchange (PE) has been proposed as a therapeutic strategy, but its effect on survival remains uncertain. We conducted a systematic review and reconstructed patient-level survival data meta-analysis to assess the association between PE and transplant-free survival (TFS) and post-transplant survival in ALF and ACLF.
MATERIALS AND METHODS: PubMed/MEDLINE and Scopus were searched through June 2026. Eligible studies compared PE with standard medical therapy (SMT) and reported Kaplan-Meier data suitable for reconstruction. Patient-level time-to-event data were reconstructed from curves using validated methods and analyzed with one-stage Cox proportional hazards models incorporating shared frailty to account for between-study heterogeneity. The endpoint was TFS. Sensitivity, subgroup, and restricted mean survival time (RMST) analyses were performed to evaluate robustness and clinical relevance.
RESULTS: Eighteen studies including 1440 patients were analyzed: nine studies in ALF (n=617) and nine in ACLF (n=823). In ALF, PE was associated with improved TFS compared with SMT (HR 0.46, 95% CI 0.36-0.58). In ACLF, PE was also associated with improved TFS (HR 0.61, 95% CI 0.51-0.73). Results were supported by sensitivity analyses and RMST estimates. PE was not associated with improved post-transplant survival.
CONCLUSIONS: PE was associated with significantly improved TFS in ALF and ACLF compared with SMT. These findings support PE as a valuable supportive strategy to improve TFS in selected patients with severe liver failure, while its role specifically as a bridge to liver transplantation requires further prospective evaluation.

### [A] A Rare Case of Fulminant Guillain-Barre Syndrome as the Cardinal Presenting Feature of New Onset Pediatric Systemic Lupus Erythematosus.

- PMID: 43061245
- Domain: Out-of-scope
- Domain 依据: No strong CCM/vascular-malformation or transferable vascular mechanism evidence
- 转化相关性: Exploratory

- 主题: 换血/血浆置换疗法
- 来源类型: 近期补位
- 独立科研兴趣: Plasma exchange / blood exchange
- 期刊: Case reports in rheumatology
- 作者: Coss S, Poisson KE, Driest K, Leib S, Wilson C, Sunderland E, et al.
- 发表日期: 2026
- DOI: 10.1155/crrh/9965425
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/43061245/
- 日报日期: 2026-11-22

#### 摘要

BACKGROUND: Here we present a complex case of systemic lupus erythematosus (SLE) presenting initially as fulminant Guillain-Barre syndrome (GBS). This particular manifestation of neuropsychiatric lupus is uncommon but can be severe and even life threatening. Increasing providers' awareness of the overlap between lupus and GBS will improve diagnostic accuracy and timing as well as expedite appropriate therapies, which may differ from typical GBS treatment.
CASE PRESENTATION: A 12-year-old previously healthy Hispanic female presented with subacute onset vision changes and imbalance with ophthalmoplegia, ataxia, and hyporeflexia. Given her classic clinical triad and suspicion for GBS variant Miller Fischer syndrome (MFS), neuroaxis imaging was obtained. MRI showed cranial nerve and ventral nerve root enhancement, and cytoalbuminologic dissociation seen on cerebrospinal fluid analysis also supported this diagnosis. Weakness including the facial and bulbar muscles and the extremities progressed rapidly and threatened respiratory functions, so the patient was intubated. Typical treatments for GBS including intravenous immunoglobulin (IVIG) and plasma exchange (PLEX) were insufficient to manage symptoms, and additional evaluations supported a diagnosis of SLE with nephritis. Coordinated care between neuroimmunology and rheumatology enabled rapid escalation of therapy to include high-dose intravenous steroid pulses, cyclophosphamide, and rituximab. Together, these therapies eventually stabilized the patient, and with support from neuropsychology, psychiatry, and rehabilitation medicine, she was able to discharge from the hospital after 115 days of admission. She has remained in remission on maintenance immunosuppression including mycophenolate mofetil as well as hydroxychloroquine.
CONCLUSIONS: Neuropsychiatric lupus can be difficult to diagnose and manage, but its severity necessitates vigilance and aggressive management. In the current case, the isolated neurological symptoms at presentation consistent with GBS could have delayed diagnosis, but neuroimmunology and rheumatology were consulted early, and appropriate diagnostic testing was performed. The patient's multidisciplinary team worked closely together to appropriately escalate her treatment beyond what is typical for GBS, ultimately leading to remission of her neuropsychiatric symptoms and recovery of her baseline neurological status. This case highlights the complex nature of neuropsychiatric lupus, suggests that SLE should be on the differential in atypical or difficult-to-treat cases of GBS, and underscores the need for coordinated interdisciplinary care to achieve best outcomes for these patients.

### [A] Pediatric Seropositive Autoimmune Encephalitis: A Health Outcome Study.

- DOI: 10.3791/74541

- 日报日期: 2026-11-22

#### 摘要

The autoimmune encephalitides (AE) are immune-mediated diseases of the central nervous system affecting children. This study from Lebanon describes the clinical presentations, treatments, and outcomes of antibody-positive autoimmune encephalitis in children. This is a retrospective study of children ≤ 18 years old with antibody-positive AE that presented to the American University of Beirut Medical Center (AUBMC) from January 2010 to October 2024. The Modified Rankin Scale (mRS) was used to assess outcomes, with scores of 0-2 indicating a good outcome and 3-5 indicating a poor outcome. Fisher's exact test was used to correlate outcome with categorical variables, including sex, geographic region, clinical presentation, cerebrospinal fluid (CSF) cell counts, brain imaging findings, antibody titers, time to treatment, first line treatment, second line treatment, and outcomes at 6 months follow-up, and Mann Whitney U test categorizing age as a continuous variable. Results: Seventeen patients were included. Detected antibodies were anti-myelin oligodendrocyte protein (MOG) (58.8%), N-methyl-D-aspartic acid (NMDA) receptor antibody (17.6%), neuromyelitis optica (NMO) aquaporin-4 (AQP4) antibodies (17.6%), and anti-glutamic acid decarboxylase (GAD) antibody (6%). Immunotherapy consisted of high-dose methylprednisolone, intravenous immunoglobulin (IVIG), and/or plasma exchange. Of patients with myelin oligodendrocyte antibody disease (MOGAD), 90% had a good outcome. Outcome was poor in 4/17 patients (23.5%). No variables were significantly correlated with the final outcome. Autoimmune encephalitis affects quality of life. These findings may increase awareness of pediatric AE and MOGAD in Lebanon and inform clinical recognition and management.

### [A] Pneumococcal meningitis complicated with thrombotic microangiopathy in a pediatric patient: a case report.

- PMID: 43061531
- Domain: Out-of-scope
- Domain 依据: No strong CCM/vascular-malformation or transferable vascular mechanism evidence
- 转化相关性: Exploratory

- 独立科研兴趣: Plasma exchange / blood exchange
- 期刊: Frontiers in pediatrics
- 作者: Matsumura M, Tampo A, Okada M
- 发表日期: 2026
- DOI: 10.3389/fped.2026.1870281
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/43061531/
- 日报日期: 2026-11-22

#### 摘要

BACKGROUND: Thrombotic microangiopathy (TMA) is characterized by three hallmark features: microangiopathic hemolytic anemia, thrombocytopenia, and organ injury caused by microcirculatory impairment.
CASE PRESENTATION: The patient was a 3-year-7-month-old boy who visited a previous hospital complaining of fever and was observed for acute upper respiratory tract infection. However, due to persistence of high fever, the patient revisited the previous hospital three days later, was hospitalized, and treated with antibiotics for bacterial meningitis. After admission, thrombocytopenia and hemolytic anemia progressed over time, and the patient was transferred to our hospital on the fourth day of hospitalization. Subsequently, he developed status epilepticus and was admitted to the intensive care unit (ICU). Blood tests performed at the time of transfer revealed hemolytic anemia with schistocytes, thrombocytopenia, and renal dysfunction. In addition, Streptococcus pneumoniae was isolated in blood and cerebrospinal fluid cultures performed at the previous hospital, and treatment was initiated for suspected Streptococcus pneumoniae-associated hemolytic uremic syndrome (SpHUS). On day 5, hemolytic anemia progressed, and the patient received two units of red blood cells. Despite receiving medication, the patient experienced status epilepticus and required tracheal intubation and mechanical ventilation. From day 6, no further progression of hemolytic anemia occurred, and thrombocytopenia and renal dysfunction improved over the course of treatment. With a reduced sedative dosage, no recurrences of seizures were observed. On day 10, the patient was extubated and discharged from the ICU, and on day 45, he was discharged home.
CONCLUSION: SpHUS has a different mechanism from other TMAs, and its treatment is unique, requiring consideration of the use of washed blood products and albumin preparations during blood transfusion and plasma exchange. Early recognition and treatment of SpHUS are crucial.

#### 摘要

Waldenström macroglobulinemia (WM) is an indolent mature B-cell lymphoproliferative disorder defined by bone marrow infiltration by lymphoplasmacytic lymphoma (LPL) and an associated serum monoclonal immunoglobulin M (IgM) gammopathy. In this case report, we describe a patient presenting with two serious and rare IgM-mediated complications of WM that require urgent treatment: secondary cold agglutinin syndrome and hyperviscosity syndrome. With prompt diagnosis and therapeutic plasma exchange, the patient's IgM-mediated complications improved, with a marked reduction in serum IgM and resolution of hyperviscosity-related symptoms. This highlights the importance of recognizing the possible complications of an otherwise indolent disease.

### [A] First Reported Coexistence of Acetylcholine Receptor-Positive Myasthenia Gravis and Genetically Confirmed Spinocerebellar Ataxia Type 6.

- PMID: 43047322
- Domain: Out-of-scope
- Domain 依据: No strong CCM/vascular-malformation or transferable vascular mechanism evidence
- 转化相关性: Exploratory
- 转化相关性依据: Independent plasma exchange/blood exchange research interest; not disease-domain relevance
- 优先级依据: A: plasma exchange/plasmapheresis/blood exchange/immunoadsorption
- 主题: 换血/血浆置换疗法
- 来源类型: 近期补位
- 独立科研兴趣: Plasma exchange / blood exchange
- 期刊: Cureus
- 作者: Abou Zeid J, Shin K, Sleiman P, Ahmed S, Zitouni A, Blackbourn L, et al.
- 发表日期: 2026 Oct
- DOI: 10.7759/cureus.118085
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/43047322/
- 日报日期: 2026-11-22

#### 摘要

When a patient with a long-standing neurological diagnosis develops new symptoms, those symptoms are frequently attributed to the disease that is already known, and a second, unrelated disorder may remain unrecognized for years. We describe a patient in whom myasthenia gravis (MG) and spinocerebellar ataxia type 6 (SCA6) occurred together, a combination that has not, to our knowledge, been previously reported. A 57-year-old man with acetylcholine receptor antibody-positive MG diagnosed in 1983 presented with progressive imbalance despite largely stable MG symptoms. His MG remained well controlled on low-dose prednisone and pyridostigmine following thymectomy, with intermittent azathioprine therapy. Neurological examination demonstrated cerebellar signs including dysmetria, dysdiadochokinesia, gaze-evoked nystagmus, and dysarthria. Brain MRI showed atrophy of the cerebellar hemispheres and vermis with associated brainstem volume loss. Extensive evaluation for acquired causes of cerebellar ataxia/degeneration, including paraneoplastic and autoimmune antibody testing, was unrevealing. Progressive ataxia continued despite largely stable neuromuscular symptoms. Family history revealed ataxia in his father and a similar disorder in his identical twin brother. Several years before the diagnosis was established, he experienced an MG exacerbation with bulbar and generalized weakness that responded to plasmapheresis. Genetic testing subsequently identified a pathogenic CACNA1A CAG repeat expansion of 22 repeats, confirming SCA6, just over a decade after the initial onset of his ataxia symptoms and establishing the coexistence of two distinct and rare neurological disorders. This case highlights the diagnostic challenge of evaluating new neurological symptoms in patients with established chronic, rare neurological disease. Progressive cerebellar dysfunction was initially overshadowed by the patient's longstanding MG, resulting in delayed recognition of a hereditary ataxia. Clinicians should maintain a broad differential diagnosis when symptoms evolve in a manner that is atypical for the known disease process. Recognition of coexisting neurological disorders is essential for accurate diagnosis, prognostication, genetic counseling, and multidisciplinary management. Coexistence of an acquired neuromuscular disorder and a hereditary cerebellar ataxia is possible; antibody testing to exclude an acquired ataxia and genetic confirmation of the inherited disorder are both required, and recognition of both diagnoses directly informs immunotherapy decisions, rehabilitation, and family counselling.

### [A] Pregnancy-associated aHUS with a non-complement variant: a case report. Can a coagulation gene variant dictate prognosis?

- PMID: 43064960
- Domain: Out-of-scope
- Domain 依据: No strong CCM/vascular-malformation or transferable vascular mechanism evidence
- 转化相关性: Exploratory
- 转化相关性依据: Independent plasma exchange/blood exchange research interest; not disease-domain relevance
- 优先级依据: A: plasma exchange/plasmapheresis/blood exchange/immunoadsorption
- 主题: 换血/血浆置换疗法
- 来源类型: 近期补位
- 独立科研兴趣: Plasma exchange / blood exchange
- 期刊: BMC nephrology
- 作者: Sun C, Fan Q, Qu Z, Yu F
- 发表日期: 2026 Oct 16
- DOI: 10.1186/s12882-026-05416-9
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/43064960/
- 日报日期: 2026-11-22

#### 摘要

BACKGROUND: Postpartum acute kidney injury (AKI) may arise from multiple etiologies, such as amniotic fluid embolism (AFE), renal cortical necrosis (RCN), thrombotic thrombocytopenic purpura (TTP), or atypical hemolytic uremic syndrome (aHUS). Among these, complement system genetic variants are identified in up to 60% of aHUS cases. Here, we describe a rare case of postpartum AKI due to concurrent AFE and pregnancy-associated aHUS (P-aHUS) in a patient with variant in coagulation factor II (F2) genes, suggesting a possible association between F2 variant and aHUS.
CASE PRESENTATION: A 31-year-old woman at 38 weeks of gestation developed sudden cardiovascular collapse, hypoxemia, and coagulopathy immediately following placental delivery, leading to a diagnosis of AFE complicated by disseminated intravascular coagulation (DIC). After successful resuscitation, she was transferred to the intensive care unit. Her clinical course subsequently progressed to acute kidney injury, accompanied by laboratory findings consistent with microangiopathic hemolytic anemia (presence of schistocytes), thrombocytopenia, and evidence of complement activation (markedly decreased C3 and CH50). Renal biopsy confirmed thrombotic microangiopathy (TMA) and genetic testing identified variant in F2 gene. Based on these results, we hypothesized that the variant in F2 gene contributed to complement activation which caused aHUS. Management involved plasma exchange three times per week, combined with anticoagulation therapy (rivaroxaban) and basic life support. Following this regimen, her hemolytic parameters and complement levels normalized, and renal function significantly improved, allowing discontinuation of dialysis. During six years of follow-up after discharge, her renal function remained stable with all key laboratory parameters within normal ranges.
CONCLUSION: We report a pregnancy-associated aHUS case without previously known complement variants but with a positive response to plasma exchange and anticoagulant therapy. The discovery of rare F2 variant hints at a new genetic factor that may induce aHUS.

### [A] Microscopic Polyangiitis Mimicking Colitis: A Fatal Case of Fulminant Multi-organ Vasculitis.

- PMID: 43034557
- Domain: Out-of-scope
- Domain 依据: No strong CCM/vascular-malformation or transferable vascular mechanism evidence
- 转化相关性: Exploratory
- 转化相关性依据: Independent plasma exchange/blood exchange research interest; not disease-domain relevance
- 优先级依据: A: plasma exchange/plasmapheresis/blood exchange/immunoadsorption

- 来源类型: 近期补位
- 独立科研兴趣: Plasma exchange / blood exchange
- 期刊: Cureus
- 作者: Qaisar A, Rashid S, Kaur S
- 发表日期: 2026 Oct
- DOI: 10.7759/cureus.117963
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/43034557/
- 日报日期: 2026-11-22

#### 摘要

Microscopic polyangiitis (MPA) is a systemic necrotising small vessel vasculitis that predominantly affects the kidneys and lungs. Gastrointestinal (GI) manifestations are less common and may mimic primary enteric disease, delaying recognition of the underlying systemic condition. A 72-year-old woman presented with a one-week history of bloody diarrhoea, abdominal pain, vomiting, lower limb oedema, and purpura following recent ibuprofen use. Initial investigations demonstrated severe metabolic acidosis, stage 3 acute kidney injury (AKI), with an estimated glomerular filtration rate (eGFR) of 4 mL/min/1.73 m², active urinary sediment, and computed tomography (CT) evidence of extensive colitis with small bowel enteritis. Faecal testing for multiple enteric pathogens, including Shiga toxin-producing Escherichia coli (STEC), and testing for Clostridioides difficile were negative. Strongly positive myeloperoxidase anti-neutrophil cytoplasmic antibodies (MPO-ANCA; >221.9 IU/mL) supported a diagnosis of ANCA-associated vasculitis (AAV), while anti-glomerular basement membrane (anti-GBM) antibodies were negative. Despite prompt treatment with intravenous (IV) methylprednisolone, cyclophosphamide, therapeutic plasma exchange (PLEX), and intermittent haemodialysis, the patient developed fulminant diffuse alveolar haemorrhage (DAH), refractory hypoxaemia, consumptive coagulopathy, and severe acidaemia. She died on day 8 of admission. Renal biopsy histopathology, reported after death, demonstrated necrotising crescentic pauci-immune glomerulonephritis. This case highlights the diagnostic challenges of MPO-ANCA-associated vasculitis presenting with severe gastrointestinal inflammation and bleeding alongside rapidly progressive renal disease. Although the renal findings confirmed ANCA-associated vasculitis, the underlying cause of the gastrointestinal abnormalities remained uncertain, with intestinal vasculitis and ischaemic injury secondary to hypoperfusion among the possible explanations. Recognition of systemic features in patients presenting with gastrointestinal illness and unexplained renal dysfunction is essential to facilitate timely investigation and treatment.

#### 摘要

Sickle cell retinopathy (SCR) is characterized by retinal vaso-occlusion and peripheral ischemia. We describe two patients with homozygous sickle cell disease (HbSS) who presented with extensive peripheral retinal nonperfusion during severe vaso-occlusive crises. Multimodal retinal imaging was performed during the acute phase and after systemic stabilization. Following systemic optimization, including exchange transfusion, follow-up fluorescein angiography demonstrated a reduction in peripheral retinal nonperfusion in both patients without progression of neovascularization. These findings suggest that retinal perfusion in SCR may remain dynamic following systemic optimization and raise questions regarding the optimal timing of local retinal treatment. Prospective quantitative studies are needed to further characterize these changes and their potential implications for management.

### [A] Nursing Protocols and Observed Outcomes of TR-350 Immunoadsorption in Neuromyelitis Optica Spectrum Disorder: A Single-Center Experience of 334 Procedures.

- PMID: 43036081

- 发表日期: 2026 Oct 12
- DOI: 10.1159/bpu/abpag021
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/43036081/
- 日报日期: 2026-11-22

#### 摘要

INTRODUCTION: We describe an institutional nursing protocol used for patients with neuromyelitis optica spectrum disorder (NMOSD) undergoing TR-350 tryptophan immunoadsorption (IA) therapy. We retrospectively analyzed the clinical and nursing records of 67 patients with NMOSD who underwent TR-350 IA between January 2019 and August 2025.
METHODS: We evaluated 334 treatment sessions and recorded demographic characteristics, observed clinical and serological changes, adverse events, and device alarms. Nursing management before, during, and after the procedure was reviewed.
RESULTS: After the IA treatment course, reductions in the median Expanded Disability Status Scale score and the geometric mean anti-aquaporin-4 immunoglobulin G (AQP4-IgG) titer, together with improvement in visual acuity, were observed. Laboratory testing revealed hypofibrinogenemia (<1.0 g/L) in 13 of 67 (19.4%) patients. Nurses identified hypotension in two sessions (2/334, 0.6%) and catheter-site bleeding in one session (1/334, 0.3%); in all, 29 device-alarm events were recorded. With nursing intervention and timely troubleshooting, 333 of 334 sessions were completed; one session (0.3%) was terminated prematurely because of extracorporeal circuit clotting.
CONCLUSION: TR-350 IA demonstrated feasibility and safety in this cohort of patients with steroid-refractory NMOSD. Adherence to nursing protocols refined locally from institutional standard operating procedures and clinical experience, together with proactive management of complications, supported a high treatment completion rate and patient safety.

### [A] Hypertensive stress rewires macrophage signaling in heart failure with preserved ejection fraction independent of obesity.

- PMID: 43035499
- Domain: Cross-domain vascular biology
- Domain 依据: Transferable mechanism appears with explicit vascular/endothelial context
- 转化相关性: Moderate

- 期刊: Nature communications
- 作者: Kinoshita Y, Sato M, Kadomatsu T, Katoh M, Ikeda K, Yoshioka G, et al.
- 发表日期: 2026 Oct 16
- DOI: 10.1038/s41467-026-78375-2
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/43035499/
- 日报日期: 2026-11-17

#### 摘要

Heart failure with preserved ejection fraction (HFpEF) remains a major unmet clinical challenge. Although obesity is common in many HFpEF cohorts from Europe and North America, three Japanese HFpEF cohorts identified hypertension and sympathetic overactivation, rather than obesity, as dominant features. To model these features, we established an HFpEF mouse model in which sympathetic activation induces hypertensive heart disease, while endothelial dysfunction acts as a second hit driving progression to HFpEF. Hypertensive stress rewires cardiac macrophage-derived intercellular signaling, impairing macrophage-to-cardiomyocyte BMP signaling and fatty acid oxidation while enhancing macrophage-to-fibroblast PDGF signaling, thereby promoting metabolic remodeling, inflammation, and fibrosis. ANGPTL2 inhibition preserves macrophage intercellular communication and prevents HFpEF development. These findings identify cardiac macrophage signaling rewiring as a central mechanism linking hypertensive stress to metabolic remodeling and inflammation, revealing a pathogenic pathway for HFpEF in the absence of obesity that is distinct from HFpEF associated with obesity.

- DOI: 10.1038/s41467-026-78588-5
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/43035482/
- 日报日期: 2026-11-17

#### 摘要

Temporomandibular joint osteoarthritis (TMJ-OA) causes chronic pain, cartilage degeneration and pathological neurovascular remodeling, yet effective local therapies remain limited. Here we develop a minimally invasive near-infrared (NIR)-responsive microneedle system (SFZICC MNs) that integrates cryptochlorogenic acid (CCA)- and carbon quantum dot-loaded ZIF-8 nanoparticles within a methacrylated silk fibroin hydrogel. The system provides mechanical integrity, photothermal responsiveness and NIR-assisted local drug release. In a mouse TMJ-OA model, SFZICC MNs combined with NIR irradiation reduce mechanical hypersensitivity, anxiety-like behaviors, cartilage degeneration and pathological neurovascularization, and outperform free CCA, blank microneedles and drug-loaded microneedles without NIR. Mechanistically, CCA directly interacts with MMP3, MMP9 and MMP13 and moderately inhibits their catalytic activities in vitro, while reducing intracellular superoxide accumulation, NF-κB p65 phosphorylation and MMP protein abundance. In vivo imaging further supports reduced overall MMP-associated proteolytic activity. These findings support SFZICC MNs as a locally responsive therapeutic platform for TMJ-OA.

### [A] Metabolic reprogramming of macrophage-specific transketolase orchestrates angiogenesis in diabetic wounds.

- PMID: 43039938
- Domain: Cross-domain vascular biology
- Domain 依据: Transferable mechanism appears with explicit vascular/endothelial context
- 转化相关性: Moderate
- 转化相关性依据: Different disease with a highly transferable vascular/endothelial mechanism
- 优先级依据: A: highly transferable cross-domain vascular mechanism
- 主题: 跨领域顶刊启发
- 来源类型: 顶刊扩展
- 来源说明: 神经血管/脑血管顶刊扩展
- 期刊: Nature communications
- 作者: Xiong Y, Lu L, Wu Q, Wang H, Hu H, Zhao H, et al.
- 发表日期: 2026 Oct 17
- DOI: 10.1038/s41467-026-78765-6
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/43039938/
- 日报日期: 2026-11-18

#### 摘要

Diabetic wound healing is profoundly impaired by macrophage metabolic dysfunction, which compromises angiogenesis. Here, we show a macrophage transketolase (TKT) as a crucial metabolic checkpoint governing vascular regeneration in diabetes. We demonstrate that loss of TKT in macrophages accelerates amino acid and fatty acid catabolism, elevates reactive oxygen species, and disrupts endothelial cell proliferation and differentiation. Mechanistically, hyperglycemia activates the AGEs-RAGE signaling cascade through suppressing TKT expression, establishing a pathological TKT-AGEs-RAGE axis that reprograms macrophage metabolism toward dysfunction. Then, we engineer a nanozyme-integrated hydrogel patch that selectively increases TKT level in macrophages, which rebalances macrophage metabolism, reduces oxidative stress, and rescues angiogenic capacity. In a diabetic pig model, localized delivery of the nano-system markedly accelerates wound closure and vascular repair. These findings not only elucidate a macrophage-specific TKT metabolic reprogramming mechanism for enhancing angiogenesis, but also offer a potential therapeutic strategy for the clinical management of diabetic wounds.

### [C] Extracorporeal Blood Purification Techniques in Septic Shock: Operating Principles, Supporting Evidence and Clinical Applications.

- PMID: 43030761

- 作者: Bonsano M, Iacoviello B, Berlot G
- 发表日期: 2026 Nov 05
- DOI: 10.3390/jcm15218619
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/43030761/
- 日报日期: 2026-11-17

#### 摘要

Several extracorporeal blood purification techniques are used to treat septic shock and other conditions characterized by the release of inflammatory mediators and bacterial toxins, notably lipopolysaccharide (endotoxin), which account for the cardiovascular, coagulation and metabolic derangements. Each technique has advantages and drawbacks, including, for most devices, the need for circuit anticoagulation and the possible removal of antibiotics. High-quality randomized trials remain scarce, and their heterogeneity in populations, techniques, dose and timing, together with the enrollment of biologically unselected patients, limits firm conclusions. In this narrative review, we describe the available techniques, critically appraise the clinical evidence for each modality against the 2026 Surviving Sepsis Campaign guidelines, and outline the phenotype-guided direction of ongoing research. The 2026 Surviving Sepsis Campaign guidelines suggest against the use of blood purification techniques in adults with sepsis or septic shock (conditional recommendation, very low certainty of evidence) and, specifically, against polymyxin-B hemoperfusion (conditional recommendation, low certainty of evidence). In the phenotype-enriched Tigris trial, polymyxin-B hemoadsorption showed a high posterior probability of mortality benefit in endotoxic septic shock; given the small sample, the imprecise primary estimate and the informative prior, this promising finding requires independent confirmation. Outside clinical trials, routine blood purification is not supported.

### [C] Metabolic, Hepatic, and Neuropathological Effects of Palm11-PrRP31 and Liraglutide in High-Fat Diet-Fed APP/PS1 Mice.

- PMID: 43039332
- Domain: Out-of-scope
- Domain 依据: No strong CCM/vascular-malformation or transferable vascular mechanism evidence
- 转化相关性: Low

- 作者: Mengr A, Sopko Z, Vaculová P, Charvát V, Neprašová B, Vrkoslav V, et al.
- 发表日期: 2026 Nov 16
- DOI: 10.1016/j.neuropharm.2026.111263
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/43039332/
- 日报日期: 2026-11-17

#### 摘要

Metabolic stress is known to accelerate Alzheimer's disease (AD) progression. In our previous study, we demonstrated that long-term high-fat diet (HFD) feeding exacerbated not only amyloid-β (Aβ) burden but also liver steatosis in APP/PS1 mice, a transgenic model of AD, with effects worsening with age. The present study investigated whether a three-month treatment (from 6 to 9 months of age) with anorexigenic peptides (palm11-PrRP31 or liraglutide) could attenuate AD-like pathology, neuroinflammation, liver steatosis, and Aβ clearance in APP/PS1 male mice on HFD. The two compounds exhibited distinct therapeutic effects. Liraglutide reduced body weight and improved glucose tolerance in both genotypes, while also attenuating insulin resistance in APP/PS1 mice. In contrast, palm11-PrRP31 significantly reduced Aβ plaque burden and showed a tendency to decrease astrocytosis. Notably, both peptides markedly reduced microgliosis and pathological tau phosphorylation at Ser202 and Thr205 in the hippocampus and cerebral cortex. HFD induced pronounced hepatic steatosis in both genotypes; however, APP/PS1 mice exhibited more severe lipid imbalance, characterized by increased triacylglycerols and decreased phospholipids and cardiolipins compared to wild-type controls. While both compounds reduced liver steatosis and inflammation in wild-type mice, only liraglutide normalized lipid composition in APP/PS1 mice. Furthermore, Aβ deposits colocalized with CD11b clusters were absent in the liver of liraglutide-treated APP/PS1 mice. In conclusion, both peptides exert neuroprotective effects under metabolic stress, but through distinct mechanisms. Palm11-PrRP31 primarily reduces amyloid pathology and neuroinflammation, whereas liraglutide acts mainly through metabolic improvement.

### [C] Long-term preservation of normal brain tissue by minibeam radiation therapy does not require mature T cells.

- PMID: 43039249
- Domain: Out-of-scope
- Domain 依据: No strong CCM/vascular-malformation or transferable vascular mechanism evidence
- 转化相关性: Low
- 转化相关性依据: Only generic or out-of-scope keyword overlap
- 优先级依据: C: broad, exploratory, low-relevance, or unmatched article
- 主题: 神经免疫/炎症治疗
- 来源类型: 今日新文献
- 期刊: International journal of radiation oncology, biology, physics
- 作者: Bertho A, Roncali L, Lamirault C, Sebrié C, Ortiz R, Espenon J, et al.
- 发表日期: 2026 Nov 16
- DOI: 10.1016/j.ijrobp.2026.10.056
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/43039249/
- 日报日期: 2026-11-17

#### 摘要

PURPOSE: Minibeam radiation therapy (MBRT) preserves normal tissue while maintaining antitumour efficacy, yet the mechanisms underlying this remarkable normal tissue tolerance remain poorly understood. Although adaptive immune responses have been implicated in the antitumour effects of spatially fractionated radiotherapy, their contribution to normal tissue preservation remains unknown. Here, we used a loss-of-function model to determine whether mature T cells are required for the expression of the long-term normal tissue-sparing phenotype associated with MBRT.
METHODS AND MATERIALS: Immunocompetent Fischer 344 rats and athymic nude rats received X-ray MBRT or conventional radiotherapy (CRT) at a mean dose of 30 Gy and were followed for up to six months. Long-term normal tissue response was assessed using complementary behavioural analyses, magnetic resonance imaging (MRI), and histopathological evaluation of immune infiltration, vascular integrity, glial activation and neuronal survival.
RESULTS: Nude rats developed beam-path alopecia and skin lesions earlier than immunocompetent rats and showed a transient reduction in cortical CD31 staining at 7 days post-irradiation, indicating greater acute radiosensitivity without mature T cells. Beyond this early window, the long-term phenotype was comparable between strains: no MRI-detectable lesions, no neuronal loss and no sustained glial activation at 6 months. MBRT induced modest late reductions in exploratory behaviour and recognition memory in both strains, with no significant strain × irradiation interaction. Histologically, MBRT elicited a moderate and transient CD3+ T-cell infiltration that resolved within seven days, whereas CRT induced a stronger and more sustained inflammatory response.
CONCLUSIONS: Mature T cells modulate the acute tissue reaction to MBRT but are not required for the long-term normal tissue-sparing phenotype. Within the sensitivity of this study, powered for large effects only, mature T cells are unlikely to be a major determinant of long-term MBRT tissue preservation, redirecting attention towards innate immune, vascular and stromal processes.

### [C] Inter-subject functional variability of gray and white matter during methadone maintenance treatment and its association with gene expression profiles.

- PMID: 43039210
- Domain: Out-of-scope
- Domain 依据: No strong CCM/vascular-malformation or transferable vascular mechanism evidence

- 来源类型: 今日新文献
- 期刊: Progress in neuro-psychopharmacology & biological psychiatry
- 作者: Yang L, Huang C, Tang Y, Li Y, Niu L, Zeng Y, et al.
- 发表日期: 2026 Nov 16
- DOI: 10.1016/j.pnpbp.2026.112000
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/43039210/
- 日报日期: 2026-11-17

#### 摘要

BACKGROUND: Methadone maintenance treatment(MMT) for opioid use disorder(OUD) may cause changes in brain functional heterogeneity whose molecular mechanisms remain unknown. This study aims to investigate MMT-related gray/white matter inter-subject variability functional connectivity(GM/WM-IVFC) alterations and to associate them with clinical and transcriptomic characteristics.
METHODS: Between March 2017 and June 2026, a total of 244 participants were voluntarily enrolled from detoxification centers, drug rehabilitation clinics, and society in Hunan Province, China, including MMT group(N = 44), individuals with sustained heroin self-abstinence and no lifetime MMT(HSA, N = 42), short-term abstinent heroin users at baseline(OUD1, N = 62) and 10-month opioid-free follow-up(OUD2, N = 43), and two healthy control cohorts(HC1, N = 48; HC2, N = 48) respectively age/gender-matched with MMT/HSA groups and OUD1. OUD1/2 and HSA groups were used to exploratively investigate the influence of previous heroin use and non-MMT abstinence duration on IVFC. Resting-state functional magnetic resonance imaging(rs-fMRI) data were acquired and analyzed to extract GM/WM-IVFC metrics, which were compared ROI-wise between groups and correlated with neuropsychological scales. Transcriptomic data from the Allen Human Brain Atlas(AHBA) were integrated via partial least squares(PLS) regression and functional enrichment analyses to further identify gene expression profiles underlying IVFC alterations.
RESULTS: After Bonferroni correction, compared to HC1, MMT group exhibited the most widespread IVFC increases, spanning 20 GM and 7 WM regions. HSA group shared some overlapping GM-IVFC elevations with MMT group in bilateral orbital parts of inferior frontal gyrus and thalamus, yet its overall mean IVFC of significant WM regions was lower than that of MMT group. Neither OUD1 nor OUD2 showed difference in overall mean IVFC of significant GM regions from HC2. Both OUD1/2 displayed elevated overall mean IVFC of significant WM regions relative to HC2. Then, the overall mean IVFC of significant GM/WM regions between MMT group and HC1 were positively correlated with impulsivity, depression, anxiety, insomnia, and daily methadone dose. PLS regression linked IVFC patterns to AHBA transcriptomic profiles in MMT group, with enriched pathways in neuroinflammation, synaptic function, glutamatergic signaling, and neuronal development.
CONCLUSIONS: These findings indicate that MMT is accompanied by cerebral IVFC elevations, whose linkages with neuropsychological aberrations and gene profiles implicate IVFC as a latent clinical phenotype for managing MMT-related complications.

### [C] Baicalein and Baicalein-loaded liposomes attenuate manganese-induced injury in the rat substantia nigra.

- Domain: Out-of-scope

- 发表日期: 2026
- DOI: 10.1371/journal.pone.0362801
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/43038196/
- 日报日期: 2026-11-17

#### 摘要

BACKGROUND/OBJECTIVES: Manganese (Mn) can accumulate in the brain, damage the basal ganglia and cause Parkinsonian phenotypes. Baicalein (BA), a natural flavonoid, shows therapeutic promise driven by its antioxidant and anti-inflammatory properties. This study aimed to evaluate whether BA and baicalein-loaded liposomes (BA-LPs) reduce Mn-induced injury to the substantia nigra.
METHODS: Sixty male Wistar rats were allocated into six groups: control, Mn (50 mg/kg), BA (30 mg/kg), BA-LPs, and Mn combined with either BA or BA-LPs. Treatments were administered intraperitoneally twice weekly for four weeks, with BA and BA-LP pretreatments one hour before Mn exposure. Markers of oxidative stress and antioxidant defence, glial and astrocyte presence, neuroinflammation, apoptosis, histopathological and ultrastructural damage, and tyrosine hydroxylase (TH) and dopamine levels in the substantia nigra were assessed.
RESULTS: Mn exposure significantly disrupted redox homeostasis, evidenced by significantly reduced Nrf2, HO-1, SOD, CAT, and GPx expression and protein activities, and elevated malondialdehyde levels. Mn exposure increased microglial and astrocytic biomarkers, as shown by elevated Iba-1 and GFAP, and induced neuroinflammation with elevated TLR4, NF-κB, IL-1β, IL-6, TNF-α, NO, and iNOS expression and protein activities. Mn increased levels of caspases 3 and 8 and a Bax/Bcl-2 ratio indicative of elevated apoptosis. Mn treatment caused histopathological and ultrastructural damage, and reduced TH and dopamine levels. In the Mn + BA and Mn + BA-LPs co-treatments, markers of antioxidant defences were significantly restored, neuroinflammation reduced, apoptosis attenuated, and TH and dopamine levels partially restored. Histological and ultrastructural findings confirmed reduced cellular damage in neuronal tissue with BA and BA-LPs co-treatments.
CONCLUSIONS: BA and BA delivered via liposomes reduce Mn-induced neurotoxicity, lowering markers of oxidative stress, neuroinflammation, apoptosis, and neurodegenerative injury, with effects typically more extensive in the BA-LPs group.

### [C] The emerging role of microglial TREM2 in Alzheimer's Disease: pathogenesis and therapeutic implications.

- PMID: 43037788
- Domain: Out-of-scope

- 发表日期: 2026 Nov 16
- DOI: 10.1007/s11033-026-13171-1
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/43037788/
- 日报日期: 2026-11-17

#### 摘要

Alzheimer's disease (AD), the most common cause of dementia, imposes a substantial and growing burden on families and society. Although AD is classically defined by amyloid and tau pathology, microglial dysfunction is increasingly recognized as a central determinant of disease progression. TREM2 (Triggering Receptor Expressed on Myeloid cells 2), predominantly expressed on microglia, is a key regulator of microglial responses in the AD brain. In this review, we propose that TREM2 functions as a context- and disease-stage-dependent checkpoint that integrates microglial survival, phagocytosis, metabolic adaptation, and inflammatory signaling, thereby shaping AD pathogenesis. This framework helps explain why TREM2 signaling can exert protective or detrimental effects across disease stages and suggests that effective TREM2-directed therapy will require stage-specific modulation rather than simple agonism or inhibition. We critically examine the physiological functions of microglia and TREM2, evaluate TREM2-related biomarkers, and discuss emerging therapeutic strategies, thereby providing a conceptual basis for microglia-targeted AD diagnosis and treatment.

#### 摘要

BACKGROUND: Macrophages in the multiple myeloma (MM) microenvironment adopt diverse functional states that extend beyond the M1/M2 framework. This review examines how spatial niches and metabolic reprogramming shape macrophage function and contribute to therapy resistance.
METHODS: We synthesized published mechanistic, single-cell, and spatial profiling studies, focusing on glycolytic, lipid, amino acid, and mitochondrial pathways across hypoxic, vascular, osteolytic, and extramedullary niches.
RESULTS: The reviewed evidence suggests that tumor-derived signals, extracellular vesicles, and local metabolic conditions jointly shape macrophage states that can promote immune dysfunction, disease progression, and therapy resistance. Single-cell and spatial profiling studies link macrophage states to cellular neighborhoods, anatomical location, and metabolic context.
CONCLUSIONS: Targeting macrophage signaling, metabolism, phagocytic checkpoints, and niche-specific pathways may help overcome therapy resistance in MM. Most supporting evidence comes from preclinical and in vitro studies, and these strategies require clinical validation.

### [C] Human milk oligosaccharides as immunomodulators of the gut-immune-brain axis: a potential route to suppressing neuroinflammation in Alzheimer's disease.

- PMID: 43037079
- Domain: Out-of-scope
- Domain 依据: No strong CCM/vascular-malformation or transferable vascular mechanism evidence
- 转化相关性: Low
- 转化相关性依据: Only generic or out-of-scope keyword overlap

- 主题: 神经免疫/炎症治疗
- 来源类型: 今日新文献
- 期刊: Nutritional neuroscience
- 作者: Fan C, Li B, Wang S
- 发表日期: 2026 Nov 16
- DOI: 10.1080/1028415X.2026.2753171
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/43037079/
- 日报日期: 2026-11-17

#### 摘要

BACKGROUND: Alzheimer's disease (AD), the most common cause of dementia, is characterized by interacting pathological processes in which chronic neuroinflammation plays a central role. The gut-brain axis connects intestinal and central nervous system function through immune, neural, and metabolic pathways, yet how immune signaling integrates these pathways in AD remains unclear. Human milk oligosaccharides (HMOs) are structurally diverse bioactive glycans whose immunomodulatory properties extend beyond their prebiotic effects. This narrative review examines HMOs within a gut-immune-brain framework, proposing that neuroinflammation amplifies the interplay between gut-derived immune signals, amyloid-β and tau pathology, and neurodegeneration.
METHODS: Relevant literature was retrieved from PubMed, Web of Science, and Scopus through January 2026. The review focused on English-language publications from 2010 onward, supplemented by earlier foundational studies that provided essential mechanistic or historical context.
RESULTS: We synthesize evidence on immune communication along the gut-brain axis and examine how HMOs may influence this communication through microbiota-dependent effects and direct interactions with immune receptors. Particular attention is given to the relationships between HMO structure, receptor engagement, immune cell responses, and inflammatory signaling. These mechanisms provide a rationale for investigating whether HMOs can modify peripheral immune signals that contribute to neuroinflammation in AD. Throughout the review, findings from infant and other experimental settings are distinguished from evidence directly relevant to aging and AD.
CONCLUSION: Research on HMOs in aging and AD remains insufficient: direct evidence from aged or AD models is scarce, human trials are lacking, infant physiology differs substantially from that of older adults, and the bioavailability of HMOs to the aged brain is still uncertain. We therefore delineate this translational gap and outline research strategies to address it. Collectively, this review provides a conceptual framework for understanding the potential immunoregulatory roles of HMOs in neuroinflammation and identifies them as a hypothesis-generating class of candidate immunomodulators for AD.

### [C] Pre-Treatment Biomarkers Associated With Remission of Type 2 Diabetes in Response to Short-Term Insulin-Based Therapy.

- PMID: 43035628
- Domain: Out-of-scope
- Domain 依据: No strong CCM/vascular-malformation or transferable vascular mechanism evidence
- 转化相关性: Low
- 转化相关性依据: Only generic or out-of-scope keyword overlap
- 优先级依据: C: broad, exploratory, low-relevance, or unmatched article
- 主题: 神经免疫/炎症治疗
- 来源类型: 今日新文献
- 期刊: Diabetes, obesity & metabolism
- 作者: Thiyagarajah K, Shen J, Parikh M, Zinman B, Beaudry JL, Retnakaran R
- 发表日期: 2026 Nov 15
- DOI: 10.1111/dom.71551
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/43035628/
- 日报日期: 2026-11-17

#### 摘要

AIMS: Short-term insulin-based therapy can induce remission of Type 2 diabetes (T2DM) in some but not all patients. The underlying determinant of whether remission can be achieved is beta-cell function, but its assessment typically requires complex challenge testing. We thus sought to determine if there exist circulating biomarkers associated with the likelihood of achieving remission prior to initiating short-term insulin-based therapy, without requiring challenge testing.
METHODS: Adults with T2DM of ≤ 7 years duration were randomized to 8 weeks treatment with (i) insulin glargine, (ii) glargine + thrice-daily lispro or (iii) glargine + twice-daily exenatide, followed by 12 weeks washout that enabled assessment for remission (A1c < 6.5% after ≥ 3 months without glucose-lowering therapy). We assessed the following fasting biomarkers at baseline in relation to subsequent remission: fibroblast growth factor-21 (FGF-21), C-reactive protein (CRP), vascular cell adhesion molecule-1 (VCAM-1), interleukin-1β (IL-1β), interleukin-6 (IL-6), interleukin-4 (IL-4), monocyte chemoattractant protein-1 (MCP-1) and dipeptidyl peptidase-4 (DPP-4).
RESULTS: Remission occurred in 31 of 90 participants (34.4%). At baseline, compared to their non-remitting peers, those in whom remission was subsequently achieved had higher Homeostasis Model Assessment of beta-cell function (HOMA-B; p = 0.02), lower fasting proinsulin: C-peptide ratio (p = 0.008) and borderline higher prevalence of detectable IL-4 (p = 0.06), though significance did not persist after multiplicity-adjustment. On exploratory logistic regression analyses, baseline detectable IL-4 (aOR = 3.58; 95% CI: 1.25-10.99) and HOMA-B (aOR = 1.77; 95% CI: 1.11-2.99) were associated with remission.
CONCLUSION: Prior to treatment, higher HOMA-B and detectable IL-4 are candidate markers warranting further study for association with subsequent remission of T2DM in response to short-term insulin-based therapy.

### [C] PFI-3 attenuates atherosclerotic plaque vulnerability and modulates a BRG1-SLUG-miR-193b-3p-CD47 axis in cultured vascular smooth muscle cells.

- PMID: 43035121
- Domain: Cross-domain vascular biology
- Domain 依据: Transferable mechanism appears with explicit vascular/endothelial context
- 转化相关性: Exploratory
- 转化相关性依据: Cross-domain vascular mechanism with exploratory CCM relevance
- 优先级依据: C: broad, exploratory, low-relevance, or unmatched article
- 主题: 神经免疫/炎症治疗
- 来源类型: 今日新文献
- 期刊: Life sciences
- 作者: Liu B, Zhang M, Li Y, Li H, Sun Z, Liu Q, et al.
- 发表日期: 2026 Nov 15
- DOI: 10.1016/j.lfs.2026.124779
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/43035121/
- 日报日期: 2026-11-17

#### 摘要

AIMS: CD47, a "don't-eat-me" signal overexpressed in atherosclerotic lesions, impairs efferocytosis and promotes plaque instability. BRG1 (SMARCA4), the catalytic ATPase of the SWI/SNF chromatin-remodeling complex, drives vascular inflammation, yet its role in CD47 regulation remains unknown. We investigated whether PFI-3, an inhibitor of the BRG1/SMARCA4, BRM/SMARCA2, and PBRM1 bromodomains, attenuates plaque vulnerability and delineated the BRG1-dependent cascade regulating CD47 in vascular smooth muscle cells (VSMCs).
MATERIALS AND METHODS: High-fat-diet-fed ApoE-/- mice received oral PFI-3 or vehicle; plaque burden, composition, and efferocytosis were assessed histologically. LPS-stimulated mouse VSMCs (mVSMCs) underwent BRG1 knockdown or PFI-3 treatment followed by macrophage engulfment assays. ChIP-qPCR, dual-luciferase, and epistatic rescue experiments defined the regulatory cascade.
KEY FINDINGS: PFI-3 reduced plaque area, necrotic core size, and macrophage infiltration while increasing collagen and α-SMA content, without altering circulating lipids. Free apoptotic cells decreased and macrophage-associated clearance increased. In vitro, LPS co-upregulated BRG1, SLUG, and CD47 in mVSMCs. BRG1 silencing suppressed SLUG and CD47 and enhanced phagocytosis. ChIP confirmed BRG1 occupancy at the SLUG promoter; SLUG repressed miR-193b-3p, which targeted the CD47 3'-UTR. PFI-3 phenocopied BRG1 knockdown by restoring miR-193b-3p and suppressing CD47.
SIGNIFICANCE: This study identifies a BRG1 → SLUG→miR-193b-3p → CD47 cascade in mVSMCs whereby BRG1 elevates CD47 under inflammation to impair efferocytosis. PFI-3 suppresses this axis in vitro and attenuates plaque vulnerability in vivo - although contributions from BRM/SMARCA2 and PBRM1 inhibition cannot be excluded. These findings support further investigation of PFI-3 and this regulatory axis in atherosclerosis.

### [C] Protocatechualdehyde ameliorates temporal lobe epilepsy via synergistic regulation of HMGB1-TLR4-NF-κB pathway and metabolic reprogramming.

- 发表日期: 2027 Feb 10
- DOI: 10.1016/j.jep.2026.122454
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42822611/
- 日报日期: 2026-11-17

#### 摘要

ETHNOPHARMACOLOGICAL RELEVANCE: Temporal lobe epilepsy (TLE) is the most prevalent subtype of epilepsy, characterized by recurrent spontaneous seizures. Gastrodia elata Blume, a classic traditional Chinese medicinal herb, has long been clinically applied for the intervention of neurological disorders including epilepsy. However, its key bioactive components and definite pharmacological mechanisms against TLE remain poorly clarified.
AIM OF THE STUDY: This study was designed to screen effective anticonvulsant active ingredients from G. elata Blume, and further explore the therapeutic efficacy and potential molecular mechanisms of protocatechualdehyde against TLE.
MATERIAL AND METHODS: Maximal electroshock seizure (MES) and pentylenetetrazole (PTZ)-induced acute seizure models were adopted to screen six phenolic active constituents from G. elata Blume. A lithium-pilocarpine-induced chronic TLE mouse model was established to assess the in vivo antiepileptic efficacy of protocatechualdehyde. Behavioral tests were carried out to monitor seizure manifestations and cognitive alterations. Furthermore, a combined strategy integrating network pharmacology, molecular docking, proteomics, serum metabolomics, and molecular biology experiments was applied to explore the underlying pharmacological mechanisms of protocatechualdehyde against TLE.
RESULTS: Protocatechualdehyde was screened out as the component with the strongest anticonvulsant activity. After 28 days of continuous intraperitoneal injection intervention, different doses of protocatechualdehyde significantly reduced seizure frequency and duration in TLE mice, ameliorated cognitive impairment, and effectively corrected hippocampal disorders in neurotransmitter and amino acid metabolism as well as cerebral energy and glucose metabolism. In addition, protocatechualdehyde inhibited the abnormal activation of microglia and astrocytes in a dose-dependent manner. Mechanically, it blocked HMGB1 nuclear export, and further inhibited the excessive activation of downstream TLR4-NF-κB inflammatory signaling pathway.
CONCLUSIONS: Protocatechualdehyde alleviates TLE-induced neuroinflammation and metabolic dysfunction via indirect modulation of the HMGB1-TLR4-NF-κB signaling cascade, accompanied by suppression of glial overactivation and restoration of brain metabolic homeostasis, which is expected to become a promising candidate agent for the treatment of TLE.

### [C] Intranasal delivery of a ROS-activatable myeloperoxidase inhibitor alleviates Alzheimer's-like symptoms and pathology.

- PMID: 43043697
- Domain: Out-of-scope
- Domain 依据: No strong CCM/vascular-malformation or transferable vascular mechanism evidence

- 作者: Wang J, Cong P, Feng E, Zeller MWG, Zhang B, Lv Y, et al.
- 发表日期: 2026 Nov 24
- DOI: 10.1073/pnas.2603280123
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/43043697/
- 日报日期: 2026-11-18

#### 摘要

Chronic neuroinflammation and oxidative stress are important contributors to Alzheimer's disease (AD). Myeloperoxidase (MPO), a key driver of oxidative inflammation released mainly by activated microglia in the neurodegenerative brain, is a potential therapeutic target to inhibit the neuroinflammatory cascade and oxidative damage in AD, but the underlying mechanisms remain unexplored, and achieving targeted brain delivery of an MPO inhibitor and minimizing systemic effects remain significant challenges. We addressed these limitations by first establishing that genetic deletion of MPO in the 5XFAD mouse model improved memory performance and reduced amyloid deposition, validating MPO as a therapeutic target. We then developed a liposome-encapsulated reactive oxygen species (ROS)-activatable MPO inhibitor (LERAMI). LERAMI uniquely combines targeted delivery with on-demand drug release, utilizing a ROS-sensitive linker to selectively release MPO inhibitors in ROS-rich regions within the AD microenvironment, minimizing off-target effects and systemic toxicity. In the 5XFAD mouse model. By reducing MPO expression and activity, LERAMI significantly improved cognitive performance, accompanied by decreased microglial activation, attenuated oxidative DNA damage, improved synaptic and neuronal density, and reduced amyloid burden. RNA sequencing revealed a robust hippocampal transcriptomic reprogramming, with upregulation of pathways critical for synaptic plasticity, calcium signaling, axon guidance, and neuroprotection, along with downregulation of inflammatory and immune response pathways. These findings demonstrate that targeted ROS-activatable MPO inhibition with LERAMI represents a promising therapeutic strategy for AD and potentially other neuroinflammatory diseases, offering a unique approach to mitigate neuroinflammation, preserve synaptic integrity, and restore cognitive function.

### [C] Inflammation-Targeted Multifunctional Nanoparticles Remodel the Spinal Cord Injury Microenvironment to Promote Neurovascular Repair.

- PMID: 43044658
- Domain: Cross-domain vascular biology
- Domain 依据: Transferable mechanism appears with explicit vascular/endothelial context
- 转化相关性: Exploratory

- 期刊: ACS applied materials & interfaces
- 作者: Li J, Wang Y, Zhang Q, Guo D, Liu Y, He X, et al.
- 发表日期: 2026 Nov 18
- DOI: 10.1021/acsami.6c18128
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/43044658/
- 日报日期: 2026-11-18

#### 摘要

Spinal cord injury (SCI) is characterized by interconnected inflammation, oxidative stress, vascular dysfunction, and impaired neural regeneration, while insufficient lesion-directed delivery further limits the efficacy of systemic therapy. Here, we developed macrophage membrane-camoufl11aged Sr-PDA/Fasudil/MnO2 nanoparticles (MM-NPs) that were designed to integrate inflammation-targeted delivery with complementary immunomodulatory, redox-regulating, and repair-supporting functions. Macrophage membrane camouflage reduced nonspecific phagocytic uptake, enhanced recognition of inflammatory endothelium, improved delivery in an in vitro endothelial barrier model, and increased lesion accumulation in vivo. The formulation provided sustained fasudil release and was designed to combine Rho-associated protein kinase (ROCK) inhibition with redox regulation and bioactive support. MM-NPs consequently attenuated microglial inflammatory activation and excessive reactive oxygen species (ROS) accumulation, improved endothelial migration and angiogenic responses, and alleviated inflammation-induced impairment of neural stem cell differentiation. In a rat SCI model, MM-NPs reduced lesion cavitation and fibrosis, improved the inflammatory microenvironment, enhanced vascular, axonal, and myelin-associated repair, and promoted neurological recovery. Transcriptomic profiling and quantitative spinal cord immunoblotting supported treatment-associated changes in ROCK-associated and NF-κB-related inflammatory signaling. Collectively, MM-NPs couple inflammation-targeted delivery with coordinated immune, redox, vascular, and neural regulation, providing a microenvironment-centered strategy for neurovascular repair after SCI.

### [C] Long non-coding RNA H19 knockout activates the SAHH/DNMT3b pathway and represses NLRP3 inflammasome expression in a diabetic rat model.

- PMID: 42959415
- Domain: Cross-domain vascular biology

- 期刊: International journal of ophthalmology
- 作者: Chen H, Pang YH, Zhang XZ, Liao NY, Li ML, Ji YY, et al.
- 发表日期: 2026
- DOI: 10.18240/ijo.2026.11.04
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42959415/
- 日报日期: 2026-11-18

#### 摘要

AIM: To explore the regulatory effect of long non-coding RNA H19 on the Nod-like receptor protein 3 (NLRP3) inflammasome and its functional role in the progression of diabetic retinopathy (DR).
METHODS: Wild-type and H19 knockout rats were subjected to a high-fat and high-sugar diet combined with streptozotocin injection to establish an in vivo diabetes mellitus (DM) model. In vitro, rat primary retinal microvascular endothelial (CP-R114) cells were cultured under high-glucose conditions to mimic the diabetic microenvironment. The MTS assay was performed to evaluate cell viability. Enzyme-linked immunosorbent assay (ELISA) was used to detect the levels of indicators related to glucose metabolism, lipid metabolism, oxidative stress, and inflammation in rat retinal tissues and CP-R114 cells. Hematoxylin and eosin (HE) staining was applied to observe the pathological morphological changes in retinal tissues. qRT-PCR was conducted to quantify the mRNA expression of H19, S-adenosylhomocysteine hydrolase (SAHH), and S-adenosylmethionine-dependent methyltransferase 3 beta (DNMT3b) in rat retinas. Western blotting and immunofluorescence staining were further utilized to detect the protein expression of SAHH, DNMT3b, NLRP3, Caspase-1, and interleukin (IL)-1β in retinal tissues.
RESULTS: In DM model rats, H19 knockout significantly improved glucose and lipid metabolic disorders and alleviated oxidative stress. Notably, H19 deficiency effectively ameliorated retinal tissue injury. The expression levels of intercellular cell adhesion molecule-1 (ICAM-1), vascular endothelial growth factor (VEGF), and pro-inflammatory factors were markedly downregulated in H19-silenced DM rats. Mechanistically, H19 knockout upregulated the expression of SAHH and DNMT3b, while inhibiting the expression of NLRP3 inflammasome-associated proteins in DM rats. Consistent with the in vivo findings, H19 silencing suppressed NLRP3 inflammasome activation and elevated SAHH and DNMT3b expression in high-glucose-stimulated CP-R114 cells.
CONCLUSION: This study demonstrates that H19 knockout alleviates DR progression in diabetic rats by activating the SAHH/DNMT3b signaling pathway and inhibiting NLRP3 inflammasome activation. Therefore, targeting H19 represents a promising novel therapeutic strategy for the clinical treatment of DR.

### [C] Disrupting the interaction between T lymphocytes and microglia: Mechanism of Huanglian Jiedu Decoction in the treatment of ischemic stroke.

- 作者: Cheng J, Zheng Y, Wang Y, Zhao Y, Han J, Li J, et al.
- 发表日期: 2026 Nov 17
